\section*{集合、常用逻辑用语}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 集合 $A = \left\{  {4,{m}^{2} + {3m}}\right\}$ 中实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[3]{$\{x | x \neq 1 \text{或} -4\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${m}^{2} + {3m} \neq 4$，解得$m \in \{x | x \neq 1 \text{或} -4\}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \{  - 1,3,0\} ,B = \left\{  {3,{m}^{2}}\right\}$ ,若 $B \subseteq  A$ ,则实数 $m$ 的值为\blkda[4]{0}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $B \subseteq  A$ ,所以 ${m}^{2} =  - 1$ (舍去) 或 ${m}^{2} = 0$，所以 $m = 0$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知全集 $U = \mathbf{R}$ ,集合 $A = \{ x \mid  x < a\} ,B = \{ x \mid   - 1 < x < 3\}$ ,且 $B \subseteq  A$ ,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$[3, +\infty)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $B \subseteq  A$ ,又因为 $A = \{ x \mid  x < a\} ,B = \{ x \mid   - 1 < x < 3\}$ ,所以 $a \geq  3$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若集合 $A = \left\{  {2,{3a} - 1,{a}^{2} - {4a}}\right\}$ ,且 $- 4 \in  A$ ,则 $a =$ \blkda[4]{-1 或 2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $A = \left\{  {2,{3a} - 1,{a}^{2} - {4a}}\right\}$ ,且 $- 4 \in  A$ ,
        
        所以 ${3a} - 1 =  - 4$ 或 ${a}^{2} - {4a} =  - 4$ ,
        
        当 ${3a} - 1 =  - 4$ 时, $a =  - 1$ ,此时 $A = \{ 2, - 4,5\}$ ,满足题意;
        
        当 ${a}^{2} - {4a} =  - 4$ 时, $a = 2$ ,此时 $A = \{ 2,5, - 4\}$ ,满足题意,
        
        综上所述, $a =  - 1$ 或 2 .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $M = \{ x \mid   - 1 < x < 3\} ,N = \{ x \mid   - 2 < x < 1\}$ ,则 $M \cup  N =$ \blkda[3]{$\{ x \mid   - 2 < x < 3\}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意可知 $M \cup  N = \{ x \mid   - 2 < x < 3\}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \{ x \mid  x < a\} ,B = \{ x \mid  x \geq  1\}$ ,且 $A \cup  B = \mathbf{R}$ ,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 1, + \infty )$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意知 $A \cup  B = \mathbf{R}$ ,可得 $a \geq  1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 设 $U$ 表示全集,下列命题中假命题有\blkda[4]{0}个
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 若 $A \cap  B = U$ ,则 $A = B = U$
        \item 若 $A \cup  B = \varnothing$ ,则 $A = B = \varnothing$
        \item 若 $A \cap  B = \varnothing$ ,则 $\overline{A} \cup  \overline{B} = U$
        \item 若 $A \cup  B = U$ ,则 $\overline{A} \cap  \overline{B} = \varnothing$
        \item 若 $A \subseteq  B$ ,则 $\overline{A} \supseteq  \overline{B}$
        \item $\varnothing  \in  \{ \varnothing \}$
        \item $\varnothing  \subseteq  \{ \varnothing \}$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \item “实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 都是正数”的否定形式为\blkda[6]{实数 $a\text{ 、 }b$ 中至少有一个是非正数}.
    
    \item $x = 2$ 是 ${x}^{2} - {3x} + 2 = 0$ 的\blkda[3]{充分不必要}条件.
    \begin{jieda}
        ${x}^{2} - {3x} + 2 = 0$ ,解得 $x = 1$ 或 2，故 $x = 2$ 是 ${x}^{2} - {3x} + 2 = 0$ 的充分不必要条件.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 $p :  - 2 < x < 2,q : \sqrt{x} < 4$ ,则 $p$ 是 $q$ 的\blkda[3]{既非充分又非必要}条件.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because q : \sqrt{x} < 4$ ,
        
        $\therefore q : 0 \leq  x < {16}$ ,
        
        $\because  - 2 < x < 2$ 既不能推出 $0 \leq  x < {16}$ ,也不能被 $0 \leq  x < {16}$ 推出
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $m - 2 < x < m + 1$ 成立的充分非必要条件是 $0 < x < 1$ ,则 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {0,2}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题知$(0,1)$是 $\left( {m - 2,m + 1}\right)$ 的真子集，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m - 2 \leq  0, \\  m + 1 \geq  1, \end{array}\right.$ 且等号不同时成立，解得 $0 \leq  m \leq  2$ 
        
        所以 $m$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {0,2}\right\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知命题 $p : \exists x \in  \mathbf{R},{x}^{2} - {3x} + 3 \leq  0$ ,则 $\neg p$ 为\blkda[6]{$\forall x \in  \mathbf{R},{x}^{2} - {3x} + 3 > 0$}.
\end{enumerate}
\clearpage
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}

    \item 下列各组集合中相等的是\blkda[4]{\ding{172}\ding{174}}
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $P = \{ x \mid  x = {2n},n \in  \mathbf{Z}\} ,Q = \{ x \mid  x = 2\left( {n - 1}\right) ,n \in  \mathbf{Z}\}$
        \item $P = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - 1 = 0}\right\}  ,Q = \left\{  {x\left| {x = \dfrac{1 + {\left( -1\right) }^{n}}{2}}\right. ,n \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right\}$
        \item $P = \{ x \mid  x = {2m} + 1,m \in  \mathbf{Z}\} ,Q = \{ x \mid  x = {4m} \pm  1,m \in  \mathbf{Z}\}$
        \item $P = \left\{  {y \mid  y = {x}^{2} + 1}\right\}  ,Q = \left\{  {x \mid  y = {x}^{2} + 1}\right\}$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 均为偶数集
            \item $P = \{1, -1\}$，$Q = \{0, 1\}$
            \item 均为奇数集
            \item 表示元素的符号不一样，$P = [1, +\infty)$，$Q = (-\infty, +\infty)$
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {1,3,{a}^{2}}\right\}$ ,集合 $B = \{ 1,2 + a\}$ ,若 $A \cup  B = A$ ,则 $a =$ \blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $A \cup  B = A$ ,所以 $2 + a = 3$ 或 ${a}^{2} = 2 + a$ .

        若 $2 + a = 3$ ,则 $a = 1$ ,此时 ${a}^{2} = 1$ ,集合 ${A}$ 中的元素不满足互异性,故 $a = 1$ 舍去.
        
        若 ${a}^{2} = 2 + a$ 则 $a =  - 1$ 或 $a = 2$ .
        
        当 $a =  - 1$ 时, ${a}^{2} = 1$ ,集合 ${A}$ 中的元素不满足互异性,故 $a =  - 1$ 舍去;
        
        当 $a = 2$ 时, $A = \{ 1,3,4\} ,B = \{ 1,3\} ,A \cup  B = A$ ,故 $a = 2$ 符合题意.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $a \in \mathbf{R}$, $M = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - {3x} - {a}^{2} + 2 = 0,x \in  \mathbf{R}}\right\}$ 的非空真子集个数是\blkda[4]{2} 
    \begin{jieda}
        $\Delta = 9 + 4a^2 - 8 = 4a^2 + 1 > 0$，故$M$中必定有两个元素，因此$M$的非空真子集个数为$2^2 - 2 = 2$.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $U = \{ \left( {x,y}\right)  \mid  x,y \in  \mathbf{R}\} ,M = \left\{  {\left( {x,y}\right) \left| {\dfrac{y - 2}{x - 1} = 3}\right. }\right\}  ,N = \{ \left( {x,y}\right)  \mid  y = {3x} - 1\} ,\overline{M} \cap N =$ \blkda[4]{$\{(1,2)\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $M$对应于直线$y = 3x-1$上除点$(1, 2)$以外的点

        $N$对应于直线$y = 3x-1$上的点，因此$\overline{M} \cap N$对应点$(1, 2)$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item $x^2 > 1$ 是 $\dfrac{1}{x} < 1$ 的\blkda[3]{充分不必要}条件.
    \begin{jieda}
        $x^2 > 1 \Longleftrightarrow x \in (-\infty, -1) \cup (1, +\infty)$

        $\dfrac{1}{x} < 1 \Longleftrightarrow x \in (-\infty, 0) \cup (1, +\infty)$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $p : x > m + 3$ 或 $x < m,q :  - 4 < x < 1$ ,且 $p$ 是 $q$ 的必要而不充分条件,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[3]{$m \leq   - 7$ 或 $m \geq  1$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $p$ 是 $q$ 的必要而不充分条件，所以命题 $q$ 中变量的取值集合是命题 $p$ 中变量的取值集合的真子集，即 $m + 3 \leq   - 4$ 或 $m \geq  1$ ，故 $m \leq   - 7$ 或 $m \geq  1$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item “ $- 4 < k < 0$ ” 是 “函数 $y = k{x}^{2} - {kx} - 1$ 的值恒为负值” 的\dotfill(\kh{A})
    \xx{充分非必要条件}{必要非充分条件}{充分必要条件}{即非充分也非必要条件}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $k = 0$时，函数$y = k{x}^{2} - {kx} - 1$恒为负值
            \item $k \neq 0$时，函数$y = k{x}^{2} - {kx} - 1$恒为负值的必要条件是$k < 0$，此时函数的最大值在其顶点处取得，计算对称轴为$x = \dfrac{1}{2}$，故函数的最大值为$-\dfrac{k}{4}-1$，解得$- 4 < k < 0$
        \end{dingautolist}
        故函数$y = k{x}^{2} - {kx} - 1$恒为负值的充要条件是$- 4 < k \leq 0$，故选A.
    \end{jieda}

    \item 请写出“如果两个三角形全等,那么它们的面积相等”的逆否命题:\blkda[10]{“如果两个三角形面积不相等，那么它们必然不全等”}
    \item 如果一个命题的逆命题是真命题,那么这个命题的否命题是\dotfill(\kh{A})
    \xx{真命题}{假命题}{不一定是真命题}{不一定是假命题}
    \begin{jieda}
        逆命题和否命题互为逆否命题，真假性相同
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - {3x} + 2 = 0}\right\}  ,B = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - {bx} + b - 1 = 0}\right\}  ,C = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - {cx} + }\right.$  $2 = 0\}$ 
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 若 $A \cup  B = A$ ,求实数 $b$ 的值
        \item 若 $A \cap  C = C$ ,求实数 $c$ 的取值范围
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item $A \cup  B = A \Longleftrightarrow B \subseteq A$，$A = \{1, 2\}$，$1 \in B, b-1 \in B$

            故$b - 1 \in \{1,2\}$，因此$b \in \{2,3\}$
            \item $A \cap  C = C \Longleftrightarrow C \subseteq A$，$A = \{1, 2\}$
            
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item $C = \varnothing$，此时$\Delta = c^2 - 8 < 0$，故$c \in (-2\sqrt{2}, 2\sqrt{2})$
                \item $C \neq \varnothing$，若$1 \in C$，则$c = 3$，解得$C = \{1,2\}$，满足要求；若$2 \in C$可得到相同结果.

                \ps 可以考虑韦达定理，可知必然当$C \neq \varnothing$时，两根为$1,2$时满足要求
            \end{dingautolist}
            综上，$c \in \{3\} \cup (-2\sqrt{2}, 2\sqrt{2})$
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}

    \item 已知全集 $U = \mathbf{R},A = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} + {px} + {12} = 0}\right\}  ,B = \left\{  {x \mid  {x}^{2} - {5x} + q = 0}\right\}$ , $\overline{A} \cap  B = \{ 2\}$ ,求 $p + q$ 的值.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\overline{A} \cap  B = \{ 2\} \Longleftrightarrow 2 \in B, 2 \notin A$，
        
        $2 \in B \Longrightarrow q = 6 \Longrightarrow B = \{2, 3\}$，又因为$2 \notin \overline{A} \cap  B$，所以$3 \in A$

        $3 \in A \Longrightarrow p = -7$，故$A = \{3,4\}$

        故$p+q = -1$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \{ x \mid  a - 1 \leq  x \leq  {2a} + 3\} ,B = \{ x \mid   - 1 \leq  x \leq  4\}$ ,全集 $U = R$ .
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 当 $a = 1$ 时,求 $\left( {{C}_{U}A}\right)  \cap  B$
        \item 若“ $x \in  B$ ”是“ $x \in  A$ ”的必要条件,求实数 $a$ 的取值范围
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 当 $a = 1$ 时,集合 $A = \{ x \mid  0 \leq  x \leq  5\} ,{C}_{U}A = \{ x \mid  x < 0$ 或 $x > 5\}$，$\left( {{C}_{U}A}\right)  \cap  B = \{ x \mid   - 1 \leq  x < 0\} .$
            
            \item 若“ $x \in  B$ ”是“ $x \in  A$ ”的必要条件,则 $A \subseteq  B$
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item 当 $A = \varnothing$ 时, $a - 1 > {2a} + 3,\therefore a <  - 4$
                \item $A \neq  \varnothing$ ,则 $a \geq   - 4$ 且 $a - 1 \geq   - 1,{2a} + 3 \leq  4,\therefore 0 \leq  a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$
            \end{dingautolist}
            综上，$a <  - 4$ 或 $0 \leq  a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ .
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\clearpage
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \{ x \mid  {5x} - a \leq  0\} ,B = \{ x \mid  {6x} - b > 0\} ,a,b \in  \mathbf{N}$ ,且 $A \cap  B \cap  \mathbf{N} = \{ 2,3,4\}$ ,则整数对$(a, b)$的个数为\blkda[4]{30}
    \begin{jieda}
        $A = \left(-\infty, \dfrac{a}{5}\right]$，$B = \left(\dfrac{b}{6}, +\infty\right)$，根据$A \cap  B \cap  \mathbf{N} = \{ 2,3,4\}$可知
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $4 \in A$，$5 \notin A$，故$\dfrac{a}{5} \in [4, 5)$，即$a \in \{20, 21, 22, 23, 24\}$
            \item $2 \in B$，$1 \notin B$，故$\dfrac{b}{6} \in [1,2)$，即$b \in \{6,7,8,9,10,11\}$
        \end{dingautolist}
        故整数对$(a, b)$的个数为$30$.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 集合 $P$ 具有性质 “若 $x \in  P$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{x} \in  P$ ”,就称集合 $P$ 是伙伴关系的集合,集合 $A = \{  - 1,0$ , $\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{3},1,2,3,4\}$ 的所有非空子集中具有伙伴关系的集合的个数为\blkda[4]{15}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意得 3 和 $\dfrac{1}{3}$ 、2 和 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ 、$-1$与自身、$1$与自身 分别为 4 组满足条件 $x$ 与 $\dfrac{1}{x}$ 的伙伴,所以满足伙伴关系的非空子集有 ${2}^{4} - 1 = {15}$ (个).
    \end{jieda}
    \item 在整数集中,被 4 除所得余数为 $k$ 的所有整数组成一个 “类”,记为 $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,即 $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   =$  $\{ {4n} + k \mid  n \in  \mathbf{Z}\} ,k = 0,1,2,3$ . 给出下列四个结论:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item ${2021} \in  \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack$
        \item $- 1 \in  \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack$
        \item ${Z} = \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  2\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$
        \item “整数 $a\text{ 、 }b$ 属于同一‘类’” 的充要条件是 “ $a - b \in  \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack$ ”
    \end{dingautolist}
    其中,正确结论的序号是\blkda[4]{\ding{172}\ding{174}\ding{175}}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 因为 ${2021} = 4 \times  {505} + 1$ ,则 ${2021} \in  \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack$
        \item  $- 1 = 4 \times  \left( {-1}\right)  + 3$ ,则 $- 1 \in  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$
        \item 任意整数除以 4,余数可以且只可以是 0、1、2、3四类,则 $\mathbf{Z} = \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  2\right\rbrack   \cup$ [3]
        \item 
        充分性：
        
        若整数 $a\text{ 、 }b$ 属于同一 “类”,则整数 $a\text{ 、 }b$ 被 4 除的余数相同,可设 $a = 4{n}_{1} + k,b =$  $4{n}_{2} + k$ ,其中 ${n}_{1}$ 、 ${n}_{2} \in  \mathbf{Z},k \in  \{ 0,1,2,3\}$ ,则 $a - b = 4\left( {{n}_{1} - {n}_{2}}\right)$ ,故 $a - b \in  \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack$

        必要性：
        
        若 $a - b \in  \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack$ ,不妨令 $a = 4{n}_{1} + {k}_{1},b = 4{n}_{2} + {k}_{2}\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2} \in  \mathbf{Z},{k}_{1},{k}_{2} \in  \{ 0,1,2,3\} }\right)$ ,则 $a -$  $b = 4\left( {{n}_{1} - {n}_{2}}\right)  + \left( {{k}_{1} - {k}_{2}}\right)$ ,显然 ${n}_{1} - {n}_{2} \in  \mathbf{Z}$ , $\left| {{k}_{1} - {k}_{2}}\right|  \in  \{ 0,1,2,3\}$ ,于是得 $\left| {{k}_{1} - {k}_{2}}\right|  =$ 0,所以 ${k}_{1} = {k}_{2}$ ,即整数 $a,b$ 属于同一“类”
        
        所以“整数 $a,b$ 属于同一‘类’” 的充要条件是 “ $a - b \in  \left\lbrack  0\right\rbrack$ ”
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $A = \left\{  {x \mid  x = {a}^{2} - {b}^{2},a \in  \mathbf{Z},b \in  \mathbf{Z},x\text{ 为正整数, }x \leq  {40}}\right\}$ ,则集合 $A$ 中共含\blkda[4]{30} 个元素
    \begin{jieda}
        $a^2-b^2 = (a+b)(a-b)$，因为$(a-b)$和$(a+b)$奇偶性相同，故当$x$只能分解为一个奇数和一个偶数相乘时，不在集合$A$内，即$2$的奇数倍（$2,6,10,14,18,22,26,30,34,38$）不在集合$A$中.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合$M = \left\{x \left| \dfrac{ax- 5}{x^2-a} < 0 \right.\right\}$，若$3 \in M, 5 \notin M$，则实数$a$的取值范围是\blkda[3]{$\left[1, \dfrac{5}{3}\right) \cup (9, 25]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item $3 \in M \Longrightarrow \dfrac{3a - 5}{9-a} < 0$
            \item $5 \notin M \Longrightarrow \dfrac{5a - 5}{25-a} \geq 0$ 或 $a = 25$(此时$\dfrac{5a - 5}{25-a}$无意义，满足条件）
        \end{enumerate}
        求解分式不等式：
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item $\dfrac{3a - 5}{9-a} < 0 \Longrightarrow (3a-5)(9-a) < 0 \Longrightarrow a \in (-\infty, \dfrac{5}{3}) \cup (9, +\infty)$
            \item $\dfrac{5a - 5}{25-a} \geq 0 \Longrightarrow (5a-5)(25-a) \geq 0, a \neq 25 \Longrightarrow a \in [1, 25)$
        \end{enumerate}
        两个不等式的解集的交集并上$\{25\}$，得到$\left[1, \dfrac{5}{3}\right) \cup (9, 25]$，即是$a$的取值范围
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合$A = \{(x, y) | |x| + 2|y| \leq 4\}$，集合$B = \left\{(x, y) \left| (x-m)^2 + y^2 = \dfrac{4}{5}\right.\right\}$，若$B \subseteq A$，则实数$m$的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$[-2,2]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        首先可以判断出来集合$A, B$都是点集，因此应先找到其在坐标系内对应的图形\\
        集合$A$所对应的区域是坐标系内以$(0, 2), (4, 0), (0, -2), (-4, 0)$为顶点的菱形区域内部（包括边界），而集合$B$对应的是以$(m, 0)$为圆心，$\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}$为半径的圆\\
        由$B \subseteq A$ 可知圆不可超出菱形的边界已知$A, B$都是关于$y$轴对称，因此先考虑$m > 0$的相切的边界场景根据三角形相似可以得到$\dfrac{4-m}{2\sqrt{5}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}}{2}$，此时$m = 2$故$m \in [-2,2]$
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\clearpage
\subsection*{D Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 如果集合 $A = \{ 1,2,3,\cdots ,8\}$ 的子集 $B$ 满足: 若 ${2k} \in  B,k$ 为正整数,则 ${2k} + 1 \in  B$ ,则称 $B$ 为 $A$ 的“好子集”, $A$ 的“好子集”有\blkda[4]{54}个
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item “好子集”中不能有8
            \item 对于绑定的元素，$2,3$，$4,5$，$6,7$来讲，存在三种情况，两个数都在“好子集”中，只有其中的奇数在“好子集”中，两个数都不在“好子集”中
        \end{dingautolist}
        因此，根据乘法原理可知“好子集”有$2\times3\times3\times3 = 54$个.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 十个元素组成的集合 $M = \{ {19},{93}, - 1,0,{25}, - {78}, - {94},1,{17}, - 2\}$  $M$ 的所有非空子集记为 ${M}_{i}\left( {i = 1,2,\cdots ,{1023}}\right)$ ,每一非空子集中所有元素的乘积记为 ${m}_{i}(i = 1,2,\cdots$ , 1023),则 ${m}_{1} + {m}_{2} + \cdots  + {m}_{1023} =$ \blkda[4]{$-1$}
    \begin{jieda}
        将$M_i$分成三类
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $\{-1\}$
            \item $S_i$满足$-1 \in S_i$，且$S_i \neq \{-1\}$
            \item $T_i$满足$-1 \notin T_i$
        \end{dingautolist}
        易知$S_i$与$T_i$一一对应，且其元素乘积互为相反数，因此在计算目标值的时候，这两部分的和为$0$，故目标值为$-1$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设集合 $S,T,S \subseteq  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * },T \subseteq  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * },S,T$ 中,至少有两个元素,且 $S,T$ 满足: \ding{172}对于任意 $x$ , $y \in  S$ ,若 $x \neq  y$ ,都有 ${xy} \in  T$ ; \ding{173}对于任意 $x,y \in  T$ ,若 $x < y$ ,则 $\dfrac{y}{x} \in  S$ . 若 $S$ 有 4 个元素,则 $S \cup  T$ 有\blkda[4]{7}个元素.
    \begin{jieda}
        设集合 $S = \left\{  {{p}_{1},{p}_{2},{p}_{3},{p}_{4}}\right\}$ ,且 ${p}_{1} < {p}_{2} < {p}_{3} < {p}_{4}$，${p}_{1},{p}_{2},{p}_{3},{p}_{4} \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,则 $p_1p_2, p_1p_3,p_1p_4, p_2p_3,p_2p_4,p_3p_4 \in  T$，则$\dfrac{p_2}{p_1},\dfrac{p_3}{p_1},\dfrac{p_4}{p_1},\dfrac{p_3}{p_2},\dfrac{p_4}{p_2},\dfrac{p_4}{p_3} \in S$，且$\dfrac{p_3p_4}{p_1p_2}, \dfrac{p_2p_4}{p_1p_3} \in S$

        易知$p_4 \geq \dfrac{p_3p_4}{p_1p_2} > \dfrac{p_4}{p_1}>\dfrac{p_4}{p_2} > \dfrac{p_4}{p_3} \geq p_1$

        故必有$\dfrac{p_3p_4}{p_1p_2} = p_4$，$ \dfrac{p_4}{p_1} = p_3$，$\dfrac{p_4}{p_2} = p_2$，$\dfrac{p_4}{p_3} = p_1$，即$S = \{p_1, p_1^2, p_1^3, p_1^4\}$

        故此时可知$T = \{p_1^3, p_1^4, p_1^5, p_1^6, p_1^7\}$，$S \cup  T = \left\{  {{p}_{1},{p}_{1}^{2},{p}_{1}^{3},{p}_{1}^{4},{p}_{1}^{5},{p}_{1}^{6},{p}_{1}^{7}}\right\}$ , 即 $S \cup  T$ 中有 7 个元素.
    \end{jieda} 
\end{enumerate}
\section*{韦达定理、不等式的性质、一元二次方程根的分布}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} - {5x} + 3 = 0$ 的两个实数根,则 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} + \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}}$ 的值是\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{19}{3}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because {x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} - {5x} + 3 = 0$ 的两个实数根,
        
        $\therefore {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{3}{1} = 3,{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{-5}{1} = 5$ ,
        
        $\therefore \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} + \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}} = \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{5}^{2} - 2 \times  3}{3} = \dfrac{19}{3}$ .
        
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {4x} - 1$ 的两个零点分别为 ${x}_{1}$ 和 ${x}_{2}$ ,则 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}$ 的值为\blkda[4]{18}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {4x} - 1$ 的两个零点分别为 ${x}_{1}$ 和 ${x}_{2}$，所以 ${x}_{1}$ 和 ${x}_{2}$ 是 ${x}^{2} - {4x} - 1 = 0$ 的两个实根，所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 4,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 1$，所以 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} = {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 2{x}_{2}{x}_{2} = {4}^{2} - 2 \times  \left( {-1}\right)  = {18}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若方程 ${x}^{2} - {3x} - 1 = 0$ 的两实数根分别是 ${x}_{1}$ 和 ${x}_{2}$ . 则 $\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}} =$ \blkda[4]{$- 3$}, $\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  =$ \blkda[4]{$\sqrt{13}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由韦达定理知 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 1$ ,
        
        所以 $\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{3}{-1} =  - 3$
        
        ${\left( {x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} = {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = 9 + 4 = {13}$ ,
        
        $\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  = \sqrt{13}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} + x - 3 = 0$ 的两个实数根,则 ${x}_{1}^{3} + {x}_{2}^{3} =$ \blkda[4]{$-10$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} + x - 3 = 0$ 的两个实数根，所以 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{1} - 3 = 0{x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{2} - 3 = 0$，即 ${x}_{1}^{2} = 3 - {x}_{1},{x}_{2}^{2} = 3 - {x}_{2}$ ,且 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - 1,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 3$ ,
        
        所以 ${x}_{1}^{3} + {x}_{2}^{3} = \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) \left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) \left( {3 - {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + 3 - {x}_{2}}\right) =  - 1 \times  \left( {6 + 1 + 3}\right)  =  - {10}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $a,b,c \in  \mathbf{R},a > b$ ,则下列不等式中成立的是\blkda[4]{\ding{174}}(填上正确的序号).
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $\dfrac{1}{a} < \dfrac{1}{b}$
        \item ${a}^{2} > {b}^{2}$
        \item $\dfrac{a}{{c}^{2} + 1} > \dfrac{b}{{c}^{2} + 1}$
        \item $a\left| c\right|  > b\left| c\right|$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $a = 1,b =  - 1,c = 0$ 时,有 $a > b$ ,但 $\dfrac{1}{a} = 1 >  - 1 = \dfrac{1}{b},{a}^{2} = 1 = {b}^{2},a\left| c\right|  = 0 = b\left| c\right|$，故\ding{172}\ding{173}\ding{175}错误

        由于 $a > b,\dfrac{1}{{c}^{2} + 1} > 0$ ,故 $\dfrac{a}{{c}^{2} + 1} > \dfrac{b}{{c}^{2} + 1}$，故\ding{174}正确
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $x,y \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则 “ $x > 0,y > 0$ ” 是 “ ${xy} > 0$ ” 的\blkda[3]{充分不必要}条件, “ ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} > 0$ ” 是 “ $x \neq  0$ ” 的\blkda[3]{必要不充分}条件. (填“充分不必要”或“必要不充分”或“充要”或“既不充分也不必要”)
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $x > 0,y > 0$ 可得 ${xy} > 0$
        
        由 ${xy} > 0$ ,得 $x > 0,y > 0$ 或 $x < 0,y < 0$ 
        
        所以 “ $x > 0,y > 0$ ” 是 “ ${xy} > 0$ ” 的充分不必要条件；
        
        由 $x \neq  0$ 可得 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} > 0$
        
        由 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} > 0$ ,得 $x \neq  0$ 或 $y \neq  0$
        
        所以 “ ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} > 0$ ” 是 “ $x \neq  0$ ” 的必要不充分条件.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 满足 $a > b$ ,下列不等式中正确的是\blkda[4]{\ding{174}}(只填序号).
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $\left| a\right|  > \left| b\right|$
        \item ${a}^{2} > {b}^{2}$
        \item ${a}^{3} > {b}^{3}$
        \item $\dfrac{a}{b} > 1$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        对于\ding{172}\ding{173}\ding{175}，取 $a = 2,b =  - 3$ ,满足 $a > b$ ,而$\left| a\right|  = 2 < 3 = \left| b\right| ,{a}^{2} = {2}^{2} < {\left( -3\right) }^{2} = {b}^{2},\dfrac{a}{b} =  - \dfrac{2}{3} < 1$，故错误；

        对于\ding{174}，${a}^{3} - {b}^{3} = \left( {a - b}\right) \left( {{a}^{2} + {ab} + {b}^{2}}\right)  = \left( {a - b}\right) \left\lbrack  {{\left( a + \dfrac{1}{2}b\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}{b}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,

        由 $a > b$ ,得 $a - b > 0,{\left( a + \dfrac{1}{2}b\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}{b}^{2} > 0$ ,因此 ${a}^{3} - {b}^{3} > 0,{a}^{3} > {b}^{3}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,“ $a > 1$ ” 是 “ $\dfrac{1}{a} < 1$ ” 的一个\blkda[3]{充分非必要}条件(充分非必要、必要非充分、充要、既非充分又非必要)
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{1}{a} < 1 \Longleftrightarrow a \in (-\infty, 0) \cup (0, 1)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $- 1 < x < 4,2 < y < 3$ ,则 ${3x} + {2y}$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(1,18)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $- 1 < x < 4,2 < y < 3$，所以 $- 3 < {3x} < {12},4 < {2y} < 6$，得 ${3x} + {2y} \in  \left( {-3 + 4,{12} + 6}\right)  = \left( {1,{18}}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $x > 3,y > 4$ ,则 ${xy}$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left( {{12}, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $x > 3,y > 4 > 0$，所以 ${xy} > {3y} > 3 \times  4 = {12}$，即 ${xy}$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {{12}, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $\alpha ,\beta$ 满足 $- \dfrac{\pi }{2} < \alpha  \leq  \beta  \leq  \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,则 $\alpha  - \beta$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$( - \pi ,0\rbrack$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $- \dfrac{\pi }{2} < \alpha  \leq  \beta  \leq  \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,所以 $- \dfrac{\pi }{2} < \alpha  \leq  \dfrac{\pi }{2}, - \dfrac{\pi }{2} \leq   - \beta  < \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,
        
        $\therefore  - \pi  < \alpha  - \beta  < \pi$，又 $\alpha  - \beta  \leq  0$，$\therefore  - \pi  < \alpha  - \beta  \leq  0$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 日常生活中,在一杯含有 $a$ 克糖的 $b$ 克糖水中 $\left( {b > a > 0}\right)$ ,再加入 $m$ 克糖 $\left( {m > 0}\right)$ (假设全部溶解), 这杯糖水变甜了. 请根据这一事实提炼出一个不等式\blkda[6]{$b > a > 0,m > 0$ 时, $\dfrac{a}{b} < \dfrac{a + m}{b + m}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意有 $b > a > 0,m > 0$ 时, $\dfrac{a}{b} < \dfrac{a + m}{b + m}$ .
        
        理由如下: $\dfrac{a}{b} - \dfrac{a + m}{b + m} = \dfrac{a\left( {b + m}\right)  - b\left( {a + m}\right) }{b\left( {b + m}\right) } = \dfrac{m\left( {a - b}\right) }{b\left( {b + m}\right) }$ ,因为 $b > a > 0,m > 0$ ,
        
        所以 $\dfrac{a}{b} - \dfrac{a + m}{b + m} = \dfrac{m\left( {a - b}\right) }{b\left( {b + m}\right) } < 0$ ,即 $\dfrac{a}{b} < \dfrac{a + m}{b + m}$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 给出四个条件:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $a{c}^{2} > b{c}^{2}$
        \item $\dfrac{a}{c} > \dfrac{b}{c}$
        \item $a > \left| b\right|$
        \item $a > b - 1$
    \end{dingautolist}
    其中能成为 $a > b$ 的充分条件的是\blkda[4]{\ding{172}\ding{174}}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 若 $a{c}^{2} > b{c}^{2}$ ,则 ${c}^{2} > 0$ ,故 $a > b$ ,即 $a{c}^{2} > b{c}^{2}$ 是 $a > b$ 的充分条件
            \item 当 $c < 0$ 时,由 $\dfrac{a}{c} > \dfrac{b}{c}$ 可得 $a < b$ ,故 $\dfrac{a}{c} > \dfrac{b}{c}$ 不是 $a > b$ 的充分条件
            \item 当 $b \geq  0$ 时, $a > \left| b\right|  = b$ ,当 $b < 0$ 时, $a > \left| b\right|  =  - b > b$ ,故 $a > \left| b\right|$ 是 $a > b$ 的充分条件
            \item 当 $a = {0.5},b = 1$ 时,满足 $a > b - 1$ ,但是不满足 $a > b$ ,故 $a > b - 1$ 不是 $a > b$ 的充分条件
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $- 1 \leq  x - y \leq  4, - 2 \leq  x + {6y} \leq  3$ ,则 $z = x - {8y}$ 的取值范围是\blkda[2]{$- 5 \leq  z \leq  {10}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设 $x - {8y} = m\left( {x - y}\right)  + n\left( {x + {6y}}\right)  = \left( {m + n}\right) x + \left( {{6n} - m}\right) y$ ，则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m + n = 1 \\  {6n} - m =  - 8 \end{array}\right.$ ,故 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} n =  - 1 \\  m = 2 \end{array}\right.$ ,
        
        因为 $- 1 \leq  x - y \leq  4, - 2 \leq  x + {6y} \leq  3$ ,则 $- 2 \leq  2\left( {x - y}\right)  \leq  8, - 3 \leq   - \left( {x + {6y}}\right)  \leq  2$ ,
        
        故 $- 5 \leq  2\left( {x - y}\right)  - \left( {x + {6y}}\right)  \leq  {10}$ 即 $- 5 \leq  z \leq  {10}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $x,y$ 为实数,满足 $3 \leq  x{y}^{2} \leq  8,4 \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y} \leq  9$ ,则 $\dfrac{x}{{y}^{3}}$ 的最小值是\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{2}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设 $\dfrac{x}{{y}^{3}} = {\left( x{y}^{2}\right) }^{m} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y}\right) }^{n}$
        
        即 $x{y}^{-3} = {x}^{m + {2n}} \cdot  {y}^{{2m} - n}$
        
        所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m + {2n} = 1 \\  {2m} - n =  - 3 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m =  - 1 \\  n = 1 \end{array}\right.$
        
        所以 $\dfrac{x}{{y}^{3}} = {\left( x{y}^{2}\right) }^{-1} \cdot  \left( \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y}\right)$
        
        因为 $3 \leq  x{y}^{2} \leq  8,4 \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y} \leq  9$ ,
        
        所以 $\dfrac{1}{8} \leq  {\left( x{y}^{2}\right) }^{-1} \leq  \dfrac{1}{3}$
        
        由不等式性质可知 $\dfrac{1}{2} \leq  {\left( x{y}^{2}\right) }^{-1} \cdot  \left( \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y}\right)  \leq  3$
        
        即 $\dfrac{1}{2} \leq  \dfrac{x}{{y}^{3}} \leq  3$ ,当且仅当 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{y} = 4 \\  {\left( x{y}^{2}\right) }^{-1} = \dfrac{1}{8} \end{array}\right.$ 时取等号,解得 $x = {2}^{\frac{7}{5}},y = {2}^{\frac{4}{5}}$ .
        
        综上可知, $\dfrac{x}{{y}^{3}}$ 的最小值为 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若方程 ${x}^{2} - {8x} + m = 0$ 的两根为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,且 $3{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2} = {18}$ ,则 $m =$ \blkda[4]{12}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由韦达定理得, ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 8,{x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = m,\Delta  = {64} - {4m} > 0$ ，即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 8 \\  3{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2} = {18} \\  {x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = m \\  \Delta  = {64} - {4m} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,可解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} = 2 \\  {x}_{2} = 6 \\  m = {12} \end{array}\right.$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知关于 $x$ 的一元二次方程 ${x}^{2} - {6x} + \left( {{4m} + 1}\right)  = 0$ 的两个实数根为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,且 $\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  = 4$ , 则实数 $m$ 的值为\blkda[4]{1}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because {x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 为方程 ${x}^{2} - {6x} + \left( {{4m} + 1}\right)  = 0$ 的两个实数根,

         $\therefore {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 6,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = {4m} + 1,\Delta  = {36} - 4\left( {{4m} + 1}\right)  \geq  0$ ,故 $m < 2$
        
        $\because \left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  = 4$ ,则 ${\left( {x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} = {16},\therefore {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = {16}$ ,
        
         $\therefore {36} - 4\left( {{4m} + 1}\right)  = {16}$ ,解得 $m = 1$ .
        
        $\because m < 2,\therefore m = 1$ 符合题意.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 $a \neq  b$ ,且 ${a}^{2} - {4a} + 1 = 0$ , ${b}^{2} - {4b} + 1 = 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{1 + {a}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{1 + {b}^{2}}$ 的值为\blkda[4]{1}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意 $a,b$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} - {4x} + 1 = 0$ 的两个解,
        
        所以 $a + b = 4,{ab} = 1$ ,则 ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} = {\left( a + b\right) }^{2} - {2ab} = {14}$ ,
        
        $\dfrac{1}{1 + {a}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{1 + {b}^{2}} = \dfrac{2 + {a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}{\left( {1 + {a}^{2}}\right) \left( {1 + {b}^{2}}\right) } = \dfrac{2 + {a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}{1 + {a}^{2} + {b}^{2} + {a}^{2}{b}^{2}} = \dfrac{2 + {14}}{1 + {14} + 1} = 1$
    \end{jieda}

    
    \item 方程 ${x}^{2} - {ax} + a - 1 = 0$ 有一正一负根的充要条件是\blkda[4]{$a < 1$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${x}^{2} - {ax} + a - 1 = 0$ 有一正一负根 $\Leftrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} a - 1 < 0 \\  \Delta  = {\left( -a\right) }^{2} - 4\left( {a - 1}\right)  > 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  a < 1}\right.$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知关于 $x$ 的方程 ${x}^{2} - {2x} + m - 1 = 0$ 的两个实数根同号,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$(1,2\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        根据题意得到 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \Delta  \geq  0 \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 4 - 4\left( {m - 1}\right)  \geq  0 \\  m - 1 > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $1 < m \leq  2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知一元二次方程 $m{x}^{2} - {2x} + m + 3 = 0$ 有两个正实根,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[3]{$0 < m \leq  \dfrac{\sqrt{13} - 3}{2}$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        设两个正实数根分别为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} m \neq  0 \\  \Delta  = 4 - {4m}\left( {m + 3}\right)  \geq  0 \\  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{2}{m} > 0 \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{m + 3}{m} > 0 \end{array}, \Longrightarrow  0 < m \leq  \dfrac{\sqrt{13} - 3}{2}}\right.$ .
    \end{jieda}

    \item 若关于 $x$ 的一元二次方程 ${x}^{2} + \left( {{3a} - 1}\right) x + a + 8 = 0$ 有两个不相等的实根 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,且${x}_{1} < 1,{x}_{2} > 1$ . 则实数 $a$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$(-\infty, -2)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + \left( {{3a} - 1}\right) x + a + 8$ ,依题意, $f\left( x\right)  = 0$ 的两个不等实根 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 满足 ${x}_{1} < 1,{x}_{2} > 1$ ,
        
        而函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 图象开口向上,因此 $f\left( 1\right)  < 0$ ,则 ${1}^{2} + \left( {{3a} - 1}\right)  \times  1 + a + 8 < 0$ ,解得 $a <  - 2$，所以实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $a <  - 2$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知方程 ${x}^{2} - {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 4 = 0$ 的一个实根小于 2 ,另一个实根大于 2 ,求实数 $a$ 的取值范围\blkda[4]{$(0, 4)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 4$ ,
        
        因为方程 ${x}^{2} - {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 4 = 0$ 的一个实根小于 2,另一个实根大于 2,
        
        则满足 $f\left( 2\right)  = {a}^{2} - {4a} < 0$ ,解得 $0 < a < 4$ ,即实数 $a$ 的取值范围为$(0,4)$.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 方程 ${x}^{2} - \left( {2 - a}\right) x + 5 - a = 0$ 的两根都大于 2 ,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(- 5,-4]$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意,方程 ${x}^{2} - \left( {2 - a}\right) x + 5 - a = 0$ 的两根都大于 2,
        
        令 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - \left( {2 - a}\right) x + 5 - a$ ,
        
        可得 $\left\{  {\begin{matrix} \Delta  \geq  0 \\  f\left( 2\right)  > 0 \\  \dfrac{2 - a}{2} > 2 \end{matrix},\text{ 即 }\left\{  \begin{matrix} {a}^{2} \geq  {16} \\  a + 5 > 0 \\  2 - a > 4 \end{matrix}\right. }\right.$

        解得  $- 5 < a \leq   - 4$.
    \end{jieda}
    
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + {2mx} + {2m} + 3$ 在区间$(0,2)$内有零点,求实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left(-\dfrac{3}{2}, -1\right]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 分对称轴位置讨论 \\
            $f(x)$对称轴为$x = -m$，$f(0) = 2m+3$，$f(2) = 7+6m$，$\Delta = 4m^2 - 8m - 12$
            \begin{enumerate}[1.]
                \item 若$-m \leq 0$，则$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$单调增，故“$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$上有零点”即$f(0) < 0, f(2) > 0$，此种情况无解.
                \item $-m \geq 2$，则$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$单调减，故“$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$上有零点”即$f(0) > 0, f(2) < 0$，此种情况无解.
                \item $-m \in (0, 2)$，则$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$先减后增，“$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$上有零点”即$\Delta \geq 0$，且有$f(0) > 0$或$f(2) > 0$，此种情况解得$m \in \left(-\dfrac{3}{2}, -1\right]$
            \end{enumerate}
            综上，$m \in \left(-\dfrac{3}{2}, -1\right]$
            \item 参变分离 \\
            $f(x)$至少有一个零点在区间$(0, 2)$内，即$f(x) = 0$在$(0, 2)$内有根，即方程$m = \dfrac{-3-x^2}{2x+2}$在$(0, 2)$内有根，即方程$m = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot \dfrac{-3-(t-1)^2}{t}$在$t \in (1, 3)$内有根，即方程$m = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot \left[-(t+\dfrac{4}{t})+2\right]$在$t \in (1, 3)$内有根.\\
            因此$m$的取值范围即$y = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot [-(t+\dfrac{4}{t})+2]$在$t \in (1, 3)$内的值域. \\
            $y = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot \left[-(t+\dfrac{4}{t})+2\right]$在$(1, 2)$单调增，在$(2, 3)$单调减，因此值域为$\left(-\dfrac{3}{2}, -1\right]$，故$m \in \left(-\dfrac{3}{2}, -1\right]$
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {2ax} + 5$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有零点,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$[\sqrt{5}, 3)$ }.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 分对称轴位置讨论

            对称轴为$x = a$，$f(1) = 6-2a$，$f(3) = 14-6a$，$\Delta = 4a^2-20$

            \begin{enumerate}[1.]
                \item 若$a \leq 1$，则$f(x)$在$(1, 3)$严增，故$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有零点即$f(1) < 0, f(3) > 0$，此种情况无解.
                \item 若$a \geq 3$，则$f(x)$在$(1, 3)$严减，故$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有零点即$f(1) > 0, f(3) < 0$，此种情况无解.
                \item $a \in (1, 3)$，则$f(x)$在$(1, 3)$先减后增，故$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有零点即$\Delta \geq 0, f(1) > 0$或$f(3)> 0$，解得$a \in [\sqrt{5}, 3)$.
            \end{enumerate}
            
            \item 参变分离

            函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {2ax} + 5$ 在区间(1,3)上有零点,即 ${2a} = \dfrac{{x}^{2} + 5}{x}$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有解,即 ${2a} = x + \dfrac{5}{x}$ 在区间$(1,3)$上有解
            
            令 $g\left( x\right)  = x + \dfrac{5}{x},x \in  \left( {1,3}\right)$ ,易知 $g\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {1,\sqrt{5}}\right)$ 上单调递减,在区间 $\left( {\sqrt{5},3}\right)$ 上单调递增，所以 $g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = g\left( \sqrt{5}\right)  = 2\sqrt{5},g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = g\left( 1\right)  = 6$，$g\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {2\sqrt{5},6}\right)$，故$\sqrt{5} \leq  a < 3$ .
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知方程 ${x^2} + {2mx} + {2m} + 1 = 0$ 的两不等实数根均在区间$(0,1)$内,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围为\blkda[3]{$\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2},1 - \sqrt{2}}\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 通解法

            令 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + {2mx} + {2m} + 1$，则函数图象开口向上,对称轴为直线 $x =  - m$ .
            
            则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \Delta  > 0 \\  0 <  - m < 1 \\  f\left( 0\right)  > 0 \\  f\left( 1\right)  > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{1}{2} < m < 1 - \sqrt{2}$ .
            
            故 $m$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2},1 - \sqrt{2}}\right)$ .
            \item 参变分离

            ${x^2} + {2mx} + {2m} + 1 = 0 \Longleftrightarrow m = -\dfrac{-x^2-1}{2x+2}$

            换元$t = x+1, t \in (1, 2)$，则$m = \dfrac{-t^2+2t-2}{2t}$在$t \in(1, 2)$有两根，即函数$y = m$与函数$y = -\dfrac{1}{2}\left(t + \dfrac{2}{t} - 2\right)$在$t \in (1, 2)$有两交点.

            $y = -\dfrac{1}{2}\left(t + \dfrac{2}{t} - 2\right)$在$(1, \sqrt{2})$严增，在$(\sqrt{2}, 2)$严减，故$m \in \left( {-\dfrac{1}{2},1 - \sqrt{2}}\right)$满足要求.
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若二次方程 ${ax^2} - \left( {4 - {a}^{2}}\right) x + 2 = 0$ 在$(0,2)$内恰有一个实根,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围为\blkda[3]{$( - 3,0)\cup (0, 1\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为是二次方程，所以$a \neq 0$，设$f(x) = {ax^2} - \left( {4 - {a}^{2}}\right) x + 2$，对称轴为$x = \dfrac{4-a^2}{a}$，$f(0) = 2$，$f(2) = 2a^2+4a-6$
        \begin{enumerate}[1.]
            \item “符合函数零点存在定理”，因为$f(0) > 0$，故此时应有$f(2) < 0$，即$a \in (-3, 0) \cup (0,1)$
            \item 右端点函数值为0，此时有$a \in \{-3, 1\}$，根据韦达定理另一根为$\dfrac{1}{a}$，故当$a = 1$时，$f(x)$在$(0, 2)$存在唯一零点.
        \end{enumerate}
        综上，$(-3,0)\cup (0, 1\rbrack$
        
        \ps 重根算作两根，因此无需验证$\Delta = 0$时，顶点是否在区间内.
    \end{jieda}

    \item 定义$max M$表示数集$M$中最大的数，则$max\left\{\dfrac{1}{a}, \dfrac{1}{b}, a^2+b^2\right\}$的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\sqrt[3]{2}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        考虑特例$\dfrac{1}{a} = \dfrac{1}{b} = a^2+b^2$，此时$a = b = \sqrt[3]{\dfrac{1}{2}}$，$max\left\{\dfrac{1}{a}, \dfrac{1}{b}, a^2+b^2\right\} = \sqrt[3]{2}$ 下面反证法证明$max\left\{\dfrac{1}{a}, \dfrac{1}{b}, a^2+b^2\right\}$取得最小值为$\sqrt[3]{2}$. \\
        假设$max\left\{\dfrac{1}{a}, \dfrac{1}{b}, a^2+b^2\right\}$的最小值为$m < \sqrt[3]{2}$
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 设$\dfrac{1}{a} = m, \dfrac{1}{b} \leq m, a^2+b^2 \leq m$ \\
            此时$a = \dfrac{1}{m}, b \geq \dfrac{1}{m}$，故$a^2+b^2 \geq \dfrac{2}{m^2} > \sqrt[3]{2} > m$，矛盾.
            \item 设$\dfrac{1}{a} \leq m, \dfrac{1}{b} = m, a^2+b^2 \leq m$\\
            此时$a \geq \dfrac{1}{m}, b = \dfrac{1}{m}$，故$a^2+b^2 \geq \dfrac{2}{m^2} > \sqrt[3]{2} > m$，矛盾.
            \item 设$\dfrac{1}{a} \leq m, \dfrac{1}{b} \leq m, a^2+b^2 = m$ \\
            此时$a \geq \dfrac{1}{m}, b \geq \dfrac{1}{m}$，故$a^2+b^2 \geq \dfrac{2}{m^2} > \sqrt[3]{2} > m$，矛盾.
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 定义$max M$表示数集$M$中最大的数.设$0<a<b<c<1$，已知$b\geq 2a$且$a+b\leq 1$，则$max\left\{b-a, c-b, 1-c\right\}$的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{4}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        设$A = a, B = b-a, C = c - b, D = 1 - c$，则$A+B+C+D = 1$且有$A \leq B$或$2A+B \leq 1$，目标值为$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$

        考虑特例$B = C = D$，则此时有$A+3B = 1$，故$A = 1-3B$
        
        因此$A \leq B$且$2A+B \leq 1$即$1-3B \leq B$且$2-5B \leq 1$，即$B \geq \dfrac{1}{4}$，故有特例$A = B = C = D = \dfrac{1}{4}$
        
        下面反证法证明此特例是使得$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$取得最小值的例子.

        假设$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$可取到最小值$m < \dfrac{1}{4}$
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $B = m < \dfrac{1}{4}, C \leq m, D \leq m$

            此时$m = B \geq A = 1 - B - C - D \geq 1-3m$，即$4m \geq 1$，矛盾
            \item $B \leq m, C = m < \dfrac{1}{4}, D \leq m$

            此时$m \geq B \geq A = 1 - B - C - D \geq 1-3m$，即$4m \geq 1$，矛盾
            
            \item $B \leq m, C \leq m , D = m < \dfrac{1}{4}$
            与上一情况对称
        \end{dingautolist}
        因此$max\left\{b-a, c-b, 1-c\right\}$的最小值为$\dfrac{1}{4}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 定义$max M$表示数集$M$中最大的数.设$0<a<b<c<1$，已知$b\geq 2a$或$a+b\leq 1$，则$max\left\{b-a, c-b, 1-c\right\}$的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{5}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        设$A = a, B = b-a, C = c - b, D = 1 - c$，则$A+B+C+D = 1$且有$A \leq B$或$2A+B \leq 1$，目标值为$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$

        
        考虑特例$B = C = D$，则此时有$A+3B = 1$，故$A = 1-3B$
        
        因此$A \leq B$或$2A+B \leq 1$即$1-3B \leq B$或$2-5B \leq 1$，即$B \geq \dfrac{1}{4}$或$B \geq \dfrac{1}{5}$，故有特例$A = \dfrac{2}{5}, B = C = D = \dfrac{1}{5}$
        
        下面反证法证明此特例是使得$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$取得最小值的例子.

        
        假设$max\left\{B, C, D\right\}$可取到最小值$m < \dfrac{1}{5}$
        
        
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $B = m < \dfrac{1}{5}, C \leq m, D \leq m$

            此时$m = B \geq A = 1 - B - C - D \geq 1-3m$，即$4m \geq 1$，且有$2A + B \geq 2 - 5m > 1$，故$2A+B > 1$，矛盾.
            
            \item $B \leq m, C = m < \dfrac{1}{5}, D \leq m$

            此时$m \geq B \geq A = 1 - B - C - D \geq 1-3m$，即$4m \geq 1$，且有$2A + B \geq 2 - 5m > 1$，故$2A+B > 1$，矛盾.
            
            \item $B \leq m, C \leq m , D = m < \dfrac{1}{5}$
            与上一情况对称
        \end{dingautolist}
        
        因此$max\left\{b-a, c-b, 1-c\right\}$的最小值为$\dfrac{1}{5}$
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\section*{解不等式（高次、分式、根式）、不等式解集与方程的根、整根问题}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item $\left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x + 3}\right)  > x - 2$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(-\infty, -2) \cup (2, +\infty)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x + 3}\right)  > x - 2 \Longleftrightarrow\left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  > 0$，所以 $x <  - 2$ 或 $x > 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则不等式 ${x}^{2} - {2x} - 3 < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(-1, 3)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        方程 ${x}^{2} - {2x} - 3 = 0$ 的解为 $x =  - 1$ 或 $x = 3$

        故不等式 ${x}^{2} - {2x} - 3 < 0$ 的解集为 $\{ x \mid   - 1 < x < 3\}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $x + {x}^{3} \geq  0$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 0, + \infty )$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $x + {x}^{3} \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow x\left( {1 + {x}^{2}}\right)  \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow x \geq 0$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\left( {{x}^{2} - 4}\right) {\left( x - 6\right) }^{2} \leq  0$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$[-2, 2] \cup \{6\}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left( {{x}^{2} - 4}\right) {\left( x - 6\right) }^{2} \leq  0 \Longleftrightarrow \left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right) {\left( x - 6\right)  }^{2} \leq  0$

        穿针引线可得解集为$[-2, 2] \cup \{6\}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\dfrac{x - 1}{x - 3} < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(1,3)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{x - 1}{x - 3} < 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right)  < 0$ ,解得 $1 < x < 3$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {\dfrac{x}{x + 2} < 0}\right. }\right\}  ,B = \{  - 1,0,1\}$ ,则 $A \cap  B =$ \blkda[4]{$\{ - 1\}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{x}{x + 2} < 0 \Longleftrightarrow  x\left( {x + 2}\right)  < 0, \Longleftrightarrow - 2 < x < 0$ ,
        
        所以 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {\dfrac{x}{x + 2} < 0}\right. }\right\}   = \{ x \mid   - 2 < x < 0\}$ ,又 $B = \{  - 1,0,1\}$，所以 $A \cap  B = \{  - 1\}$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 不等式 $\dfrac{\left( {{2x} - 1}\right) {\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}}{{x}^{2} - 4} \geq  0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-2,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{\left( {{2x} - 1}\right) {\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}}{{x}^{2} - 4} \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             {x}^{2} - 4 \neq  0  \\
             \left( {{x}^{2} - 4}\right) \left( {{2x} - 1}\right) {\left( x + 1\right) }^{2} \geq  0 
        \end{array}\right.$

        穿针引线可得解集为$\left( {-2,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\dfrac{5 - x}{{x}^{2} - {2x} - 3} \leq   - 1$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-1,1\rbrack \cup \lbrack 2,3}\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{5 - x}{{x}^{2} - {2x} - 3} \leq   - 1 \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{{x}^{2} - {3x} + 2}{{x}^{2} - {2x} - 3} \leq  0 \Longleftrightarrow\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {x + 1}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right)  \leq  0 \\  x \neq   - 1\text{ 且 }x \neq  3 \end{array}\right.$

        穿针引线可得解集为$\left( {-1,1\rbrack \cup \lbrack 2,3}\right)$ 
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知函数 $y = f\left( x\right) \left( {x \in  R}\right)$ 的图象如图所示,则不等式 ${xf}\left( x\right)  < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{ $\left( {-1,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {1,3}\right)$}.
    
    \begin{center}
    \includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2alapjef24c73avqjjg_2_241_1430_502_220_0.jpg}
    \end{center}
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 ${xf}\left( x\right)  < 0$ 等价为 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x > 0 \\  f\left( x\right)  < 0 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x < 0 \\  f\left( x\right)  > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 
        
        根据图可知 $1 < x < 3$ ,或 $- 1 < x < 0$，故不等式 ${xf}\left( x\right)  < 0$ 的解集是 $\left( {-1,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {1,3}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\sqrt{x + 3} < 2$ 的解是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack  - 3,1)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\sqrt{x + 3} < 2 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             x \geq -3  \\
             x+3 < 4
        \end{array}\right. \Longleftrightarrow -3 \leq  x < 1$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\dfrac{\sqrt{x + 1}}{x - 6} \leq  0$ 的解为\blkda[4]{$\lbrack  - 1,6)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{\sqrt{x + 1}}{x - 6} \leq  0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + 1 \geq  0 \\  x - 6 < 0 \end{array}\right. \Longleftrightarrow - 1 \leq  x < 6$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {{x}^{2} - x - 2 \leq  0}\right. }\right\}  ,B = \left\{  {x\left| {\sqrt{x - 1} \leq  2}\right. }\right\}$ ,则 $A \cap  B =$ \blkda[4]{$[1, 2]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $B = \left\{  {x \mid  \sqrt{x - 1} \leq  2}\right\}   = \{ x \mid  0 \leq  x - 1 \leq  4\}  = \{ x \mid  1 \leq  x \leq  5\}$
        
        $A = \{ x \mid   - 1 \leq  x \leq  2\}$，$A \cap  B = \{ x \mid  1 \leq  x \leq  2\}$
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 关于 $x$ 的不等式 $\dfrac{\left( {x + 3}\right) {\left( x - 2\right) }^{999}{\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}}{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2015}{\left( x - 4\right) }^{2016}} \leq  0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(- \infty , - 3 \rbrack  \cup  \{  - 1\}  \cup  (1,2\rbrack$}
    \begin{jieda}
        原不等式等价于: $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {x + 3}\right) {\left( x - 2\right) }^{999}{\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2015}{\left( x - 4\right) }^{2016} \leq  0, \\  x - 1 \neq  0, \\  x - 4 \neq  0, \end{array}\right. $
        
        结合高次不等式奇穿偶不穿的性质求解不等式组可得原不等式的解集为: $(- \infty , - 3 \rbrack  \cup  \{  - 1\}  \cup  (1,2\rbrack$
    \end{jieda} 
    
    \item 不等式 $\sqrt{{4x} - {x}^{2}} < x-1$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left(\dfrac{3+\sqrt{7}}{2}, 4\right]$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\sqrt{{4x} - {x}^{2}} < x-1 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             {4x} - {x}^{2} \geq 0  \\
             x-1 > 0 \\
             {4x} - {x}^{2} < x^2-2x+1
        \end{array}\right. \\
        \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             0 \leq x \leq 4  \\
             x > 1 \\
             x \in \left(-\infty, \dfrac{3-\sqrt{7}}{2}\right) \cup \left(\dfrac{3+\sqrt{7}}{2}, +\infty \right) 
        \end{array}\right. \Longleftrightarrow x \in \left(\dfrac{3+\sqrt{7}}{2}, 4\right]$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\left( {x - 2}\right) \sqrt{x + 3} \geq  0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 2, + \infty ) \cup  \{  - 3\}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $\sqrt{x+3} > 0$且$x-2 \geq 0$，解得$x \in [2, +\infty)$
            \item $\sqrt{x+3} = 0$且$x-2 \in \mathbf{R}$，解得$x = -3$
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\sqrt{4 - {x}^{2}} > x$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack  - 2,\sqrt{2})$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\sqrt{4 - {x}^{2}} > x \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             4-x^2 \geq 0 \\
             x \geq 0 \\
             4 - x^2 > x^2
        \end{array}\right.$或$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             4-x^2 \geq 0 \\
             x < 0 
        \end{array}\right.$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 解关于 $x$ 的不等式: 
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item $\sqrt{x - 1} + {2x} \leq  5$ 
        \item $\dfrac{{ax} - 1}{x - 2} > \dfrac{a}{2}\left( {a \in  \mathbf{R}}\right)$
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item $\sqrt{x - 1} + {2x} \leq  5 \Longleftrightarrow \sqrt{x-1} \leq 5 - 2x \Longleftrightarrow \left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                 x \geq 1  \\
                 5-2x \geq 0 \\
                 x-1 \leq (5-2x)^2
            \end{array}\right.$，解得$x \in [1, 2]$
            \item $\dfrac{{ax} - 1}{x - 2} > \dfrac{a}{2} \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{{ax} - 1}{x - 2} - \dfrac{a}{2} > 0 \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{ax+2a-2}{2(x-2)} > 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
                 (ax+2a-2)(x-2) > 0 \\
                 x \neq 2
            \end{array}\right. \\
            \Longleftrightarrow (ax+2a-2)(x-2) > 0$
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item $a = 0$

                $(ax+2a-2)(x-2) > 0\Longleftrightarrow 
                 (-2)(x-2) > 0 \Longleftrightarrow x \in (-\infty, 2)$
            \item $a > 0$

            $(ax+2a-2)(x-2) > 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left(x+2-\dfrac{2}{a}\right)(x-2) > 0 $

            方程$\left(x+2-\dfrac{2}{a}\right)(x-2) = 0$的两根分别为$\dfrac{2}{a}-2$和$2$

            \begin{enumerate}[1.]
                \item $a > \dfrac{1}{2}$

                $\dfrac{2}{a}-2 < 2$，故原不等式解集为$\left(-\infty, \dfrac{2}{a}-2\right)\cup (2, +\infty)$

                
                \item $a = \dfrac{1}{2}$

                $\dfrac{2}{a}-2 = 2$，故原不等式解集为$\left(-\infty, 2\right)\cup (2, +\infty)$

                
                \item $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$

                $\dfrac{2}{a}-2 > 2$，故原不等式解集为$(-\infty, 2) \cup \left(\dfrac{2}{a}-2, +\infty\right)$
            \end{enumerate}

            
            \item $a < 0$

            $ax+2a-2)(x-2) > 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left(x+2-\dfrac{2}{a}\right)(x-2) < 0$

            方程$\left(x+2-\dfrac{2}{a}\right)(x-2) = 0$的两根分别为$\dfrac{2}{a}-2$和$2$

            因为$a < 0$，所以$\dfrac{2}{a}-2 < 2$恒成立，故原不等式解集为$\left(\dfrac{2}{a}-2, 2\right)$
            
            \end{dingautolist}
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {\dfrac{{ax} - 2}{x - a} \leq  0}\right. }\right\}$ ,若 $2 \notin  A$ ,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(1,2\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        集合 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {\dfrac{{ax} - 2}{x - a} \leq  0}\right. }\right\}$ ,若 $2 \notin  A$ ,故 $\dfrac{{2a} - 2}{2 - a} > 0$ 或 $2 - a = 0$ ,解得 $1 < a \leq  2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $\alpha  : \dfrac{2}{x + 1} > 1,\beta  : m \leq  x \leq  2$ ,若 $\alpha$ 是 $\beta$ 的充分条件,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(-\infty, -1]$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{2}{x + 1} > 1 \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{1 - x}{x + 1} > 0\Longleftrightarrow \left( {1 - x}\right) \left( {1 + x}\right)  > 0$，解得 $- 1 < x < 1$，即 $\alpha  :  - 1 < x < 1$ 
        
        若 $\alpha$ 是 $\beta$ 的充分条件,则 $(-1, 1) \subseteq \{ x \mid  m \leq  x \leq  2\}$ ，可得 $m \in (-\infty, -1]$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 “ $\left( {x - m}\right) \left( {x - m - 2}\right)  < 0$ ” 是 “ $\dfrac{{2x} + 1}{x - 1} < 0$ ” 的必要非充分条件,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-1, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left( {x - m}\right) \left( {x - m - 2}\right)  < 0$ 解得 $m < x < m + 2$，$\dfrac{{2x} + 1}{x - 1} < 0$ 解得 $- \dfrac{1}{2} < x < 1$ .
        
        由题意可得得 $\left(- \dfrac{1}{2}, 1\right) \subset (m, m + 2)$ 包含 $- \dfrac{1}{2} < x < 1$，故 $m \in  \left\lbrack  {-1, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$
    \end{jieda}
    \item  已知集合 $A = \left\{  {x\left| {\dfrac{7}{x + 3} \geq  1}\right. }\right\}  ,B = \left\{  {x\left| {{x}^{2} - {3mx} + 2{m}^{2} + m - 1 < 0}\right. }\right\}$ ,若 $B \subset  A$ ,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-1,\dfrac{5}{2}}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{7}{x + 3} \geq  1 \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{7}{x + 3} - 1 \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{4 - x}{x + 3} \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {x + 3}\right) \left( {x - 4}\right)  \leq  0 \\  x + 3 \neq  0 \end{array}\right.$，故$A = ( - 3,4\rbrack$
        
        ${x}^{2} - {3mx} + 2{m}^{2} + m - 1 < 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\lbrack  {x - \left( {m + 1}\right) }\right\rbrack  \left\lbrack  {x - \left( {{2m} - 1}\right) }\right\rbrack  < 0$

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 $m + 1 = {2m} - 1$ ,即 $m = 2$ 时, $B = \varnothing$ ,满足 $B \subset  A$ ,符合题意
            \item 当 $m + 1 > {2m} - 1$ ,即 $m < 2$ 时, $B = \left( {{2m} - 1,m + 1}\right)$，由 $B \subset  A$ ,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2m} - 1 \geq   - 3 \\  m + 1 \leq  4 \end{array}\right.$，解得 $- 1 \leq  m < 2$
            \item 当 $m + 1 < {2m} - 1$ ,即 $m > 2$ 时, $B = \left( {m + 1,{2m} - 1}\right)$，由 $B \subset  A$ ,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m + 1 \geq   - 3 \\  {2m} - 1 \leq  4 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $2 < m \leq  \dfrac{5}{2}$
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$m \in \left\lbrack  {-1,\dfrac{5}{2}}\right\rbrack$
    \end{jieda}
    \item ${ax} + b < 0$ 的解集为 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$ ,则 $\left( {{ax} - b}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(-2,1)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
         ${ax} + b < 0$ 的解集为 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$，即$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
              b = a  \\
              a > 0
         \end{array}\right.$

         故$\left( {{ax} - a}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  < 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  < 0$，解得$- 2 < x < 1$ 
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $a{x}^{2} + {bx} + 2 > 0$ 的解集是 $\left\{  {x\left| { - \dfrac{1}{2} < x < \dfrac{1}{3}}\right. }\right\}$ ,则 $a + b =$ \blkda[4]{$-14$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 $a{x}^{2} + {bx} + 2 > 0$ 的解集是 $\left\{  {x\left| { - \dfrac{1}{2} < x < \dfrac{1}{3}}\right. }\right\} \Longleftrightarrow \left\{  \begin{array}{l} a < 0 \\   - \dfrac{b}{a} =  - \dfrac{1}{6}, \\  \dfrac{2}{a} =  - \dfrac{1}{6} \end{array}\right.$ 
        
        解得$\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} a =  - {12} \\  b =  - 2 \end{array}}\right.$，故$a + b =  - {14}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若关于 $x$ 的不等式 $a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c < 0$ 的解集是 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {3, + \infty }\right)$ ,则关于 $x$ 的不等式$a{x}^{2} + {cx} + {2b} \geq  0$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-1,4}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 $a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c < 0$ 的解集是 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {3, + \infty }\right) \Longleftrightarrow \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  
        a < 0 \\
        a-b+c = 0 \\  
        9+3b+c =  0 
        \end{array}\right.$
        
        解得 $\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             a < 0 \\
             b =  - {2a}  \\
             c =  - {3a} 
        \end{array}\right.$，故$a{x}^{2} + {cx} + {2b} \geq  0 \Longleftrightarrow a{x}^{2} - {3ax} - {4a} \geq  0$
        
        因 $a < 0$ ,故得 ${x}^{2} - {3x} - 4 \leq  0$ ,解得 $x \in \left\lbrack  {-1,4}\right\rbrack$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 关于 $x$ 的不等式 ${x}^{2} - \left( {1 + {2a}}\right) x + {2a} < 0$ 的解集中恰有 2 个整数,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-1, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{3}{2},2}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        ${x}^{2} - \left( {1 + {2a}}\right) x + {2a} < 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x - {2a}}\right)  < 0$ ,

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 ${2a} = 1$ 时,不等式化为 ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} < 0$ ,此时无解
            \item 当 ${2a} > 1$ 时,解得 $1 < x < {2a}$ ,要使解集中恰有两个整数,则 $3 < {2a} \leq  4$ ,得 $\dfrac{3}{2} < a \leq  2$
            \item 当 ${2a} < 1$ 时,解得 ${2a} < x < 1$ ,要使解集中恰有两个整数,则 $- 2 \leq  {2a} <  - 1$ ,得 $- 1 \leq  a <  - \dfrac{1}{2}$
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$a \in \left\lbrack  {-1, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{3}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知关于 $x$ 的不等式组 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} - {2x} - 8 > 0, \\  2{x}^{2} + \left( {{2k} + 7}\right) x + {7k} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ 仅有一个整数解,则实数 $k$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$ [-5, 3) \cup (4,5]$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 ${x}^{2} - {2x} - 8 > 0$ 的解集为 $\{ x \mid  x <  - 2$ 或 $x > 4\}$ .
        
        不等式 $2{x}^{2} + \left( {{2k} + 7}\right) x + {7k} < 0$ 化为 $\left( {x + k}\right) \left( {x + \dfrac{7}{2}}\right)  < 0$ .

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 $k = \dfrac{7}{2}$ 时,不等式组的解集为 $\varnothing$ ,不符合题意.
            
            \item 当 $k > \dfrac{7}{2}$ 时,不等式 $2{x}^{2} + \left( {{2k} + 7}\right) x + {7k} < 0$ 的解集为 $\left\{x\left |  { - k < x <  - \dfrac{7}{2}}\right. \right\}$，由解集中的整数为$-4$,得 $- 5 \leq   - k <  - 4$ ,解得 $4 < k \leq  5$ .

            \item 当 $k < \dfrac{7}{2}$ 时,不等式 $2{x}^{2} + \left( {{2k} + 7}\right) x + {7k} < 0$ 的解集为 $\left\{  {x\left| { - \dfrac{7}{2} < x <  - k}\right. }\right\}$，由解集中的整数为$-3$,得 $- 3 <  - k \leq  5$ ,解得 $- 5 \leq  k < 3$ .
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$k \in [-5, 3) \cup (4,5]$.
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知关于$x$的不等式组$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
         \dfrac{x-a}{x-1+a} < 0\\
         x+3a>1 
    \end{array}\right.$的整数解恰有两个，则实数$a$的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(1, 2]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        首先可以确定第一个不等式的解集为$(a, 1-a)$或$(1-a, a)$，因为$a$和$1-a$关于$\dfrac{1}{2}$对称，因此$\dfrac{1}{2}$必然在该不等式的解集中。而第二个不等式的解集为$(1-3a, +\infty)$
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 若$a \in [0, 1]$，则$(a, 1-a)$或$(1-a, a)$没有整数解，故整个不等式组的解集中也没有整数解.
            \item 若$a < 0$，则$a < 0 < 1 < 1-a < 1-3a$，则整个不等式组解集为空集.
            \item 若$a > 1$，则$1-3a < 1-a < 0 < 1 < a$整个不等式组的解集为$(1-a, a)$，此时解集中必有两个整数解$0, 1$，故当$a \in (1, 2]$时，满足要求.
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$a \in (1, 2]$ \\
        \ps 本题画数轴可以更方便分析，本题判断出$a$和$1-a$关于$\dfrac{1}{2}$对称后可以猜测到两个整根是0和1.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知不等式组$ \left\{ \begin{array}{l}
         x^2-x+a-a^2 < 0  \\
         x + 2a > 1
    \end{array} \right.$的整数解恰好有两个，$a$的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$(1, 2]$}
    \begin{jieda}
        设函数$f(x) = x^2 - x$，则第一个不等式即$f(x) < f(a)$，根据函数的性质可知不等式即$\left|x-\dfrac{1}{2}\right| < \left|a - \dfrac{1}{2}\right|$，故$\dfrac{1}{2}-\left|a - \dfrac{1}{2}\right| < x < \dfrac{1}{2} + \left|a - \dfrac{1}{2}\right|$，故第一个不等式解集为$(1 - a, a)$或$(a, 1-a)$，第二个不等式解集为$(1-2a, +\infty)$
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $a \in [0, 1]$，此时第一个不等式中不含有两个整数解，不符合要求.
            \item $a < 0$，第一个不等式解集为$(a, 1-a)$，而$1-a < 1-2a$，故不等式组解集为空集，不符合要求.
            \item $a > 1$，第一个不等式解集为$(1 - a, a)$，故不等式解集为$(1 - a, a)$，当$a \in (1, 2]$满足要求.
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若关于$x$的不等式$k|x| > |x - 2|$恰有4个整数解，则实数$k$的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left(\dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{2}{3}\right]$}
        \begin{jieda}
            易知$x = 0$不是不等式的解，故$k|x| > |x - 2|$进行参变分离，同解变形$k > \left|\dfrac{x-2}{x}\right|$，即$k > \left|1 - \dfrac{2}{x}\right|$ \\
            由函数$f(x) = \left|1 - \dfrac{2}{x}\right|$的图像可知整根的集合为$\{2, 3, 4, 5\}$，故$f(5) < k \leq f(6)$，即$k \in \left(\dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{2}{3}\right]$
            \ps \textbf{解决整根问题的第一步往往是确认整根的集合}
        \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知关于 $x$ 的不等式组: $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 4{x}^{2} - {4ax} =  - {3x} - a + 1, \\  x > \dfrac{a - 1}{4} \end{array}\right.$ 有且只有一个实数解,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty ,1\rbrack \cup \lbrack 2, + \infty }\right)  \cup  \left\{  \dfrac{5}{4}\right\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $4{x}^{2} - {4ax} =  - {3x} - a + 1$ ,得 $4{x}^{2} - \left( {{4a} - 3}\right) x + \left( {a - 1}\right)  = 0$ ,进而得 $\lbrack x - (a -$ 1)] $\left( {{4x} - 1}\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $x = a - 1$ 或 $x = \dfrac{1}{4}$ .
        
        因为不等式组有且只有一个实数解，所以 
        
        $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a - 1 > \dfrac{a - 1}{4}, \\  \dfrac{1}{4} \leq  \dfrac{a - 1}{4} \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a - 1 \leq  \dfrac{a - 1}{4}, \\  \dfrac{1}{4} > \dfrac{a - 1}{4} \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a - 1 = \dfrac{1}{4} \\  \dfrac{1}{4} > \dfrac{a - 1}{4}\end{array}\right.$
        
        解得 $a \in ( - \infty ,1\rbrack  \cup   \lbrack 2, + \infty ) \cup  \left\{  \dfrac{5}{4}\right\}$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{D Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 设关于 $x$ 的不等式 $\dfrac{{x}^{2} + \left( {2{a}^{2} + 2}\right) x - {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7}{{x}^{2} + \left( {{a}^{2} + {4a} - 5}\right) x - {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7} < 0$ 的解集是一些区间的并集,且这些区间的长度和 (规定: 区间$(a, b)$的长度为 $b - a$ ) 不小于 12,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 \dotfill(\kh{A})
    \xx{$a \leq   - 1$ 或 $a \geq  5$}{$a <  - 1$ 或 $a \geq  5$}{$a <  - 2$ 或 $a \geq  3$}{$a \leq   - 2$ 或 $a \geq  3$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为$- {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7 < 0$，而分子分母的二次项系数均为1，因此分子分母对应的方程均有互异实根，设${x}^{2} + \left( {2{a}^{2} + 2}\right) x - {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7 = 0$的两根分别为$x_1, x_2$，${x}^{2} + \left( {{a}^{2} + {4a} - 5}\right) x - {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7 = 0$的两根分别为$x_3, x_4$，
        
        根据上述分析可知$x_1 < 0 < x_2, x_3 < 0 < x_4$，目标式为$|x_2-x_4| + |x_1-x_3|$

        $x_1 x_2 = x_3x_4 = - {a}^{2} + {4a} - 7 < 0$，因为乘积相同，故必有$x_1 < x_3, x_2 < x_4$或$x_1 > x_3, x_2 > x_4$，因此$x_2-x_4,x_1-x_3$正负性相同，故目标式可以化为$|(x_1+x_2)-(x_3+x_4)|$
        
        而根据$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             x_1 + x_2 = \left( 2{a}^{2} + 2\right)  \\
             x_3 + x_4 = \left( {{a}^{2} + {4a} - 5}\right)
        \end{array}\right.$可知$(x_1+x_2) - (x_3+x_4) = a^2-4a+7 > 0$，故目标式为$(x_1+x_2)-(x_3+x_4) = a^2-4a+7 \geq 12$，解得$a \in (-\infty, -1] \cup [5, +\infty)$

    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\section*{基本不等式、 对勾函数}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 不等式 $a + \dfrac{1}{a} \geq  2$ 成立的充要条件是\blkda[4]{$a > 0$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        均值不等式 $a + \dfrac{1}{a} \geq  2$ 成立的条件是 $a > 0$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item “ $a = 1$ ” 是 “ ${x}^{2} + \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}} \geq  2$ ” 的\blkda[4]{充分不必要}条件. (填“充分非必要”、“必要非充分”、“充分必要”、“既不充分也不必要”)
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $a = 1$ 时, ${x}^{2} + \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}} = {x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} \geq  2\sqrt{{x}^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}} = 2$，当且仅当 ${x}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} \Longrightarrow  x =  \pm  1$ 时,取等号,所以充分性成立
        
        由 ${x}^{2} + \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}} \geq  2\sqrt{{x}^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}}} = 2\sqrt{a} \geq  2$ ，所以 $a \geq  1$ ,故必要性不成立,、
        
        所以 “ $a = 1$ ” 是 “ ${x}^{2} + \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}} \geq  2$ ” 的充分不必要条件
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $a,b \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,且 ${ab} > 0$ ,则下列不等式中,恒成立的序号是\blkda[4]{\ding{175}\ding{176}\ding{177}}.
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} > {2ab}$
        \item $a + b \geq  2\sqrt{ab}$
        \item $\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{1}{b} > \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{ab}}$
        \item $\dfrac{b}{a} + \dfrac{a}{b} \geq  2$
        \item $\dfrac{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}{2} \geq  {ab}$
        \item $\dfrac{{\left( a + b\right) }^{2}}{4} \leq  \dfrac{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}{2}$
        \item $\dfrac{a + b}{2} \geq$  $\dfrac{ab}{a + b}$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        易知因为 ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} \geq  {2ab}$ 对于 $a,b \in  \mathbf{R}$ 恒成立,当且仅当 $a = b$ 时,等号成立,所以 \ding{172} 错误，\ding{176}\ding{177}正确;

        对于\ding{173},\ding{174},显然 $a < 0,b < 0$ 时,不等式均不成立,即\ding{173}和\ding{174}错误；

        对于\ding{175},因为 ${ab} > 0$ ,所以 $\dfrac{b}{a} > 0,\dfrac{a}{b} > 0$ ,由基本不等式可得 $\dfrac{b}{a} + \dfrac{a}{b} \geq  2\sqrt{\dfrac{b}{a} \cdot  \dfrac{a}{b}} = 2$ ,当且仅当 $a = b$ 成立即\ding{175}正确;

        对于\ding{178}，当$a=b=-1$时不等式不成立，故错误
    \end{jieda}
    \item  若 $0 < x < 1,0 < y < 1$ ,则 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2}\text{ 、 }x + y\text{ 、 }{2xy}\text{ 、 }2\sqrt{xy}$ 中最大的一个是\blkda[4]{$x + y$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $0 < x < 1,0 < y < 1$ ,由基本不等式得 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} \geq  {2xy};x + y \geq  2\sqrt{xy}$ ;
        
        又因为 $0 < x < 1,0 < y < 1$ ,
        
        所以 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - \left( {x + y}\right)  = {x}^{2} - x + {y}^{2} - y = x\left( {x - 1}\right)  + y\left( {y - 1}\right)  < 0$ ,
        
        故 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} < x + y$ ,
        
        所以最大的一个是 $x + y$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $x > 1$ ,则 $x + \dfrac{1}{x - 1}$ 的最小值是\blkda[4]{3}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $x + \dfrac{1}{x - 1} = x - 1 + \dfrac{1}{x - 1} + 1 \geq  2\sqrt{\left( {x - 1}\right)  \times  \dfrac{1}{x - 1}} + 1 = 3$ ,
        
        当且仅当 $x - 1 = \dfrac{1}{x - 1}$ 即 $x = 2$ 时取等号,
        
        $\therefore x = 2$ 时 $x + \dfrac{1}{x - 1}$ 取得最小值 3 .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $x < 0$ ,则函数 ${y} = {x} + \dfrac{1}{{x}}$ 的最大值是\blkda[4]{-2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $x < 0$ ,所以 $- x > 0, - \dfrac{1}{x} > 0$ ,
        
        故 $x + \dfrac{1}{x} =  - \left\lbrack  {\left( {-x}\right)  + \left( {-\dfrac{1}{x}}\right) }\right\rbrack   \leq   - 2\sqrt{\left( {-x}\right) \left( {-\dfrac{1}{x}}\right) } =  - 2$ ,
        
        当且仅当 $- x =  - \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,即 $x =  - 1$ 时,等号成立,
        
        所以 $x < 0$ 时, ${y} = {x} + \dfrac{1}{{x}}$ 的最大值是 -2 .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $a > 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{{a}^{2} + 4}{a}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{{a}^{2} + 4}{a} = a + \dfrac{4}{a} \geq  2\sqrt{a \cdot  \dfrac{4}{a}} = 4,a = 2$ 时取等号
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $x$ 、 $y$ 满足 $x + y = {10}$ ,且 $x$ 、 $y$ 都是正数,则 ${xy}$ 的最大值为\blkda[4]{25}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由于 $x$ 、 $y$ 都是正数,故 ${xy} \leq  {\left( \dfrac{x + y}{2}\right) }^{2} = {25}$ ,当且仅当 $x = y = 5$ 时等号成立，故 ${xy}$ 的最大值为 25
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知正实数 $x,y$ 满足: $x + \dfrac{1}{y} = 1$ ,则 $\dfrac{x}{y}$ 的最大值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{4}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{x}{y} = x \times  \dfrac{1}{y} \leq  \dfrac{1}{4}{\left( x + \dfrac{1}{y}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}$，当且仅当 $x = \dfrac{1}{y} = \dfrac{1}{2}$，即 $x = \dfrac{1}{2},y = 2$ 时取得等号
        
        故 $\dfrac{x}{y}$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{1}{4}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知正实数 $a,b$ 满足 ${ab} = 8$ ,则 $a + {2b}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{8}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $a,b > 0,{ab} = 8$，故$b = \dfrac{8}{a}$ ，所以 $a + {2b} = a + 2 \cdot  \dfrac{8}{a} \geq  2\sqrt{a \cdot  \dfrac{16}{a}} = 8$ ,
        
        当且仅当 $a = \dfrac{16}{a}$ ,即 $a = 4,b = 2$ 时取等号，所以 $a + {2b}$ 的最小值为 8
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 $a > 0,b > 0$ ,且 $a + b = 4$ ,则下列不等式恒成立的是\blkda[4]{\ding{175}}(填序号).
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $\dfrac{1}{ab} \leq  \dfrac{1}{4}$
        \item $\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{1}{b} \leq  1$
        \item $\sqrt{ab} \geq  2$
        \item ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} \geq  8$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $4 = a + b \geq  2\sqrt{ab}$ (当且仅当 $a = b$ 时,等号成立)，即 $\sqrt{ab} \leq  2,{ab} \leq  4,\dfrac{1}{ab} \geq  \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,故\ding{172}\ding{174}不成立;
        
        $\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{1}{b} = \dfrac{a + b}{ab} = \dfrac{4}{ab} \geq  1$ ,故\ding{173}不成立;
        
        ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} = {\left( a + b\right) }^{2} - {2ab} = {16} - {2ab} \geq  8$ ,故\ding{175}成立.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知全集 $U = R$ ,实数 $a,b$ 满足 $a > b > 0$ ,集合 $M = \left\{  {x \mid  \sqrt{ab} < x < a}\right\}  ,N = \{ x \mid  b < x < \dfrac{a + b}{2}\}$ ,则 $\overline{M} \cap  N =$ \blkda[4]{$\left\{  {x\left| {b < x \leq  \sqrt{ab}}\right. }\right\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由于 $\overline{M} = \left\{  {x \mid  x \leq  \sqrt{ab}\text{ 或 }x \geq  a}\right\}$ ,又 $a > b > 0,b < \sqrt{ab} < \dfrac{a + b}{2} < a$ 则
        
        $\overline{M} \cap  N = \left\{  {x \mid  b < x \leq  \sqrt{ab}}\right\}$        
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 函数 $y = \dfrac{x + 1}{\sqrt{x} - 1}\left( {x > 1}\right)$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$2\sqrt{2} + 2$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $y = \dfrac{x + 1}{\sqrt{x} - 1} = \dfrac{x - 1}{\sqrt{x} - 1} + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 1} = \sqrt{x} - 1 + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 1} + 2 \geq  2\sqrt{\left( {\sqrt{x} - 1}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 1}} + 2 = 2\sqrt{2} + 2$，当且仅当 $\sqrt{x} - 1 = \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x} - 1}$ ,即 $x = 3 + 2\sqrt{2}$ 时,等号成立.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 下列各式中, 最小值为4的是\dotfill(\kh{B})
    \xx
    {$x+2\sqrt{x}+5$}
    {$\dfrac{x^2+5}{\sqrt{x^2+1}}$}
    {$x^2+3+\dfrac{4}{x^2+3}$}
    {$x+\dfrac{4}{x}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        注意取等条件是否可以取到！
    \end{jieda}
    \item 函数 $y = \dfrac{2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 5}{x - 1}$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{3}{2},3}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值和最小值的乘积为\blkda[4]{${40}\sqrt{2} + {10}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        令 $t = x - 1,x = t + 1,\because x \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{3}{2},3}\right\rbrack  ,\therefore t \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        $\therefore y = \dfrac{2{\left( t + 1\right) }^{2} - 3\left( {t + 1}\right)  + 5}{t} = \dfrac{2{t}^{2} + t + 4}{t} = 2\left( {t + \dfrac{2}{t}}\right)  + 1$ ,
        
        令 $g\left( t\right)  = 2\left( {t + \dfrac{2}{t}}\right)  + 1,t \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        由对勾函数的性质可知,函数 $g\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上为减函数,在 $\left\lbrack  {\sqrt{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 上为增函数,
        
        $g\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = {10},g\left( \sqrt{2}\right)  = 4\sqrt{2} + 1,g\left( 2\right)  = 7$，故$g{\left( t\right) }_{\max } = {10},g{\left( t\right) }_{\min } = 4\sqrt{2} + 1$
        
        因此函数 $y = \dfrac{2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 5}{x - 1}$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{3}{2},3}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值和最小值的乘积为 ${40}\sqrt{2} + {10}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $a,b > 0,a + \dfrac{1}{b} = 1$ ,则 $b + \dfrac{1}{a}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        易知 $b + \dfrac{1}{a} = \left( {b + \dfrac{1}{a}}\right) \left( {a + \dfrac{1}{b}}\right)  = {ab} + \dfrac{1}{ab} + 2 \geq  2\sqrt{{ab} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{ab}} + 2 = 4$ ,
        
        当且仅当 ${ab} = 1$ ,即 $a = \dfrac{1}{2},b = 2$ 时取得最小值.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知实数 $a,b$ 满足 $- 1 < a < 1 < b$ ,且 $a + b = 2$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3a}{b - 1}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\sqrt{3} - 1$}.
    \begin{jieda}
    
        由 $a + b = 2$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3a}{b - 1} = \dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3\left( {2 - b}\right) }{b - 1} = \dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3}{b - 1} - 3$ .

        $\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3}{b - 1} - 3 = \dfrac{1}{2}\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3}{b - 1}}\right\rbrack  \left\lbrack  {\left( {a + 1}\right)  + \left( {b - 1}\right) }\right\rbrack   - 3 = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {1 + 3 + \dfrac{b - 1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{3\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b - 1}}\right)  - 3 \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {4 + 2\sqrt{\dfrac{b - 1}{a + 1} \cdot  \dfrac{3\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b - 1}}}\right)  - 3 = \sqrt{3} - 1$
        
        当且仅当 $\dfrac{b - 1}{a + 1} = \dfrac{3\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b - 1}$ ,即 $a =  - 2 + \sqrt{3},b = 4 - \sqrt{3}$ 时,等号成立.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $a > 0,b > 0$ ,且 $\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{2}{b + 1} = 1$ ,则 $a + b$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$2\sqrt{2} + 1$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $a > 0,b > 0,\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{2}{b + 1} = 1$，得 $a + b = \left( {a + 1}\right)  + \left( {b + 1}\right)  - 2 = \left( {\dfrac{1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{2}{b + 1}}\right) \left\lbrack  {\left( {a + 1}\right)  + \left( {b + 1}\right) }\right\rbrack   - 2 = \dfrac{b + 1}{a + 1} + \dfrac{2\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b + 1} + 1 \geq  2\sqrt{\dfrac{b + 1}{a + 1} \cdot  \dfrac{2\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b + 1}} + 1 = 2\sqrt{2} + 1$
        
        当且仅当 $\dfrac{b + 1}{a + 1} = \dfrac{2\left( {a + 1}\right) }{b + 1}$ ,即 $a = \sqrt{2},b = \sqrt{2} + 1$ 时, $a + b$ 取得最小值 $2\sqrt{2} + 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $a > 0,b > 0$ ,且 ${9a} + b = {ab}$ ,则 $a + {4b}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{49}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $a > 0,b > 0$ 且 ${9a} + b = {ab}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{9}{b} = 1$ ,
        
        所以 $a + {4b} = \left( {a + {4b}}\right) \left( {\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{9}{b}}\right)  = 1 + {36} + \dfrac{4b}{a} + \dfrac{9a}{b} \geq  {37} + 2\sqrt{\dfrac{4b}{a} \times  \dfrac{9a}{b}} = {37} + {12} = {49}$ ,
        
        当且仅当 $\dfrac{4b}{a} = \dfrac{9a}{b}$ 即 $a = 7,b = \dfrac{21}{2}$ 时取等号，所以 $a + {4b}$ 最小值为 49 .
    \end{jieda}

    \item 已知正数 $x$ 、 $y$ 满足 $x + {2y} = 1$ ,则 $\dfrac{2}{{3x} + {4y}} + \dfrac{1}{x + {3y}}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{8}{5}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设$k(x + {2y}) = a(3x+4y)+b(x+3y)$，即$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             3a+b = k  \\
             4a+3b = 2k
        \end{array}\right.$，$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             a = 1 \\
             b = 2 \\
             k = 5
        \end{array}\right.$是一组解，故$\dfrac{2}{{3x} + {4y}} + \dfrac{1}{x + {3y}} = \dfrac{2}{{3x} + {4y}} + \dfrac{2}{2x + {6y}} = \dfrac{1}{5}\left(\dfrac{2}{{3x} + {4y}} + \dfrac{2}{2x + {6y}}\right) \left((3x+4y)+2(x+3y)\right) = \dfrac{2}{5}\left(1+1+\dfrac{{3x} + {4y}}{2x + {6y}}+\dfrac{2x + {6y}}{{3x} + {4y}}\right) \geq \dfrac{2}{5}(2+2) = \dfrac{8}{5}$ .
    \end{jieda}

    \item 若 $a$ 、 $b$ 为正实数,且 $a + {4b} + 3 = {ab}$，则 ${ab}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{${11} + 4\sqrt{7}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $a + {4b} + 3 = {ab} \Longleftrightarrow ab = \dfrac{4b+3}{b-1} \cdot b$

        令$t = b-1, t > 0$，则$ab = \dfrac{(4t+7)(t+1)}{t} = 4t + \dfrac{7}{t} + 11 \geq 11+4\sqrt{7}$，当且仅当$t = \dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}$时取等号
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知正实数 $x,y$ 满足 ${xy} - x - y = 3$ ,则 ${2x} + y$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$4\sqrt{2} + 3$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        正实数 $x,y$ 满足 ${xy} - x - y = 3$ ,故 $\left( {x - 1}\right) y = 3 + x$ ,所以 $y = \dfrac{3 + x}{x - 1}$ ,
        
        则 $\dfrac{3 + x}{x - 1} > 0$ ,又 $x > 0$ ,解得 $x > 1$ ,
        
        故 ${2x} + y = {2x} + \dfrac{3 + x}{x - 1} = {2x} + 1 + \dfrac{4}{x - 1} = 2\left( {x - 1}\right)  + \dfrac{4}{x - 1} + 3 \geq  2\sqrt{2\left( {x - 1}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{4}{x - 1}} + 3 = 4\sqrt{2} + 3,$
        
        当且仅当 $2\left( {x - 1}\right)  = \dfrac{4}{x - 1}$ ,即 $x = 1 + \sqrt{2}$ 时,等号成立，故 ${2x} + y$ 的最小值为 $4\sqrt{2} + 3$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知 $a > 0,b > 0$ ,且 ${ab} = a - b + 3$ ,则 $a + b$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$2\sqrt{2}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 ${ab} = a - b + 3$ ,解得: $b = \dfrac{a + 3}{a + 1} = 1 + \dfrac{2}{a + 1}$，则 $a + b = a + 1 + \dfrac{2}{a + 1} \geq  2\sqrt{2}$
        
        当且仅当 $a = \sqrt{2} - 1,b = \sqrt{2} + 1$ 时，等号成立
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $a > 0,b > 0$ ,且 ${5ab} + {b}^{2} = 1$ ,则 $a + b$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{4}{5}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 ${5ab} + {b}^{2} = 1$ ,所以 $a = \dfrac{1 - {b}^{2}}{5b} = \dfrac{1}{5b} - \dfrac{b}{5}$ ,

        所以 $a + b = \dfrac{1}{5b} - \dfrac{b}{5} + b = \dfrac{1}{5b} + \dfrac{4b}{5} \geq  2\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{5b} \cdot  \dfrac{4b}{5}} = \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,当且仅当 $a = \dfrac{3}{10},b = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时,等号成立，所以 $a + b$ 的最小值为 $\dfrac{4}{5}$ .
    \end{jieda} 
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 若正实数 $x\text{ 、 }y$ 满足 ${2x} + y = 2$ ,则 $\dfrac{4{x}^{2}}{y + 1} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{2x} + 2}$ 的最小值是\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{4}{5}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        根据题意,若 ${2x} + y = 2$ ,则 $\dfrac{4{x}^{2}}{y + 1} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{2x} + 2} = \dfrac{{\left( 2 - y\right) }^{2}}{y + 1} + \dfrac{{\left( 2 - 2x\right) }^{2}}{2\left( {x + 1}\right) } =$  $\dfrac{{\left\lbrack  \left( y + 1\right)  - 3\right\rbrack  }^{2}}{y + 1} + 2\dfrac{{\left\lbrack  \left( x + 1\right)  - 2\right\rbrack  }^{2}}{x + 1} = \left( {y + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{9}{y + 1} + 2\left( {x + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{16}{2\left( {x + 1}\right) } - {14} = \dfrac{9}{y + 1} +$  $\dfrac{16}{2\left( {x + 1}\right) } - 9$
        
         $\dfrac{4{x}^{2}}{y + 1} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{2x} + 2} =$  $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{9}{y + 1} + \dfrac{16}{2\left( {x + 1}\right) }}\right\rbrack  \dfrac{\left( {{2x} + 2}\right)  + \left( {y + 1}\right) }{5} - 9 \\
         = \dfrac{1}{5}\left\lbrack  {{16} + 9 + \dfrac{{18}\left( {x + 1}\right) }{y + 1} + \dfrac{8\left( {y + 1}\right) }{x + 1}}\right\rbrack   - 9 \geq \dfrac{1}{5}\left\lbrack  {{25} + 2\sqrt{\dfrac{{18}\left( {x + 1}\right) }{y + 1} \times  \dfrac{8\left( {y + 1}\right) }{x + 1}}}\right\rbrack   - 9 = \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,当且仅当 $y + 1 = \dfrac{3}{2}\left( {x + 1}\right)  = \dfrac{15}{7}$ 时等号成立, 即 $\dfrac{4{x}^{2}}{y + 1} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{2x} + 2}$ 的最小值是 $\dfrac{4}{5}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知关于 $x$ 的不等式 $- {x}^{2} + {6ax} - 3{a}^{2} \geq  0\left( {a > 0}\right)$ 的解集为 $\left\lbrack  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + \dfrac{3a}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}$ 的最小值是\blkda{$2\sqrt{6}$}
    \begin{jieda}
         不等式 $- {x}^{2} + {6ax} - 3{a}^{2} \geq  0\left( {a > 0}\right)$ 的解集为 $\left\lbrack  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 ${x}_{1}\text{、}{x}_{2}$ 是方程 ${x}^{2} - {6ax} + 3{a}^{2} = 0$ 的实数根，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = {6a} \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = 3{a}^{2}\end{array}\right.$
         
         ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + \dfrac{3a}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = {6a} + \dfrac{1}{a} \geq  2\sqrt{{6a} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{a}} = 2\sqrt{6}\text{,当且仅当}{6a} =  $
        $\dfrac{1}{a}$ ,即 $a = \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{6}$ 时取等号，所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + \dfrac{3a}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}$ 的最小值为 $2\sqrt{6}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $x \leq   - 1$ 时,不等式 $x + \dfrac{9}{x} - 2 < {k}^{2} - {9k}$ 恒成立,则 $k$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty ,1}\right)  \cup  \left( {8, + \infty }\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
         由于 ${k}^{2} - {9k} > {\left( x + \dfrac{9}{x} - 2\right) }_{\max } =  - 8$ ,所以 $k \in  \left( {-\infty ,1}\right)  \cup  \left( {8, + \infty }\right)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 当 $0 < m < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时,若 $\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{2}{1 - {2m}} \geq  {k}^{2} - {2k}$ 恒成立,则实数 $k$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$[-2,4]$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由于 $0 < m < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  {2m} \cdot  \left( {1 - {2m}}\right)  \leq  \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  {\left\lbrack  \dfrac{{2m} + \left( {1 - {2m}}\right) }{2}\right\rbrack  }^{2} = \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,当且仅当 ${2m} = 1 - {2m}$ ,即 $m = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时取等号,所以 $\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{2}{1 - {2m}} = \dfrac{1}{m\left( {1 - {2m}}\right) } \geq  8$ ,因为 $\dfrac{1}{m} +$  $\dfrac{2}{1 - {2m}} \geq  {k}^{2} - {2k}$ 恒成立,所以 ${k}^{2} - {2k} - 8 \leq  0$ ,所以 $- 2 \leq  k \leq  4$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $\forall x > a$ ,关于 $x$ 的不等式 ${2x} + \dfrac{2}{x - a} \geq  5$ 恒成立,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        若关于 $x$ 的不等式 ${2x} + \dfrac{2}{x - a} \geq  5$ 恒成立，则 ${\left( 2x + \dfrac{2}{x - a}\right) }_{\min } \geq  5$
        
        因为 $x > a$ ,故 ${2x} + \dfrac{2}{x - a} = 2\left( {x - a}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{x - a} + {2a} \geq  2\sqrt{2\left( {x - a}\right)  \times  \dfrac{2}{x - a}} + {2a} = 4 + {2a}$ ，当且仅当 $x = a + 1$ 时取等,故得 $4 + {2a} \geq  5$ ,解得 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $x > 0,y > 0$ 且 $\dfrac{1}{2}x < y < {2x},x + y = 3$ ,若 $\dfrac{1}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2}{{2y} - x} \geq  a$ 恒成立,则实数 $a$ 的范围是 \blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{3 + 2\sqrt{2}}{3}}\right\rbrack$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $x > 0,y > 0$ 且 $x + y = 3$ ,若 $\dfrac{1}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2}{{2y} - x} \geq  a$ 恒成立，则 $a \leq  {\left( \dfrac{1}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2}{{2y} - x}\right) }_{\min }$
        
        $\dfrac{1}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2}{{2y} - x} = \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {\dfrac{1}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2}{{2y} - x}}\right) \left( {{2x} - y + {2y} - x}\right) = \dfrac{1}{3}\left\lbrack  {3 + \dfrac{{2y} - x}{{2x} - y} + \dfrac{2\left( {{2x} - y}\right) }{{2y} - x}}\right\rbrack   \geq  \dfrac{1}{3}\left\lbrack  {3 + 2\sqrt{\dfrac{{2y} - x}{{2x} - y} \cdot  \dfrac{2\left( {{2x} - y}\right) }{{2y} - x}}}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{3 + 2\sqrt{2}}{3}$，当且仅当 $\dfrac{{2y} - x}{{2x} - y} = \dfrac{2\left( {{2x} - y}\right) }{{2y} - x}$ ,即 $x = \sqrt{2},y = 3 - \sqrt{2}$ 时,等号成立
        
        $\therefore a \leq  \dfrac{3 + 2\sqrt{2}}{3}$ ,即实数 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{3 + 2\sqrt{2}}{3}}\right\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}

\end{enumerate}

\section*{含绝对值不等式、不等式恒成立与有解问题}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 不等式 $\left| {x + 1}\right|  > 3$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty , - 4}\right)  \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 $\left| {x + 1}\right|  > 3$ 等价于 ${\left( x + 1\right) }^{2} > 9$ ,即 ${x}^{2} + {2x} - 8 > 0$ ,解得 $x <  - 4$ 或 $x > 2$ , 所以不等式 $\left| {x + 1}\right|  > 3$ 的解集是 $\left( {-\infty , - 4}\right)  \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若不等式 $\left| {x - a}\right|  < 2\left( {a \in  \mathbf{R}}\right)$ 的解集为$(-1, t)$,则实数 $t$ 等于\blkda[4]{3}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        不等式 $\left| {x - a}\right|  < 2$ ,化为 $a - 2 < x < a + 2$ ,因此不等式 $\left| {x - a}\right|  < 2$ 的解集为 $\left( {a - 2,a + 2}\right)$ , 依题意, $\left( {-1,t}\right)  = \left( {a - 2,a + 2}\right)$ ,于是 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a - 2 =  - 1 \\  t = a + 2 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $a = 1,t = 3$ , 所以实数 $t$ 等于 3
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\left| {1 - {4x}}\right|  > {2x}$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left(-\infty, \dfrac{1}{6}\right) \cup \left(\dfrac{1}{2}, +\infty\right)$} 
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left| {1 - {4x}}\right|  > {2x} \Longleftrightarrow 1 - {4x} > {2x}$ 或 $1 - {4x} <  - {2x}$ , 解得 $x < \dfrac{1}{6}$ 或 $x > \dfrac{1}{2}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\left| {{x}^{2} - 4}\right|  < x + 2$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$(1, 3)$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left| {{x}^{2} - 4}\right|  < x + 2 \Longleftrightarrow -(x+2) < x^2-4 < (x+2) \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             (x+2)(x-3) < 0  \\
             (x+2)(x-1) > 0
        \end{array}\right.$，解得$ x \in (1,3)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 $\left| {x + 2}\right|  + \left| {x - 2}\right|  \leq  4$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 $x <  - 2$ 时, $- x - 2 + 2 - x \leq  4$ ,解得 $x \geq   - 2$ ,此时解集为空集
            \item 当 $- 2 \leq  x \leq  2$ 时, $x + 2 + 2 - x \leq  4$ ,即 $4 \leq  4$ ,符合要求,此时解集为 $\left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$
            \item 当 $x > 2$ 时, $x + 2 + x - 2 \leq  4$ ,解得 $x \leq  2$ ,此时解集为空集
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，解集为 $\left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 不等式 $\left| {{2x} + 1}\right|  - \left| {5 - x}\right|  > 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty , - 6}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{4}{3}, + \infty }\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由不等式 $\left| {{2x} + 1}\right|  - \left| {5 - x}\right|  > 0$，可得 ${\left( 2x + 1\right) }^{2} > {\left( 5 - x\right) }^{2}$，即 $\left( {x + 6}\right) \left( {{3x} - 4}\right)  > 0$
        
        解得 $x \in  \left( {-\infty , - 6}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{4}{3}, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则 “ ${x}^{3} > 8$ ” 是 “ $\left| x\right|  > 2$ ” 的\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
    \xx{充分不必要条件}{必要不充分条件}{充要条件}{既不充分也不必要条件}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${x}^{3} > 8 \Longleftrightarrow  x > 2$，$\left| x\right|  > 2 \Longleftrightarrow  x > 2$ 或 $x <  - 2$

        故“ ${x}^{3} > 8$ ” 是 “ $\left| x\right|  > 2$ ” 的充分不必要条件
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则 “ $\left| {x + 1}\right|  + \left| {x - 1}\right|  \leq  2$ ” 是 “ $\dfrac{1}{x} > 1$ ” 的\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
    \xx{充分不必要条件}{必要不充分条件}{充要条件}{既不充分也不必要条件}
    \begin{jieda}
