        \ps 三次函数$f(x) = ax^2+bx^2+cx+d$对称中心坐标为$\left(-\dfrac{b}{3a}, f\left(-\dfrac{b}{3a}\right)\right)$
    
        由题意设对称中心的坐标为(a, b),则有 ${2b} = f\left( {a + x}\right)  + f\left( {a - x}\right)$ 对任意 $x \in  R$ 均成立, 代入函数解析式得，${2b} = {\left( a + x\right) }^{3} - 3{\left( a + x\right) }^{2} + 5\left( {a + x}\right)  - 1 + {\left( a - x\right) }^{3} - 3{\left( a - x\right) }^{2} + 5\left( {a - x}\right)  - 1$
        
        整理得到${2b} = {\left( a + x\right) }^{3} - 3{\left( a + x\right) }^{2} + 5\left( {a + x}\right)  - 1 + {\left( a - x\right) }^{3} - 3{\left( a - x\right) }^{2} + 5\left( {a - x}\right)  - 1,$
        
        整理得到 ${2b} = \left( {{6a} - 6}\right) {x}^{2} + 2{a}^{3} - 6{a}^{2} + {10a} - 2 = 0$ 对任意 $x \in  R$ 均成立,
        
        所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {6a} - 6 = 0 \\  2{a}^{3} - 6{a}^{2} + {10a} - 2 = {2b} \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $a = 1,b = 2$，即对称中心$(1,2)$.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + {mx}$ ,若 $\forall x \in  \mathbf{R},f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = f\left( {1 + x}\right)$ ,则 $m =$ \blkda[4]{$-2$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因 $\forall x \in  \mathbf{R},f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = f\left( {1 + x}\right)$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的对称轴为直线 $x = 1$ ,

        而由 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + {mx} = {\left( x + \dfrac{m}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{{m}^{2}}{4}$ 可知其对称轴为直线 $x =  - \dfrac{m}{2}$ ,故 $- \dfrac{m}{2} = 1$ ,解得 $m =  - 2$ .
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 函数 $y = \dfrac{x - 1}{x - b}$ 的图像关于点(3, c)中心对称,则 $b + c =$ \blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        函数 $y = \dfrac{x - 1}{x - b} = \dfrac{\left( {x - b}\right)  + b - 1}{x - b} = 1 + \dfrac{b - 1}{x - b}$ 的图像关于点(3, c)中心对称，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 3 - b = 0 \\  c = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $b = 3,c = 1$ ,
        
        所以 $b + c = 4$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 奇函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像关于直线 $x = 2$ 对称, $f\left( 3\right)  = 3$ ,则 $f\left( {-1}\right)  + f\left( 0\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$-3$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数是奇函数,所以 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0$
        
        因为函数关于直线 $x = 2$ 对称, $f\left( {4 - x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( 3\right)$ ,
        
        $f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - f\left( 1\right)  =  - f\left( 3\right)  =  - 3$ ,所以 $f\left( {-1}\right)  + f\left( 0\right)  =  - 3$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ . 当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,2}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + 4$ ,则 $f\left( {2024}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{8}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 是以 3 为周期的周期函数,
        
        所以 $f\left( {2024}\right)  = f\left( {{674} \times  3 + 2}\right)  = f\left( 2\right)  = {2}^{2} + 4 = 8$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 是以 2 为最小正周期的周期函数,且当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,2}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2}$ ,则$f\left( 5\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{0}, $f\left( \dfrac{9}{2}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{4}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $f\left( 5\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = {\left( 1 - 1\right) }^{2} = 0,f\left( \dfrac{9}{2}\right)  = f\left( {4 + \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  = f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2} - 1\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( \left| x\right| \right) ,f\left( {x - 1}\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,且当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ ,则 $f\left( \dfrac{3}{2}\right)  =$ \blkda{$\dfrac{1}{4}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意可知,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ ,故函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数,又 $f\left( {x - 1}\right)  = f(x + 1)$ ,即周期为 2
        
        故 $f\left( \dfrac{3}{2}\right)  = f\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  = f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的奇函数,且 $f\left( {2 + x}\right)  = f\left( {-x}\right)$ . 若 $f\left( {-3}\right)  = 3$ ,则 $f\left( 5\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{3}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的奇函数，则 $f\left( {2 + x}\right)  = f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$
        
        所以 $f\left( {4 + x}\right)  =  - f\left( {2 + x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 是周期为 4 的函数,则 $f\left( 5\right)  = f\left( {-3}\right)  = 3$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知偶函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( {x + 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + {2f}\left( 2\right)$ ,则 $f\left( {2022}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{0}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令 $x =  - 2$ ,则 $f\left( 2\right)  = f\left( {-2}\right)  + {2f}\left( 2\right)$ ,又 $f\left( {-2}\right)  = f\left( 2\right)$，所以 $f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ ,于是 $f\left( {x + 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + {2f}\left( 2\right)$ 化为: $f\left( {x + 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$，所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 的周期 $T = 4$
        
         $f\left( {2022}\right)  = f\left( {{505} \times  4 + 2}\right)  = f\left( 2\right)  = 0$
    \end{jieda}

    \item 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的周期为 2,且当 $x \in  \lbrack  - 1,1)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = x$ ,则 $y = f\left( x\right)$，$x \in  \lbrack {2k} - 1,{2k} + 1)\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 的解析式为\blkda[4]{$f\left( x\right)  = x - {2k}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的周期为 2,当 $x \in  \lbrack  - 1,1)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = x$,且 $k \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,当 ${2k} - 1 \leq  x < {2k} + 1$ 时,则 $- 1 \leq  x - {2k} < 1$，故当 $x \in  \lbrack {2k} - 1,{2k} + 1)\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x - {2k}}\right)  = x - {2k}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{3} + {\left( 2x + 1\right) }^{2} + 3}{{x}^{2} + 1}$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值为 $M$ ,最小值为 $N$ ,则 $M + N$ 的值为\blkda[4]{8}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{3} + {\left( 2x + 1\right) }^{2} + 3}{{x}^{2} + 1} = \dfrac{{x}^{3} + 4{x}^{2} + {4x} + 4}{{x}^{2} + 1} = \dfrac{{x}^{3} + {4x}}{{x}^{2} + 1} + 4$ ,
        
        设 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{3} + {4x}}{{x}^{2} + 1},x \in  \left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        则 $g\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{-{x}^{3} - {4x}}{{\left( -x\right) }^{2} + 1} =  - \dfrac{{x}^{3} + {4x}}{{x}^{2} + 1} =  - g\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以函数 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ 上为奇函数,
        
        所以 $g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } + g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = 0$
        
        由题意,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} M = g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } + 4 \\  N = g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } + 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,
        
        所以 $M + N = g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } + g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } + 8 = 8$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 单调递减,且 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 为偶函数,若 $m > 0,n > 0$ , ${2m} \neq  n$ ,且有 $f\left( {2m}\right)  = f\left( n\right)$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{2m}{n}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{1}{2} + \sqrt{2}$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 为偶函数,则 $f\left( {-x + 2}\right)  = f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 的对称轴为 $x = 2$ ,
        
        函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 单调递减,则函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ 单调递增,
        
        若 $m > 0,n > 0,{2m} \neq  n$ ,且有 $f\left( {2m}\right)  = f\left( n\right)$ ,
        
        则 $\dfrac{{2m} + n}{2} = 2$ ,即 ${2m} + n = 4,{2m} = 4 - n$ ,
        
        $\therefore \dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{2m}{n} = \dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{4 - n}{n} = \dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{4}{n} - 1 = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {{2m} + n}\right) \left( {\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{4}{n}}\right)  - 1$
        
        $= \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {{2m} + n}\right) \left( {\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{4}{n}}\right)  - 1 = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {6 + \dfrac{n}{m} + \dfrac{8m}{n}}\right)  - 1$
        
        $\geq  \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {6 + 2\sqrt{\dfrac{n}{m} \cdot  \dfrac{8m}{n}}}\right)  - 1 = \dfrac{1}{2} + \sqrt{2},$
        
        当且仅当 $\dfrac{n}{m} = \dfrac{8m}{n}$ 且 ${2m} + n = 4,m > 0,n > 0$ ,即 $m = 2\left( {\sqrt{2} - 1}\right) ,n = 4\left( {2 - \sqrt{2}}\right)$ 时,等号成立,
        
        故 $\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{2m}{n}$ 的最小值为 $\dfrac{1}{2} + \sqrt{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知奇函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R},f\left( {x + 3}\right)  =  - f\left( {-x}\right)$ ,且 $f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,6}\right\rbrack$ 上的零点个数的最小值为\blkda[4]{9}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  =  - f\left( {-x}\right)$ ,可得 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象关于点 $\left( {\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right)$ 对称,
        
        又 $f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,所以 $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  =  - f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        则 $f\left( x\right)$ 的周期为 3,所以 $f\left( 0\right)  = f\left( 3\right)  = f\left( 6\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        $f\left( 5\right)  = f\left( 2\right)  = 0,f\left( 4\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( {-2}\right)  =  - f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        而 $f\left( {1.5}\right)  = f\left( {-{1.5}}\right)  =  - f\left( {1.5}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( {1.5}\right)  = f\left( {4.5}\right)  = 0$ .
        
        故 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,6}\right\rbrack$ 上的零点个数的最小值为 9 .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,若 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - 2$ 为奇函数,且 $f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = f\left( {3 + x}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( {2023}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - 2$ 为奇函数,则 $f\left( {-x + 1}\right)  - 2 =  - \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - 2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        所以, $f\left( {1 + x}\right)  + f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = 4$ ,
        
        在等式 $f\left( {1 + x}\right)  + f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = 4$ 中,令 $x = 0$ ,可得 ${2f}\left( 1\right)  = 4$ ,解得 $f\left( 1\right)  = 2$ ,
        
        又因为 $f\left( {1 - x}\right)  = f\left( {3 + x}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( {1 + x}\right)  + f\left( {3 + x}\right)  = 4$ 
        
        所以, $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  + f\left( {x + 5}\right)  = 4$
        
        因此 $f\left( {x + 5}\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( {x + 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 为周期函数,且该函数的周期为 4 ,
        
        所以, $f\left( {2023}\right)  = f\left( {4 \times  {505} + 3}\right)  = f\left( 3\right)  = 4 - f\left( 1\right)  = 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的奇函数,且满足 $f\left( {2 + x}\right)  =  - f\left( {2 - x}\right) ,f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( {2023}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{0}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的奇函数,则 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        因为 $f\left( {2 + x}\right)  =  - f\left( {2 - x}\right)  = f\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( {x + 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 为周期函数,且周期为 4,则 $f\left( 4\right)  = f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        在等式 $f\left( {2 + x}\right)  =  - f\left( {2 - x}\right)$ 中,令 $x = 0$ ,可得 $f\left( 2\right)  =  - f\left( 2\right)$ ,所以, $f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        因为 $f\left( 3\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - f\left( 1\right)  =  - 1$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 3\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  = 1 + 0 - 1 + 0 = 0$ ,
        
        因为 ${2023} = 4 \times  {505} + 3$ ,
        
        所以, $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( {2023}\right)  = {505} \times  \left\lbrack  {f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 3\right)  + f\left( 4\right) }\right\rbrack   + f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 3\right)$
        
        $= {505} \times  0 + 1 + 0 - 1 = 0$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 对于 $x \in  R$ 恒有 $f\left( {2 - x}\right)  + f\left( x\right)  = 2$ ,若 $f\left( x\right)$ 与函数 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x - 1}$ 的图像的点交为 $\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) \ldots \left( {{x}_{n},{y}_{n}}\right)$ ,则 $\left( {{x}_{1} + {y}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {{x}_{2} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + \ldots  + \left( {{x}_{n} + {y}_{n}}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{${2n}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 对于 $x \in  R$ 恒有 $f\left( {2 - x}\right)  + f\left( x\right)  = 2$，所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像关于点(1,1)对称;
        
        $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x - 1} = 1 + \dfrac{1}{x - 1}$ 的图像关于点(1,1)对称,
        
        所以当 $\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 和 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的图像的交点时,点 $\left( {2 - {x}_{1},2 - {y}_{1}}\right)$ 也是 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 和 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像的交点.
        
        所以 $\left( {{x}_{1} + {y}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {{x}_{2} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + \ldots  + \left( {{x}_{n} + {y}_{n}}\right)$
        
        $= \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + \cdots  + {x}_{n}}\right)  + \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} + \cdots {y}_{n}}\right)$
        
        $= \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + \cdots  + {x}_{n}}\right)  + \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} + \cdots {y}_{n}}\right)$
        
        $= 2 \times  \dfrac{n}{2} + 2 \times  \dfrac{n}{2} = {2n}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right) ,f\left( {x + 2}\right)  = f\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 时，$f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - {3x}$ ,则 $f\left( {2023}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$-2$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由已知可知 $f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数，且关于$x = 2$对称，因此可以推出周期为4
        
        故 $f\left( {2023}\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = {\left( 1\right) }^{3} - 3 \times  1 =  - 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,且 $f\left( {x + y}\right)  + f\left( {x - y}\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( y\right) ,f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,则$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2024}}f\left( k\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{0}.
    \begin{jieda}
        依题意, $f\left( 1\right)  = 1,f\left( {x + y}\right)  + f\left( {x - y}\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( y\right)$ ,
        
         令 $y = 1$ 得 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  + f\left( {x - 1}\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        $f\left( {x + 3}\right)  = f\left( {x + 2}\right)  - f\left( {x + 1}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right) ,$
        
        
        
        所以 $f\left( {x + 6}\right)  = f\left( {x + 3 + 3}\right)  =  - f\left( {x + 3}\right)  =  - \left( {-f\left( x\right) }\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 是周期为 6 的周期函数.
        
        令 $x = 1,y = 0$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( 1\right) f\left( 0\right) ,f\left( 0\right)  = 2$ ,
        
        $f\left( 2\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  =  - 1,f\left( 3\right)  = f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)  =  - 2,$
        
        $f\left( 4\right)  = f\left( 3\right)  - f\left( 2\right)  =  - 1,f\left( 5\right)  = f\left( 4\right)  - f\left( 3\right)  = 1$ ,
        
        $f\left( 6\right)  = f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  = 2$ ,所以 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 3\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  + f\left( 5\right)  + f\left( 6\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        因为 ${2024} = {337} \times  6 + 2$ ,所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2024}}f\left( k\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 为定义在整数集上的函数, $f\left( 1\right)  = 1,f\left( 2\right)  = 0,f\left( {-1}\right)  < 0$ ,对任意的整数 $x,y$ 均有 $f\left( {x + y}\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( {1 - y}\right)  + f\left( {1 - x}\right) f\left( y\right)$ . 则 $f\left( {55}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$-1$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令 $x = y = 1$ ,则 $f\left( 2\right)  = f\left( 1\right) f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( 0\right) f\left( 1\right)  = {2f}\left( 0\right)  = 0,\therefore f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ;
        
        令 $x = 2,y =  - 1$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  = {f}^{2}\left( 2\right)  + {f}^{2}\left( {-1}\right)  = {f}^{2}\left( {-1}\right)  = 1$ ,又 $f\left( {-1}\right)  < 0,\therefore f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1$ ;
        
        令 $y = 1$ ,则 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( {1 - x}\right) f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( {1 - x}\right) ,\therefore f\left( x\right)$ 关于直线 $x = 1$ 对称;
        
        令 $y =  - x$ ,则 $f\left( 0\right)  = f\left( x\right) f\left( {1 + x}\right)  + f\left( {1 - x}\right) f\left( {-x}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-x}\right) }\right\rbrack  f\left( {1 + x}\right)  = 0$ ,
        
        $\because f\left( {1 + x}\right)  = 0$ 不恒成立, $\therefore f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-x}\right)  = 0$ 恒成立, $\therefore f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数,
        
        $\because f\left( {x + 2}\right)  = f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right) ,\therefore f\left( {x + 4}\right)  =  - f\left( {x + 2}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        $\therefore f\left( x\right)$ 是周期为 4 的周期函数, $\therefore f\left( {55}\right)  = f\left( {4 \times  {14} - 1}\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的偶函数, $f\left( {5.5}\right)  = 2,g\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - 1}\right) f\left( x\right)$ 若 $g\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 是偶函数,则 $g\left( {-{0.5}}\right)  =$\blkda[4]{3}
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $g\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 为偶函数,则 $g\left( x\right)$ 关于 $x = 1$ 对称,即 $g\left( x\right)  = g\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,即 $\left( {x - 1}\right) f\left( x\right)  = (1 -x)f\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {2 - x}\right)  = 0$ ,所以, $f\left( x\right)$ 关于(1,0)对称,又 $f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的偶函数,则$f\left( x\right)  =  - f\left( {2 - x}\right)  =  - f\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,从而 $f\left( {x - 4}\right)  = f\left\lbrack  {\left( {x - 2}\right)  - 2}\right\rbrack   =  - f\left( {x - 2}\right)  =  - \left\lbrack  {-f\left( x\right) }\right\rbrack   = f\left( x\right)$，即 $f\left( {x - 4}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 周期为 4 ,所以 $f\left( {5.5}\right)  = f\left( {1.5}\right)  = f\left( {-{2.5}}\right)  = f\left( {2.5}\right)  = 2$ ,所以 $g\left( {-{0.5}}\right)  = g\left( {2.5}\right)  = {1.5f}\left( {2.5}\right)  = 3$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R},f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 为奇函数, $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 为偶函数,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {kx} + b$ 若 $f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( 3\right)  = 8$ ,则 $f\left( \dfrac{2021}{2}\right)  =$ \blkda{$-4$}
    \begin{jieda}
    $f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，关于$x = 2$轴对称，故其周期为$4$.

    因为$f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，定义域为$\mathbf{R}$，所以$f(1) = 0$，故$k+b = 0$

    因为$f(x)$关于$x = 2$轴对称，故$f(3) = f(1) = 0$，故$f(0) = 8$
    
    因为$f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，$f(2) = -f(0) = -8$，故$2k+b = -8$

    综上，可得$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
         k = -8 \\
         b = 8
    \end{array}\right.$
    
    因为$f(x)$周期为4，所以$f\left(\dfrac{2021}{2}\right) = f\left(\dfrac{5}{2}\right) = f\left(\dfrac{3}{2}\right) = -4$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R},f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 为奇函数, $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 为偶函数,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} + b$ . 若 $f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( 3\right)  = 6$ ,则 $f\left( \dfrac{2025}{2}\right)  =$\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{5}{2}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，关于$x = 2$轴对称，故其周期为$4$.

        因为$f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，定义域为$\mathbf{R}$，所以$f(1) = 0$，故$a+b = 0$

        因为$f(x)$关于$x = 2$轴对称，故$f(3) = f(1) = 0$，故$f(0) = 6$
        
        因为$f(x)$关于$(1, 0)$中心对称，$f(2) = -f(0) = -6$，故$4a+b = -6$

        综上，可得$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             a = -2 \\
             b = 2
        \end{array}\right.$
        
        因为$f(x)$周期为4，所以$f\left(\dfrac{2025}{2}\right) = f\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\right) = -f\left(\dfrac{3}{2}\right) = \dfrac{5}{2}$
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知定义在$\mathbf{R}$上的偶函数$f(x)$满足$f(x) = -f(\pi - x)$，当$x \in \left[0, \dfrac{\pi}{2}\right)$时$f(x) = \sqrt{x}$，则函数$y = f(x)$在$\left[-\dfrac{3\pi}{2}, \dfrac{5\pi}{2}\right]$上的零点个数为\blkda{9}
    \begin{jieda}
        $f(x) = -f(\pi - x)$即$f(x)$关于$\left(\dfrac{\pi}{2}, 0\right)$中心对称，又因为$f(x)$是偶函数，所以$2\pi$是$f(x)$的一个周期. 因为定义域为$\mathbf{R}$，所以$f\left(\dfrac{k\pi}{2}\right) = 0, k \in \mathbf{Z}$；结合图像可知有9个零点.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的偶函数,对于任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $f\left( {x + 6}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + f\left( 3\right)$ 成立,当 ${x}_{1}$ 、 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ ,且 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ 时,都有 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} > 0$ . 给出以下三个命题:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 直线 $x =  - 6$ 是函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 图象的一条对称轴
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {-9, - 6}\right\rbrack$ 上为增函数
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {-9,9}\right\rbrack$ 上恰有五个零点.    
    \end{dingautolist}
    以上命题中, 正确的序号是\blkda[4]{\ding{172}}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令 $x =  - 3$ ,有 $f\left( {-3 + 6}\right)  = f\left( {-3}\right)  + f\left( 3\right)$，即$f(-3) = 0$
        
        又 $f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的偶函数,所以 $f\left( 3\right)  = 0$，则有 $f\left( {x + 6}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 的周期为 6 .
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d29ci7n7aajc738p8c80_29_760_1405_737_185_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 函数为偶函数,则函数关于 $y$ 轴对称,又由函数的周期为 6,则直线 $x =  - 6$ 是函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 图象的一条对称轴
            \item 当 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ ,且 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ 时,都有 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} > 0$ ,则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 [0,3]上为增函数,根据函数的对称性和周期性等性质,所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-9, - 6}\right\rbrack$ 上为减函数
            \item 在 $f\left( {x + 6}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + f\left( 3\right)$ 式中,令 $x = 0$ ,又因为 $f\left( 3\right)  = 0$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ 上为严格增函数,故 $f\left( 0\right)  \neq  0$ ,故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-9,9}\right\rbrack$ 上零点个数一定是偶数个
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 定义域为实数集的偶函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = f\left( {x - 1}\right) ,x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 恒成立,若当 $x \in  [2 , 3]$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = x$ ,给出如下四个结论:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象关于直线 $x =  - 4$ 对称
        \item 对任意实数 $a$ ,关于 $x$ 的方程 $f\left( x\right)  - \left| {x - a}\right|  = 0$ 一定有解
        \item 若存在实数 $a$ ,使得关于 $x$ 的方程 $f\left( x\right)  - \left| {x - a}\right|  = 0$ 有一个根为 2,则此方程所有根之和为$-20$
        \item 若关于 $x$ 的不等式 $f\left( x\right)  - \left| {x - a}\right|  < 0$ 在区间 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上恒成立,则 $a$ 有最大值
    \end{dingautolist}
    其中,所有正确结论的编号是\blkda{\ding{172}\ding{173}}.
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 因为函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = f\left( {x - 1}\right)$ , 所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 为周期为 2 的函数,又因为函数为偶函数, 图象关于 $y$ 轴对称,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 也关于直线 $x =  - 4$ 对称
            \item 方程 $f\left( x\right)  - \left| {x - a}\right|  = 0$ 一定有解,即函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象与函数 $y = \left| {x - a}\right|$ 的图象一定有交点. 在同一个直角坐标系中作出两个函数 $y = \left| x\right|$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象如图所示. 由图象可得,将 $y =  \mid  x \mid$ 左右平移后一定会与函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 相交
            \begin{center}
            \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d29ci7n7aajc738p8c80_30_1077_1575_433_334_0.jpg}
            \end{center}
            \item 根据图象可知若 $x = 2$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = \left| {x - a}\right|$ 的一个交点，则$a = 0$或$4$
            
            当 $a = 0$ 时, $y = \left| x\right|$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象都关于 $y$ 轴对称,所有交点的横坐标之和为 0

            
            当 $a = 4$ 时, $y = \left| x\right|$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象都关于 $x = 4$ 轴对称，每一组关于$x = 4$对称的两根之和为$8$，而有两个函数有无数个交点.
            \item 若关于 $x$ 的不等式 $f\left( x\right)  - \left| {x - a}\right|  < 0$ 在区间 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上恒成立,即 $\left| {x - a}\right|  >$  $f\left( x\right)$ 恒成立,通过图象的平移可知, $a <  - 2$ ,即实数 $a$ 没有最大值
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{D Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{x + 2}{x + 3} + \cdots  + \dfrac{x + {2021}}{x + {2022}}$ 的图像的对称中心为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\dfrac{2023}{2},{2022}}\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{x + {2021}}{x + {2022}}$
        
        $\dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + {2021}}{x + {2022}} = 2 - \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + {2022}}}\right) = 2 - \dfrac{2021}{{x}^{2} + {2023x} + {2022}}$
        
        $\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             f\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{0}{1} + \dfrac{1}{2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{2 \times  {21}}{2022}  \\
             f\left( {-{2023}}\right)  = \dfrac{2023}{2022} + \dfrac{2023}{2021} + \cdots  + \dfrac{2}{1} 
        \end{array}\right.$，有$f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( {-{2023}}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{1} + \dfrac{4}{2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{4044}{2022} = {4044}$
        故 $f\left( x\right)$ 应关于 $\left( {-\dfrac{2023}{2},{2022}}\right)$ 对称
        
        
        检验：$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{x + {2021}}{x + {2022}}  \\
             f\left( {-{2023} - x}\right)  = \dfrac{-{2023} - x}{-{2022} - x} + \dfrac{-{2022} - x}{-{2021} - x} + \cdots  + \dfrac{-2 - x}{-1 - x} \\ \quad \quad \quad \quad  \quad \quad  = \dfrac{x + {2023}}{x + {2022}} + \dfrac{x + {2022}}{x + {2021}} + \cdots  + \dfrac{x + 2}{x + 1}
        \end{array}\right.$
        
        故$f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-{2023} - x}\right)  = \dfrac{2\left( {x + 1}\right) }{x + 1} + \dfrac{2\left( {x + 2}\right) }{x + 2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{2\left( {x + {2022}}\right) }{\left( x + {2022}\right) }$
        $= 2 \times  {2022}$
        $= {4044}$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{x + 2}{x + 3}$ ,给出下列判断:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\mathbf{R}$
        \item $f\left( x\right)$ 在定义域内有三个零点
        \item $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象是中心对称图象
    \end{dingautolist} 其中,正确判断的有\blkda[4]{\ding{172}\ding{173}\ding{174}}
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意可知,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{x + 2}{x + 3} = \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{x + 1}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{x + 2}}\right)  +$  $\left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{x + 3}}\right)  = 3 - \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{1}{x + 3}}\right)$ ,其定义域为 $\{ x \mid  x \neq   - 1,x \neq   - 2,x \neq   - 3\}$ .
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 显然函数在 $x \in  \left( {-2, - 1}\right)$ 内连续且严格单调递增,当 $x \Longrightarrow   - {2}^{ + }$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow$  $- \infty$ ,当 $x \Longrightarrow   - {1}^{ - }$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow   + \infty$ ,可知函数的值域是 $\mathbf{R}$
            \item 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{x + 1} + \dfrac{x + 1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{x + 2}{x + 3} = 3 - \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{1}{x + 3}}\right)$ ,显然函数在 $\left( {-\infty , - 3}\right) ,\left( {-3, - 2}\right) ,\left( {-2, - 1}\right) ,\left( {-1, + \infty }\right)$ 四个区间内连续且严格单调递增，分析各区间端点的极限值,根据零点存在性定理,可知 $f\left( x\right)$ 在定义域内有三个零点
            \item 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = 3 - \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{1}{x + 3}}\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( {-4 - x}\right)  = 3 + \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + 2} + }\right.$
            
            $\left. \dfrac{1}{x + 3}\right)  = 6 - \left\lbrack  {3 - \left( {\dfrac{1}{x + 1} + \dfrac{1}{x + 2} + \dfrac{1}{x + 3}}\right) }\right\rbrack   = 6 - f\left( x\right)$ ,得 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-4 - x}\right)  = 6$ ,即函数的图象关于点$(-2,3)$中心对称

            \ps 对于对称中心可以通过带特殊值或者根据三个权重相同的部分各自的对称中心先猜出对称中心后证明.
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}

\quad

\section*{函数中的恒成立与存在性问题、方程的根与函数的零点}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 对于实数$a, b$定义运算“*”：$a*b = \left\{  
         \begin{array}{ll}  
            a^2 - ab, & a \leq b, \\  
            b^2 - ab, & a > b.
         \end{array}
    \right.$ 设$f(x) = (2x-1) * (x-1)$，且关于$x$的方程$f(x) = m (m \in \mathbf{R})$恰有三个互不相等的实数根$x_1, x_2, x_3$，则$x_1x_2x_3$的取值范围是\blkda[3]{$\left(-\dfrac{\sqrt{3} - 1}{16}, 0\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        根据运算定义规则，首先求$2x-1$和$x-1$的交点，为$(0, -1)$，\\
        故$f(x) = \left\{ 
                 \begin{array}{ll}  
                    (2x-1)^2 - (2x-1)(x-1), & x \leq 0, \\  
                    (x-1)^2 - (2x-1)(x-1), & x > 0.
                 \end{array}
                \right. $ \\
        即$f(x) = \left\{ 
                 \begin{array}{ll}  
                    2x^2-x, & x \leq 0, \\  
                    x-x^2, & x > 0.
                 \end{array}
                \right. $ \\
        观察函数图像可知，在满足题意的情况下，$y = m \left(0 < m < \dfrac{1}{4}\right)$与$f(x)$在$y$轴左侧有一个交点，在$y$轴右侧有两个交点
        
        由韦达定理知$x_2x_3 = m$，$x_1 = \dfrac{1-\sqrt{1+8m}}{4}$，故$x_1x_2x_3 = -\dfrac{1}{4}(m \cdot (\sqrt{1+8m} - 1))$
        
        因为$-\dfrac{1}{4}(m \cdot (\sqrt{1+8m} - 1))$在$m \in \left(0, \dfrac{1}{4}\right)$单调递减，故其取值范围是$\left(-\dfrac{\sqrt{3} - 1}{16}, 0\right)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 用$C(A)$表示非空集合$A$中的元素个数，定义$|A-B| = \left \{ \begin{array}{ll}
        C(A) - C(B), & C(A) \geq C(B) \\
        C(B) - C(A), & C(A) < C(B)
    \end{array}\right.$，若$A = \{1,2\}, B = \{x | |x^2+2x-3| = a\}$，且$|A-B| = 1$，由$a$的所有可能值构成的集合为$S$，那么$C(S) = $\blkda{1}
    \begin{jieda}
        $|x^2+2x-3| = a$即$y = a$与$y = |x^2+2x-3|$的交点横坐标，而$|A-B| = 1$即两者交点数为3或1. 易知两者交点个数为0,2,3,4，故考虑其有三个交点的情况，即$a = 4$，故$S$中只有一个元素.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 用$C(A)$表示非空集合A中元素个数，定义$|A-B| = \left \{ \begin{array}{ll}
         C(A)-C(B),& C(A) \geq C(B)  \\
         C(B)-C(A),& C(A) < C(B)
    \end{array}\right.$，
    已知集合$A = \{x | x^2 + x = 0\}, B = \{x | (3x^2+ax)(x^2+ax+2) = 0\}$，且$|A-B|=1$，设实数$a$的所有可能取值构成集合$S$，则$C(S) = $\blkda{5}
    \begin{jieda}
        $A = \{0, -1\}, C(A) = 2$,因为$|A-B| = 1$，所以$C(B) = 1 \mbox{或} 3$。 \\
        方程$(3x^2+ax)(x^2+ax+2) = 0$的根包括$0, -\dfrac{a}{3}, \dfrac{-a+\sqrt{a^2-8}}{2}, \dfrac{-a-\sqrt{a^2-8}}{2}$ 
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 当$a \in (-2\sqrt{2}, 2\sqrt{2})$时，后两者并非实根，因此$C(B) \neq 3$，当$a = 0$的时候$B = \{0\}, C(B) = 1$
            \item 当$a \in \{-2\sqrt{2}, 2\sqrt{2}\}$时，$B = \{0, -\dfrac{a}{3}, -\dfrac{a}{2}\}, C(B) = 3$
            \item 当$a \in (-\infty, -2\sqrt{2}) \cup (2\sqrt{2}, +\infty)$时，若有$-\dfrac{a}{3} = \dfrac{-a \pm \sqrt{a^2-8}}{2}$可使得$C(B) = 3$，解得$a = \pm 3$
        \end{enumerate}
        综上$S = \{0, 2\sqrt{2}, -2\sqrt{2}, 3, -3\}$，因此$C(S) = 5$
    \end{jieda} 
    
    \item 已知方程$|x| = ax+1$有一负解，且无正解，则实数$a$的取值范围是\blkda{$[1, +\infty)$ }
    \begin{jieda}
        将方程的根转化为$y = |x|$与$y = ax+1$的交点的横坐标.$y = ax+1$过$(0, 1)$，斜率为$a$，故当$a \in [1, +\infty)$时，两函数在$x > 0$时无交点，而在$x < 0$时有一个交点，满足要求.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 设$f(x)$是定义在$\bR$上的奇函数, 且对于任意的$x\zr$, $f(1+x)-f(1-x)=0$恒成立; 当$x\in[0,1]$时, $f(x)=2x$. 
    若方程$f(x)=ax$恰好有5个不同的解, 则实数$a$的取值范围为\blkda[2.5]{$\left(-\dfrac{2}{3},-\dfrac{2}{7}\right)\cup\left\{\dfrac{2}{5}\right\}$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
    $f(x)$图像关于原点对称, 关于直线$x=1$对称, 可知周期$T=4$. \\
    $y = ax$的图像是过原点的直线，随着$a$的变化，其斜率变化. 绘制草图后可以判断要分为$a>0$与$a<0$两种情形, 
    不难求得$a=\dfrac{2}{5}$或$a\in\left(-\dfrac{2}{3},-\dfrac{2}{7}\right)$. 
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知关于$x$的方程$(x^2-1)^2-|x^2-1|+k = 0$. 下列语句中说法正确的有\blkda{\ding{172} \ding{173} \ding{174} \ding{175}}
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item 存在实数$k$使得方程恰有2个不同的实数解.
        \item 存在实数$k$使得方程恰有4个不同的实数解.
        \item 存在实数$k$使得方程恰有5个不同的实数解.
        \item 存在实数$k$使得方程恰有8个不同的实数解.
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        换元$t = |x^2-1|$，参变分离有$k = t - t^2$
        
        对于内层方程$t = |x^2-1|$：
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 若$t < 0$，则方程无解
            \item 若$t = 0$，则方程有2个解
            \item 若$t \in (0, 1)$，则方程有4个解
            \item 若$t = 1$，则方程有3个解
            \item 若$t > 1$，则方程有2个解
        \end{dingautolist}
        对于外层方程$k = t - t^2$：
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 若$k > \dfrac{1}{4}$，则外层方程无解，复合方程无解
            \item 若$k = \dfrac{1}{4}$，则存在唯一的$t = \dfrac{1}{2}$使外层方程成立，对复合方程本身有4个解
            \item 若$0 < k < \dfrac{1}{4}$，则外层方程两解$t_1, t_2 \in (0, 1)$，复合方程有8个解
            \item 若$k = 0$，则外层方程两解$t_1 = 0, t_2 = 1$，复合方程有5个解
            \item 若$k < 0$，则外层方程两解$t_1 < 0, t_2 > 1$，复合方程有2个解
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数$f(x) = x - 2$，$g(x) = mx^2-2mx+1(m \in \mathbf{R})$. 
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 对任意的$x \in \mathbf{R}$，不等式$g(x) > f(x)$恒成立，求$m$取值范围.
        \item 对任意的$x_1 \in [1, 2], x_2 \in [-1, 0]$，不等式$g(x_1) > f(x_2)$恒成立，求$m$取值范围.
        \item 对任意的$x_1 \in [1, 2]$，存在$x_2 \in [3, 4]$，使得$g(x_1) = f(x_2)$，求$m$取值范围.
        \item 对任意的$x_1 \in [1, 2]$，存在$x_2 \in [0, 2]$，使得$g(x_1) > f(x_2)$，求$m$取值范围.
    \end{enumerate}
    
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 问题转化为$g(x) - f(x) > 0$在$\mathbf{R}$上恒成立，即$mx^2-(2m+1)x+3 > 0$恒成立. $m = 0$不满足要求，$m < 0$不满足要求，$m > 0$时应有$\Delta < 0$，即$m \in \left(\dfrac{2-\sqrt{3}}{2}, \dfrac{2+\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)$
            \item 问题转化为$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$上的最小值大于$f(x)$在$[3, 4]$上的最大值. $f(x)$在$[-1, 0]$上的最大值为$-2$，故问题转化为$g(x) + 2 > 0$在$[1, 2]$上恒成立，设$h(x) = g(x) + 2 = mx^2-2mx + 3 = m(x-1)^2-(m-3)$，下面分析$h(x)$的最值
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item $m > 0$，$h(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递增，因此最小值为$h(1) = 3-m$
                \item $m = 0$，$h(x) = 3$，最小值为$3$
                \item $m < 0$，$h(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递减，因此最小值为$h(2) = 3$
            \end{dingautolist}
            故当$m \in (-\infty, 3)$时，满足要求.
            \item 问题转化为$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$上的值域是$f(x)$在$[3, 4]$上值域的子集. $f(x)$在$[3, 4]$上值域为$[1, 2]$，$g(x) = m(x-1)^2 + (1-m)$，下面分析$g(x)$的值域
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item $m > 0$，$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递增，值域为$[1-m, 1]$
                \item $m = 0$，$g(x) = 1$，值域为$\{1\}$
                \item $m < 0$，$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递增，值域为$[1, 1-m]$
            \end{dingautolist}
            故当$m \in [-1, 0]$时满足要求.
            \item 问题转化为$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$上的最小值大于$f(x)$在$[0, 2]$上的最小值. $f(x)$在$[0, 2]$上最小值为-2，下面分析$g(x)$的最值
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item $m > 0$，$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递增，因此最小值为$g(1) = 1-m$
                \item $m = 0$，$g(x) = -1$，最小值为$-1$
                \item $m < 0$，$g(x)$在$[1, 2]$内单调递减，因此最小值为$g(2) = 1$
            \end{dingautolist}
            故当$m \in (-\infty, 3)$时，满足要求.
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = x,g\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} - x$ ,其中 $a > 0$ ,若对任意的 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack$ ,总存在 ${x}_{2} \in$  $\left\lbrack  {1,4}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right) f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{1}\right) g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 成立,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{5}{4},\dfrac{4}{3}}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $x \in [1, 4], f(x) \neq 0$欲满足目标式必有$x \in [1, 3], g(x) \neq 0$
    
        设$h(x) = \dfrac{g(x)}{f(x)} = ax-1$，则$f\left( {x}_{1}\right) f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{1}\right) g\left( {x}_{2}\right) \Longleftrightarrow \dfrac{1}{h(x_1)} = h(x_2)$

        设$m(x) = \dfrac{1}{h(x)}$，则问题转化为“对任意的 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack$ ,总存在 ${x}_{2} \in$  $\left\lbrack  {1,4}\right\rbrack$ ,使得$m(x_1) = h(x_2)$”，即$m(x)$在$[1,3]$的值域是$h(x)$在$[1,4]$的值域的子集.

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $a > 1$ \\
            $\left[\dfrac{1}{3a-1}, \dfrac{1}{a-1}\right] \subseteq \left[a-1, 4a-1\right]$，故有$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             \dfrac{1}{3a-1} \geq a - 1 \\
             \dfrac{1}{a-1} \leq 4a-1
            \end{array}\right.$，解得$\dfrac{5}{4} \leq  a \leq  \dfrac{4}{3}$
            \item $a \in \left(\dfrac{1}{3}, 1\right)$\\
            $\left(-\infty, \dfrac{1}{a-1}\right] \cup \left[\dfrac{1}{3a-1}, +\infty\right) \subseteq \left[a-1, 4a-1\right]$，无解
            \item $a\in \left(0, \dfrac{1}{3}\right)$\\
            $\left[\dfrac{1}{3a-1}, \dfrac{1}{a-1}\right] \subseteq \left[a-1, 4a-1\right]$，故有$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
             \dfrac{1}{3a-1} \geq a - 1 \\
             \dfrac{1}{a-1} \leq 4a-1
            \end{array}\right.$，无解
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$a \in \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{5}{4},\dfrac{4}{3}}\right\rbrack$
        
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\clearpage
\subsection*{D Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知$f(x) = x^2 + \dfrac{1}{x^2} + k \left(x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right)+11, x \in (0, +\infty)$，若$f(x) = 0$有两个解，则实数$k$的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left(-\infty, -\dfrac{13}{2}\right) \cup \{-6\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        首先考虑换元$t = x + \dfrac{1}{x}$，$f(x) = g(t) = t^2+k\cdot t + 9$，当$t = 2$时，只对应一个$x$，当$t > 2$时，对应着两个$x$. 故若$f(x)$在$(0, +\infty)$，有两个零点可以转化为$g(t)$在$(2, +\infty)$有一个零点，进一步转化为$y = -k$与$y = t + \dfrac{9}{t}$在$(2, +\infty)$有一个交点. 因此$k \in \left(-\infty, -\dfrac{13}{2}\right) \cup \{-6\}$ \\
        \ps \textbf{换元后及时判断新元的取值范围，并且分析清楚换元前后的对应关系！}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若关于$x$的方程$\dfrac{|x|}{x+6} = kx^2$有四个不同的实数解，求实数$k$的取值范围.
    \begin{jieda}
        易知必有一根为0 \\
        参变分离有$\dfrac{1}{k} = \dfrac{(x+6)x^2}{|x|} = \left \{ \begin{array}{ll}
            \dfrac{(x+6)x^2}{x}, & x > 0 \\
            -\dfrac{(x+6)x^2}{x},  & x < 0
        \end{array}\right.$
        由于原方程必有一根为0，故可以转化为方程$\dfrac{1}{k} = \left \{ \begin{array}{ll}
            (x+6)x, & x > 0 \\
            -(x+6)x,  & x < 0
        \end{array}\right.$有三个不等实根.因此$\dfrac{1}{k} \in (0, 9)$，即$k \in \left(\dfrac{1}{9}, +\infty\right)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数$f(x) = \left\{  
         \begin{array}{ll}  
            x^3, & x \geq 0, \\  
            -x, & x < 0.
         \end{array}
    \right.$ 若函数$g(x) = f(x) - |kx^2-2x| (k \in \mathbf{R})$恰有4零点，$k$的取值范围是\blkda[3.5]{$(-\infty, 0) \cup (2\sqrt{2}, +\infty)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        容易判断，$g(x)$有一零点为0. 设$F(x) = \left\{  
         \begin{array}{ll}  
            x^2, & x \geq 0, \\  
            1, & x < 0.
         \end{array}
        \right.$，$H(x) = |kx-2|$，故其余三个零点是函数$G(x) = F(x) - H(x)$的零点.易知$k = 0$时$G(x)$没有三个零点. 
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            
            \item 当$k < 0$时，$G(x)$在$\left(-\infty, \dfrac{2}{k}\right)$单调增，在$\left(\dfrac{2}{k}, 0\right)$单调减，在$\left(0, -\dfrac{k}{2}\right)$单调减，在$\left(-\dfrac{k}{2}, +\infty\right)$单调增.$G\left(\dfrac{3}{k}\right) = 0, G\left(\dfrac{1}{k}\right) = 0, G(0) = -2, G(2-k) = 2-2k$，故$G(x)$有三个零点，分别是$\dfrac{3}{k}, \dfrac{1}{k}$，以及一个位于$(0, 2-k)$的零点.
            \item 当$k > 0$时，当$x < 0$，$G(x) = kx-1$，无零点. \\
            当$x \in \left(0, \dfrac{2}{k}\right)$，$G(x)$单调增,$G\left(\dfrac{1}{k}\right) = 0$. \\
            当$x \geq \dfrac{2}{k}$，$G(x) = x^2 - kx + 2$，要使得$G(x)$在$\left[\dfrac{2}{k}, +\infty\right)$有两个零点，必有$\Delta > 0$，即$k > 2\sqrt{2}$. 抛物线的对称轴为$x = \dfrac{k}{2}$，因为当$k > 2\sqrt{2}$时$\dfrac{k}{2} > \dfrac{2}{k}$，又因为$G\left(\dfrac{2}{k}\right) > 0$，所以当$k > 2\sqrt{2}$时，$G(x)$在$\left[\dfrac{2}{k}, +\infty\right)$有两个零点.
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上，$k \in (-\infty, 0) \cup (2\sqrt{2}, +\infty)$ \\
        \ps 从数形结合角变形$H(x)$来看会更直观.
\end{jieda}
    \item 已知二次函数 $f(x)=-\dfrac{1}{2} x^2+x$，是否存在实数 $m, n(m<n)$, 使得 $f(x)$ 的定义域为 $[m, n]$, 值域为 $[k m, k n](k>1)$.
    \begin{jieda}
    $f(x)=-\dfrac{1}{2} x^2+x=-\dfrac{1}{2} \times(x-1)^2+\dfrac{1}{2} \leq \dfrac{1}{2}$
    $\therefore k n \leq \dfrac{1}{2}, \therefore m<n<\dfrac{1}{2}$
    又 $\because$ 函数的对称轴为 $x=1$
    $\therefore f(x)$ 在 $[m, n]$ 单调递增则有 $f(m)=k m, f(n)=k n$
    故$m, n$是$-\dfrac{1}{2}x^2 + x = kx$的两根，故$m = 2(1-k), n = 0$ \\
    \ps 根据值域判断函数在定义域内的单调性是简化，解决问题的突破口。\\
    常规解决方法是分析单调性，根据最值列方程组.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若函数$f(x)$在区间$[a, b]$上的值域恰是$\left[\dfrac{1}{b}, \dfrac{1}{a}\right]$，则称区间$[a, b]$是$f(x)$的一个倒域区间. 求函数$g(x) = \left \{ \begin{array}{ll}
        -x^2+2x & 0 \leq x \leq 2 \\
        x^2 + 2x & -2 \leq x < 0
    \end{array}\right.$的所有倒域区间.
    \begin{jieda}
        $g(x)$的定义域为$[-2, 2]$，$g(x)$值域为$[-1, 1]$，故有$-2 \leq a < b \leq 2, -1 \leq \dfrac{1}{b} < \dfrac{1}{a} \leq 1$，解得$1 \leq a < b \leq 2$或$-2 \leq a < b \leq -1$.
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item  $1 \leq a < b \leq 2$，此时$g(x)$单调递减，有$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                 -a^2 + 2a = \dfrac{1}{a}  \\
                 -b^2 + 2b = \dfrac{1}{b} 
            \end{array} \right.$，即$a, b$是$-x^2 + 2x = \dfrac{1}{x}$的两根，即$a, b$是$-x^3 + 2x^2 -1 = 0$的两根，可以看出有一根为$1$，即$a, b$是$(x-1)(-x^2+x+1) = 0$的两根，故$a = 1, b = \dfrac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}$
            \item $-2 \leq a < b \leq -1$，此种情况和前一种对称，可得$a = -\dfrac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}, b = -1$
        \end{dingautolist}
        因此两个倒域区间为$\left[1, \dfrac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}\right]$和$\left[-\dfrac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}, -1\right]$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若函数$f(x)$在区间$[m, n]$上的值域恰是$[m, n]$，则称区间$[m, n]$是$f(x)$的一个等域区间. 若区间$[m,n]$是函数$y = \dfrac{(a+1)x - \dfrac{1}{a}}{ax}(a \neq 0)$的一个等域区间.求$n-m$的最大值.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\dfrac{(a+1)x - \dfrac{1}{a}}{ax} = \dfrac{a+1}{a} - \dfrac{1}{a^2x}$，故函数为$y = \dfrac{a+1}{a} - \dfrac{1}{a^2x} (a \neq 0)$，易知函数的定义域为$(-\infty, 0) \cup (0, +\infty)$，故必有$m < n < 0$或$0 < m < n$. 故易知函数在$[m, n]$内严格单调递增，故$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                \dfrac{a+1}{a} - \dfrac{1}{a^2m} = m \\
                \dfrac{a+1}{a} - \dfrac{1}{a^2n} = n
            \end{array} \right.$，即$m, n$是$a^2x^2 - (a+1)ax + 1 = 0$的两根. 由韦达定理有$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                m+n = \dfrac{a+1}{a}\\
                m \cdot n = \dfrac{1}{a^2}
            \end{array} \right.$，由$\Delta > 0$得$((a+1)^2 - 4)a^2 > 0$，故$a \in (-\infty, -3]\cup [1, +\infty)$ \\
            $n - m = \sqrt{(m+n)^2 - 4mn} = \sqrt{(\dfrac{a+1}{a})^2 - \dfrac{4}{a^2}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{a^2+2a-3}{a^2}} \leq \dfrac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}$，当$a = 3$时取等号.
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}

\section*{指数运算、对数运算}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 把下列指数式化为对数式,把对数式化为指数式 $\left( {a > 0\text{ 且 }a \neq  1}\right)$：
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item ${a}^{1} = a$
        \item ${a}^{0} = 1$
        \item ${\log }_{{a}}N = b$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\sqrt[3]{{a}^{2}} = \dfrac{2}{3}$
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item ${\log }_{a}a = 1$
            \item ${\log }_{a}1 = 0$
            \item ${a}^{b} = N$
            \item ${a}^{\frac{2}{3}} = \sqrt[3]{{a}^{2}}$
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 求下列各式的值:
    \begin{enumerate*}[i.]
        \item ${2}^{{\log }_{2}8}$
        \item ${3}^{{\log }_{3}9}$
        \item ${10}^{\lg 5}$
        \item ${e}^{\ln 7}$
        \item ${\log }_{6}({6}^{2})$
        \item $\lg {10}^{-5}$
        \item $\lg {10}^{6}$
        \item $\ln {e}^{3}$
    \end{enumerate*}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate*}[i.]
            \item 8
            \item 9
            \item 5
            \item 7
            \item 2
            \item $-5$
            \item 6
            \item 3
        \end{enumerate*}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 求下列各式的值:
    \begin{enumerate*}[i.]
        \item ${\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{16}$
        \item ${\log }_{\frac{1}{2}}{32}$
        \item ${\log }_{0.4}1$
        \item ${\log }_{2.5}{6.25}$
        \item ${4}^{{\log }_{4}3}$
        \item ${9}^{{\log }_{3}7 + 1}$
    \end{enumerate*}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate*}[i.]
            \item $-4$
            \item $-5$
            \item 0
            \item 2
            \item 3
            \item 441
        \end{enumerate*}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $\lg a < 1$ ,化简： $\sqrt{{\left( \lg a\right) }^{2} - \lg \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{10}} = $\blkda[4]{$1 - \lg a$} .
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $\lg \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{10} = \lg {a}^{2} - \lg {10} = 2\lg a - 1$ ,且 $\lg a < 1$ ,
        
        于是 $\sqrt{{\left( \lg a\right) }^{2} - \lg \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{10}} = \sqrt{{\left( \lg a\right) }^{2} - 2\lg a + 1} = \sqrt{{\left( \lg a - 1\right) }^{2}} = \left| {\lg a - 1}\right|  = 1 - \lg a$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 求值: ${7}^{1 + {\log }_{7}5} = $\blkda[4]{35}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${7}^{1 + {\log }_{7}5} = 7 \cdot  {7}^{{\log }_{7}5} = 7 \times  5 = {35}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 计算下列各式的值.
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item ${\log }_{2}{10} \times  \lg 4 =$ \blkda[4]{2}
        \item ${\log }_{2}3 \times  {\log }_{\sqrt{3}}8 =$ \blkda[4]{6}
        \item ${\log }_{3}4 \times  {\log }_{4}5 \times  {\log }_{5}6 \times  {\log }_{6}7 \times  {\log }_{7}8 \times  {\log }_{8}9 =$ \blkda[4]{2}
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item ${\log }_{2}{10} \times  \lg 4 = \dfrac{1}{\lg 2} \times  2\lg 2 = 2$
            \item ${\log }_{2}3 \times  {\log }_{\sqrt{3}}8 = \dfrac{\lg 3}{\lg 2} \times  \dfrac{\lg {2}^{3}}{\lg {3}^{\frac{1}{2}}} = \dfrac{\lg 3}{\lg 2} \times  \dfrac{3\lg 2}{\dfrac{1}{2} \times  \lg 3} = 6$
            \item ${\log }_{3}4 \times  {\log }_{4}5 \times  {\log }_{5}6 \times  {\log }_{6}7 \times  {\log }_{7}8 \times  {\log }_{8}9 = \dfrac{\lg 4}{\lg 3} \times  \dfrac{\lg 5}{\lg 4} \times  \dfrac{\lg 6}{\lg 5} \times  \dfrac{\lg 7}{\lg 6} \times  \dfrac{\lg 8}{\lg 7} \times  \dfrac{\lg 9}{\lg 8} = \dfrac{\lg 9}{\lg 3} = \dfrac{\lg {3}^{2}}{\lg 3} = \dfrac{\lg {3}^{2}}{\lg 3} = 2$
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
    \item $\left( {{\log }_{3}2 + {\log }_{9}2}\right) \left( {{\log }_{4}3 + {\log }_{8}3}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$\dfrac{5}{4}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\left( {{\log }_{3}2 + {\log }_{9}2}\right) \left( {{\log }_{4}3 + {\log }_{8}3}\right)  = \left( {\dfrac{\lg 2}{\lg 3} + \dfrac{\lg 2}{\lg 9}}\right) \left( {\dfrac{\lg 3}{\lg 4} + \dfrac{\lg 3}{\lg 8}}\right)  \\
        = \left( {\dfrac{\lg 2}{\lg 3} + \dfrac{\lg 2}{2\lg 3}}\right) \left( {\dfrac{\lg 3}{2\lg 2} + \dfrac{\lg 3}{3\lg 2}}\right) = \dfrac{3\lg 2}{2\lg 3} \cdot  \dfrac{5\lg 3}{6\lg 2} = \dfrac{5}{4}$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 计算： $\left( {{\log }_{2}5 + {\log }_{4}{125}}\right) \left( {{\log }_{5}4 + {\log }_{25}{64}}\right) = $\blkda[4]{$\dfrac{25}{2}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        原式 $= \left( {{\log }_{2}5 + {\log }_{{2}^{2}}{5}^{3}}\right) \left( {{\log }_{5}{2}^{2} + {\log }_{5^2}{2}^{6}}\right)$

        $= \left( {{\log }_{2}5 + \dfrac{3}{2}{\log }_{2}5}\right) \left( {2{\log }_{5}2 + 3{\log }_{5}2}\right)$
        
        $= \dfrac{5}{2}{\log }_{2}5 \times  5{\log }_{5}2 = \dfrac{25}{2}{\log }_{2}5 \times  {\log }_{5}2 = \dfrac{25}{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 化简: ${\log }_{2}\sqrt{\dfrac{7}{48}} + {\log }_{2}{12} - \dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{2}{42} - 1$ .
    \begin{jieda}
        原式 $= {\log }_{2}\dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{\sqrt{48}} + {\log }_{2}{12} - {\log }_{2}\sqrt{42} - {\log }_{2}2$
        
        $= {\log }_{2}\dfrac{\sqrt{7} \times  {12}}{\sqrt{48} \times  \sqrt{42} \times  2}$
        
        $= {\log }_{2}\dfrac{\sqrt{7} \times  {12}}{4\sqrt{3} \times  \sqrt{7} \times  \sqrt{6} \times  2}$
        
        $= {\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}} = {\log }_{2}{2}^{-\frac{3}{2}} =  - \dfrac{3}{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 计算: ${9}^{\frac{1}{2}{\log }_{3}4} + {5}^{1 + {\log }_{5}2} + {\log }_{3}\sqrt{3} + {{e}}^{\ln 3}$ .
    \begin{jieda}
        ${9}^{\frac{1}{2}{\log }_{3}4} + {5}^{1 + {\log }_{5}2} + {\log }_{3}\sqrt{3} + {{e}}^{\ln 3} = {3}^{{\log }_{3}4} + {5}^{{\log }_{5}\left( {2 \times  5}\right) } + \dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{3}3 + 3 = 4 + {10} + \dfrac{1}{2} + 3 = {17.5}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 设 ${2}^{x} = {3}^{y} = {72}$ ,则 $\dfrac{3}{x} + \dfrac{2}{y} =$ \blkda[4]{1}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 ${2}^{x} = {3}^{y} = {72}$ ,可得 $x = {\log }_{2}{72},y = {\log }_{3}{72}$ ,
        
        所以 $\dfrac{3}{x} + \dfrac{2}{y} = 3{\log }_{72}2 + 2{\log }_{72}3 = {\log }_{72}9 + {\log }_{72}8 = {\log }_{72}{72} = 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $\lg a = {2.31},\lg b = {1.31}$ ,则 $\dfrac{a}{b} =$ \blkda[4]{10}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $\lg a = {2.31},\lg b = {1.31}$，所以 $\lg a - \lg b = \lg \dfrac{a}{b} = 1$，所以 $\dfrac{a}{b} = {10}$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    
    \item 已知 ${\log }_{8}9 = a,{\log }_{3}5 = b$ ,试用 $a,b$ 表示 $\lg 5$ .
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{8}9 = \dfrac{{\log }_{3}9}{{\log }_{3}8} = \dfrac{2}{3{\log }_{3}2} = a,\therefore {\log }_{3}2 = \dfrac{2}{3a}$
        
        $\lg 5 = \dfrac{{\log }_{3}5}{{\log }_{3}{10}} = \dfrac{{\log }_{3}5}{{\log }_{3}5 + {\log }_{3}2} = \dfrac{b}{b + \dfrac{2}{3a}} = \dfrac{3ab}{2 + {3ab}}.$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若$\log_427=m$, $\log_325=n$, 则$\lg2$可用$m,n$表示为\blkda{$\dfrac{3}{3+mn}$}. 
        \begin{jieda}
            $m = \dfrac{3log_23}{2}$，$n = \dfrac{2log_25}{log_23}$，$lg2 = \dfrac{1}{1+log_25}$，故$log_25 = \dfrac{m\cdot n}{2}$，$lg2 = \dfrac{3}{3+mn}$ \\
            \ps 对数运算表示类的题目，首先要统一底数，选取题干中的一个质因子作为底数，然后将所有的对数式都拆彻底（底数和真数互质），然后再凑.
        \end{jieda}
    \item 若 ${\log }_{5}x + {\log }_{5}y = 2{\log }_{5}\left( {x - {2y}}\right)$ ,则 $\dfrac{x}{y} =$ \blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 ${\log }_{5}x + {\log }_{5}y = 2{\log }_{5}\left( {x - {2y}}\right)$ ,故 ${\log }_{5}{xy} = {\log }_{5}{\left( x - 2y\right) }^{2}$ ,
        
        所以 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} x > 0,y > 0 \\  x - {2y} > 0 \\  {xy} = {\left( x - 2y\right) }^{2} \end{matrix}\right.$ ,由 ${xy} = {\left( x - 2y\right) }^{2}$ 得 ${x}^{2} - {5xy} + 4{y}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  x = y$ 或 $x = {4y}$ ,
        
        又 $x > 0,y > 0,x - {2y} > 0$ ,所以舍去 $x = y$ ,故 $x = {4y}$ ,则 $\dfrac{x}{y} = 4$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若 $a > 0$ 且 $a \neq  1,b > 0,c > 0$ ,给出以下式子:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item ${\log }_{a}\dfrac{b}{c} = \dfrac{{\log }_{a}b}{{\log }_{a}c}$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\left( {b \cdot  c}\right)  = {\log }_{a}\left( {b + c}\right)$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\left( {b \cdot  c}\right)  = {\log }_{a}b + {\log }_{a}c$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\left( {b - c}\right)  = \dfrac{{\log }_{a}b}{{\log }_{a}c}$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\left( {b + c}\right)  = {\log }_{a}b \cdot  {\log }_{a}c$
        \item ${\log }_{a}\dfrac{b}{c} = {\log }_{a}b - {\log }_{a}c$
    \end{dingautolist}
    则正确的式子为\blkda[4]{\ding{174}\ding{177}}(填序号). 
    \item 通常我们以分贝(dB)为单位来表示声音大小的等级,如果强度为 $v$ 的声音对应的分贝数为 $f\left( v\right) {{dB}}$ ,那么满足: $f\left( v\right)  = {10} \times  \lg \dfrac{v}{1 \times  {10}^{-{12}}}$ ,若在地铁中多人外放电子设备加上行车噪音,车厢内的声音的分贝能达到 ${90}{{dB}}$ ,则 ${90}{{dB}}$ 的声音与 ${50}{{dB}}$ 的声音强度之比为\blkda[4]{${10000}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $f\left( v\right)  = {90}$ 时,则有 ${10} \times  \lg \dfrac{v}{1 \times  {10}^{-{12}}} = {90}$ ,解得 ${v}_{1} = {10}^{-3}$ ,
        
        当 $f\left( v\right)  = {50}$ 时,则有 ${10} \times  \lg \dfrac{v}{1 \times  {10}^{-{12}}} = {50}$ ,解得 ${v}_{2} = {10}^{-7}$ ,
        
        所以当 $\dfrac{{v}_{1}}{{v}_{2}} = \dfrac{{10}^{-3}}{{10}^{-7}} = {10}^{4} = {10000}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item \begin{enumerate}
        \item 设 ${3}^{x} = {4}^{y} = {36}$ ,求 $\dfrac{2}{x} + \dfrac{1}{y}$ 的值
        \item 若 $x{\log }_{2}3 = 1$ ,求 ${3}^{x} + {9}^{-x}$ 的值
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 由已知得 $x = {\log }_{3}{36},y = {\log }_{4}{36}$ ,

            所以 $\dfrac{2}{x} + \dfrac{1}{y} = \dfrac{2}{{\log }_{3}{36}} + \dfrac{1}{{\log }_{4}{36}} = 2{\log }_{36}3 + {\log }_{36}4 = {\log }_{36}9 + {\log }_{36}4 = {\log }_{36}{36} = 1$
            \item 因为 $x{\log }_{2}3 = 1$ ,所以 ${\log }_{2}{3}^{x} = 1$ ,所以 ${3}^{x} = 2$ ,

            所以 ${3}^{x} + {9}^{-x} = {3}^{x} + {\left( {3}^{x}\right) }^{-2} = 2 + \dfrac{1}{4} = \dfrac{9}{4}$ .
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda} 
    
    \item 方程 $\dfrac{50}{{10}^{x} + 1} - 5 = {10}^{x + 1}$ 的实数解为\blkda[4]{$\lg \dfrac{3}{2}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令 ${10}^{x} = t$ ,则 $\dfrac{50}{t + 1} - 5 = {10t}$ ,整理得 $2{t}^{2} + {3t} - 9 = \left( {t + 3}\right) \left( {{2t} - 3}\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $t =  - 3$ (舍去) 或 $t = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,所以 ${10}^{x} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,故 $x = \lg \dfrac{3}{2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 方程 ${5}^{{5x} + 1} = {3}^{x - 1}$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left\{\dfrac{lg3+lg5}{lg3-5lg5}\right\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        两侧同时以$5$取对数有$5x+1 = (x-1)log_53$，整理可得$(5-log_53)x = -(1+log_53)$，故$x = \dfrac{1+log_53}{log_53-5} = \dfrac{lg3+lg5}{lg3-5lg5}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 方程 ${2}^{2 + x} - {2}^{2 - x} = {15}$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left\{2\right\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        设$t = 2^x, t > 0$故$4t-\dfrac{4}{t} = 15$，解得$t = 4$或$-\dfrac{1}{4}$（舍去），故$x = 2$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 方程 ${2}^{\left| x + 1\right| } = 3$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\left\{  {{\log }_{2}\dfrac{3}{2}, - {\log }_26}\right\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${2}^{\left| x + 1\right| } = 3 \Longleftrightarrow |x+1| = log_23 \Longleftrightarrow x+1 = \pm log_23$，故解集为$\left\{  {{\log }_{2}\dfrac{3}{2}, - {\log }_26}\right\}$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 ${\log }_{3}\left( {{\log }_{4}x}\right)  = 1$ ,则 $x =$ \blkda[4]{64}.
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{3}\left( {{\log }_{4}x}\right)  = 1$ ,则 ${\log }_{4}x = 3$ ,故 $x = {64}$ .
    \end{jieda} 

    \item 方程 ${\log }_{7}\left\lbrack  {{\log }_{2}\left( {{\log }_{x}{16}}\right) }\right\rbrack   = 0$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\{4\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{7}\left\lbrack  {{\log }_{2}\left( {{\log }_{x}{16}}\right) }\right\rbrack   = 0 \Longleftrightarrow {{\log }_{2}\left( {{\log }_{x}{16}}\right) } = 1 \Longleftrightarrow log_x16 = 2 \Longleftrightarrow x = 4$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 ${\log }_{{x}^{2} - 1}\left( {2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 1}\right)  = 1$，则$x = $\blkda[4]{2}
    \begin{jieda}
    
        ${\log }_{{x}^{2} - 1}\left( {2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 1}\right)  = 1 \Longleftrightarrow  \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             {x}^{2} - 1 = 2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 1  \\
             x^2-1 \in (0, 1) \cup (1, +\infty) \\
             2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 1 > 0
        \end{array}\right . \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             {x}^{2} - {3x} + 2 = 0  \\
             x^2 \in (1, 2) \cup (2, +\infty) \\
             2{x}^{2} - {3x} + 1 > 0
        \end{array}\right .$ ,

        解得: $x = 1$ (舍去) 或 $x = 2$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 方程 ${\log }_{4}\left( {{3x} - 1}\right)  = {\log }_{4}\left( {x - 1}\right)  + {\log }_{4}\left( {3 + x}\right)$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\{2\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{4}\left( {{3x} - 1}\right)  = {\log }_{4}\left( {x - 1}\right)  + {\log }_{4}\left( {3 + x}\right) \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             x > 1  \\
             x > -3 \\
             3x-1 = (x-1)(3+x)
        \end{array}\right.$

        解得: $x = -1$ (舍去) 或 $x = 2$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 方程 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 2 - {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 的解集是\blkda[4]{$\{\sqrt{5}\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 2 - {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 1}\right) \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
             x > -1  \\
             x > 1 \\
             x-1 = \dfrac{4}{x+1}
        \end{array}\right.$，解得$x = \sqrt{5}$（负舍）
    \end{jieda}
    \item ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + 4}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 1 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 8}\right)$ 的解是\blkda[4]{$x = 4$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $x + 4 > 0,x - 1 > 0,x + 8 > 0$ 得 $x$ 取值范围是 $x > 1$
        
        $\because {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 4}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 1 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 8}\right) ,\therefore {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 4}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = {\log }_{2}2 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 8}\right)$
        
        $\therefore {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 4}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right)  = {\log }_{2}2\left( {x + 8}\right)$ 即 $\left( {x + 4}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right)  = 2\left( {x + 8}\right)$
        
        化简得 ${x}^{2} + x - {20} = 0$ ,解得 $x =  - 5$ (舍) 或 $x = 4$
        
        即 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + 4}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 1 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 8}\right)$ 的解是 $x = 4$
    \end{jieda}
    
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 解方程 ${4}^{x} + {4}^{-x} - 5\left( {{2}^{x} + {2}^{-x}}\right)  + 6 = 0$
    \begin{jieda}
        设$t = {2}^{x} + {2}^{-x}, t \geq 2$，则${4}^{x} + {4}^{-x} = t^2 - 2$，故有$t^2-5t+4=0$，解得$t = 4$或$1$（舍去）

        因此${2}^{x} + {2}^{-x} = 4$，设$p = 2^x$，故$p + \dfrac{1}{p} = 4$，解得$p = 2 \pm \sqrt{3}$，故原方程解集为$\left\{log_2(2+\sqrt{3}), log_2(2-\sqrt{3})\right\}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 求方程 ${9}^{x} + {4}^{x} = \dfrac{5}{2} \cdot  {6}^{x}$ 的解集
    \begin{jieda}
        ${9}^{x} + {4}^{x} = \dfrac{5}{2} \cdot  {6}^{x} \Longleftrightarrow \left(\dfrac{3}{2}\right)^x+\left(\dfrac{2}{3}\right)^x = \dfrac{5}{2}$，故$\left(\dfrac{3}{2}\right)^x = 2$或$\dfrac{1}{2}$，因此解集为$\left\{\log_{\frac{3}{2}}2, log_{\frac{3}{2}}\dfrac{1}{2}\right\}$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 解方程：${\log }_{2}\left( {{4}^{x} + 4}\right)  = x + {\log }_{2}\left( {{2}^{x + 1} - 3}\right)$ .
    \begin{jieda}
        ${\log }_{2}\left( {{4}^{x} + 4}\right)  = x + {\log }_{2}\left( {{2}^{x + 1} - 3}\right) \Longleftrightarrow {\log }_{2}\left( {{4}^{x} + 4}\right)  = {\log }_{2}[\left( {{2}^{x + 1} - 3}\right)\cdot \left(2^x\right)] \\
        \Longleftrightarrow 4^x+4 = \left( {{2}^{x + 1} - 3}\right)\cdot \left(2^x\right)$， 令$t = 2^x, t > 0$，则$t^2+4 = (2t-3)t$，解得$t = 4$或$-1$（舍去）
    
        故原方程解集为：$\{2\}$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 解方程: ${\log }_{3}\left( {{3}^{x} - 1}\right)  \cdot  {\log }_{3}\left( {{3}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  = 2$ .
    \begin{jieda}
        令$t = {\log }_{3}\left( {{3}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)$，则有$t(t+1) = 2$，解得$t \in \{1, -2\}$
        
        故原方程解集为：$\{log_310, log_34 - 1\}$
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\section*{幂函数及其性质}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 下列函数中, 是奇函数又是严格增函数的为 \dotfill{(\kh{B})}
    \xx{$y = x + 1$}{$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$}{$y = \dfrac{1}{x}$}{$y =  - {x}^{3}$}
    \item 下列函数中,在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增的是 \dotfill{(\kh{C})}
    \xx{$y =  - {x}^{2} + 1$}{$y = \left| {x - 1}\right|$}{$y = {x}^{3}$}{$y = \dfrac{1}{x}$}
    \item 图中 ${C}_{1},{C}_{2},{C}_{3}$ 分别为幂函数 $y = {x}^{{\alpha }_{1}},y = {x}^{{\alpha }_{2}},y = {x}^{{\alpha }_{3}}$ 在第一象限内的图象,则 ${\alpha }_{1}$ , ${\alpha }_{2},{\alpha }_{3}$ 依次可以是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
    
    \begin{center}
    \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2em7r3ef24c73b07a3g_1_242_562_316_304_0.jpg}
    \end{center}
    \xx{$\dfrac{1}{2},3, - 1$}{$- 1,3,\dfrac{1}{2}$}{$\dfrac{1}{2}, - 1,3$}{$- 1,\dfrac{1}{2},3$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由题图知: ${\alpha }_{1} < 0,0 < {\alpha }_{2} < 1,{\alpha }_{3} > 1$ ,

        所以 ${\alpha }_{1},{\alpha }_{2},{\alpha }_{3}$ 依次可以是 $- 1,\dfrac{1}{2},3$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 现有下列函数: 
    \begin{enumerate*}
        \item $y = {x}^{3}$
        \item $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{x}$
        \item $y = 4{x}^{2}$
        \item $y = {x}^{5} + 1$
        \item $y = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2}$
        \item $y = x$
        \item $y = {a}^{x}\left( {a > 1}\right)$
    \end{enumerate*}
    其中幂函数的序号为\blkda[4]{(1)(6)}
    \item 若幂函数 $y = {x}^{a}$ 的图象经过点 $\left( {\sqrt[4]{3},3}\right)$ ,则实数 $a =$ \blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为幂函数 $y = {x}^{a}$ 的图象经过点 $\left( {\sqrt[4]{3},3}\right)$ ,所以 $3 = {\left( \sqrt[4]{3}\right) }^{a}$，所以 $3 = {3}^{\frac{a}{4}}$ ,所以 $a = 4$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 函数 $y = {\left( x + 2\right) }^{\frac{1}{2}}$ 的定义域为\blkda[4]{$\lbrack  - 2, + \infty )$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        要使 $y = {\left( x + 2\right) }^{\frac{1}{2}} = \sqrt{x + 2}$ 有意义,则 $x + 2 \geq  0$ ,解得 $x \geq   - 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若幂函数 $y = {x}^{-{m}^{2} + {2m}}$ ( $m$ 为整数)的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,则 $m$ 的值为\blkda[4]{1}.
    \begin{jieda}
        若幂函数 $y = {x}^{-{m}^{2} + {2m}}$ ( $m$ 为整数) 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,则 $- {m}^{2} + {2m} > 0$ ,解得$0 < m < 2$，而 $m$ 是整数,则只能 $m = 1$ ,经检验符合题意.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若幂函数 $y = \left( {{a}^{2} - a - 5}\right) {x}^{a}$ 的图像关于 $y$ 轴对称,则实数 $a =$ \blkda[4]{$-2$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由幂函数可得 ${a}^{2} - a - 5 = 1$ ,解得 $a = 3$ 或 $a =  - 2$ ,
        
        又因为函数图像关于 $y$ 轴对称,则 $a$ 为偶数,所以 $a =  - 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 幂函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像经过点 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},4}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\right)$ 的值为\blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{\alpha }$ ,将 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},4}\right)$ 代入 $f\left( x\right)$ ,可得: $\alpha  =  - 2$，所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-2}$ ,所以 $f\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\right) }^{-2} = 2$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若幂函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像经过点 $\left( {-2, - \dfrac{1}{8}}\right)$ ,则满足 $f\left( x\right)  = {27}$ 的 $x$ 的值是\blkda{$\dfrac{1}{3}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $y = x^{-3}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 当 $\alpha  \in  \mathbf{R}$ 时,函数 $y = {x}^{\alpha } - 2$ 的图象恒过定点 $A$ ,则点 $A$ 的坐标为\blkda[4]{$(1, -1)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由于对任意的 $\alpha  \in  \mathbf{R},y = {x}^{\alpha }$ 恒经过点(1,1),所以函数 $y = {x}^{\alpha } - 2$ 的图象恒过定点 $A\left( {1, - 1}\right)$ 
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = {\left( 2x - 1\right) }^{n} + 1$ ,则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象恒过的定点 $P$ 的坐标为\blkda[4]{$(1,2)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        令 ${2x} - 1 = 1$ ,得 $x = 1,y = 2$，故函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 图象过定点 $P\left( {1,2}\right)$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若幂函数 $y = \left( {{m}^{2} - m - 1}\right) {x}^{m}$ 为偶函数,则 $m =$ \blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because$ 函数 $y = \left( {{m}^{2} - m - 1}\right) {x}^{m}$ 为幂函数,
        
        $\therefore {m}^{2} - m - 1 = 1$ ,解得 $m = 2$ 或 $m =  - 1$ ,
        
        又 $\because y = {x}^{m}$ 为偶函数，$\therefore m = 2$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知集合 $A = \left\{  {y \mid  y = {x}^{2},x \in  R}\right\}$ ,集合 $B = \left\{  {y \mid  y =  - {x}^{2} + {3x} - 1,x \in  R}\right\}$ ,集合 $C$ 为函数$f\left( x\right)  = \sqrt{-{x}^{2} + {4x} + m - 7}$ 的定义域.
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 求 $A \cap  B$
        \item 若 $A \cup  C \subseteq  A$ ,求实数 $m$ 的取值范围
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        集合 $A = \left\{  {y \mid  y = {x}^{2},x \in  R}\right\}   = \{ y \mid  y \geq  0\}$ ,集合 $B = \left\{  {y \mid  y =  - {x}^{2} + {3x} - 1}\right.,x \in  R\}  = \left\{  {y\left| {y =  - {\left( x - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{5}{4} \leq  \dfrac{5}{4}}\right. }\right\}  ,$
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item $A \cap  B = \{ y \mid  y \geq  0\}  \cap  \left\{  {y\left| {y =  - {\left( x - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{5}{4} \leq  \dfrac{5}{4}}\right. }\right\}   = \left\{ y \left |  0 \leq  y \leq  \dfrac{5}{4}\right\}\right.$
            \item 集合 $C$ 为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sqrt{-{x}^{2} + {4x} + m - 7}$ 的定义域,
            
            $\therefore  - {x}^{2} + {4x} + m - 7 \geq  0$ . 解得: $2 - \sqrt{m - 3} \leq  x \leq  2 + \sqrt{m - 3}$ 且 $m \geq  3$ .
            
            所以 $C = \left\{  {x\left| {2 - \sqrt{m - 3} \leq  x \leq  2 + \sqrt{m - 3}\text{ 且 }m \geq  3}\right. }\right\}$ ;
            
            若 $A \cup  C \subseteq  A$ ,所以 $C \subseteq  A$ ,则 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} 2 - \sqrt{m - 3} \geq  0, \\  m \geq  3, \end{array}} \right.$，解得: $3 \leq m \leq  7$
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 下列幂函数中,其图象关于 $y$ 轴对称且过点 $\left( {0,0}\right) \text{ 、 }\left( {1,1}\right)$ 的是 \dotfill{(\kh{B})}  
    \xx{$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$}{$y = {x}^{4}$}{$y = {x}^{-2}$}{$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$}
    \item 已知幂函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 经过点 $\left( {3,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ \dotfill{(\kh{D})}  
    \xx{是偶函数,且在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是增函数}{是偶函数,且在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是减函数}{是奇函数,且在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是减函数}{是非奇非偶函数,且在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是增函数}
    \begin{jieda}
        设幂函数的解析式为 $y = {x}^{\alpha }$ ,
        
        将点 $\left( {3,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ 的坐标代入解析式得 ${3}^{\alpha } = \sqrt{3}$ ,解得 $\alpha  = \frac{1}{2}$ ,
        
        $\therefore y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$ ,函数的定义域为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,是非奇非偶函数,且在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是增函数
    \end{jieda}
    \item 如图,下列 3 个幂函数的图象,则其图象对应的函数可能是\dotfill{(\kh{A})} 

    \threetu{\includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2f9lh3ef24c73b0d7eg_1_240_194_185_170_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2f9lh3ef24c73b0d7eg_1_425_194_175_169_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2f9lh3ef24c73b0d7eg_1_603_193_175_166_0.jpg}}
    
    \xx{\ding{172}$y = {x}^{-1}$\ding{173}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$\ding{174}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$}
    {\ding{172}$y = {x}^{-1}$\ding{173}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$\ding{174}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$}
    {\ding{172}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$\ding{173}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$\ding{174}$y = {x}^{-1}$}
    {\ding{172}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$\ding{173}$y = {x}^{-1}$\ding{174}$y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由函数 $y = {x}^{-1} = \frac{1}{x}$ 是反比例函数,其对应图象为\ding{172};
        
        函数 $y = {x}^{\frac{1}{2}} = \sqrt{x}$ 的定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,应为图\ding{173}:
        
        因为 $y = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 且为奇函数,故应为图\ding{174}.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设集合 $A = (1, +\infty)$, $B = \left\{  {y \mid  y = {x}^{k},x \in  A}\right\}$ (其中 $k$ 是常数),则 “ $k < 0$ ” 是 “ $A \cap  B = \varnothing$ ” 的\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
    \xx{充分非必要条件}{必要非充分条件}{充分必要条件}{既非充分又非必要条件}
    \begin{jieda}
        $k < 0$时$B = (0, 1)$可知充分性，$k = 0$时$B = \{1\}$，可知非必要性.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 下列命题中正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}  
    \xx{当 $\alpha  = 0$ 时,函数 $y = {x}^{\alpha }$ 的图像是一条直线}{幂函数的图像都经过(0,0)和(1,1)点}{幂函数 $y = {x}^{-\frac{3}{2}}$ 的定义域为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$}{幂函数的图像不可能出现在第四象限}
    \begin{jieda}
        对于 ${A},\alpha  = 0$ 时,函数 $y = {x}^{0}$ 的图像是一条直线除去(0,1)点,故 ${A}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${B}$ ,幂函数的图像都经过(1,1)点,当指数大于 0 时,都经过(0,0)点,当指数小于 0 时, 不经过(0,0)点,故 ${B}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${C}$ ,函数 $y = {x}^{-\frac{3}{2}} = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{x}^{3}}}$ ,故定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,故错误;
        
        对于 ${D}$ ,由幂函数的性质,幂函数的图像一定过第一象限,不可能出现在第四象限,故正确.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知幂函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像过点 $\left( {3,\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ ,则该幂函数的值域是\blkda[4]{$\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{\alpha },\alpha  \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,
        
        代入点 $\left( {3,\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ 可得 ${3}^{\alpha } = \frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} = {3}^{-\frac{1}{2}}$ ,即 $\alpha  =  - \frac{1}{2}$ ,
        
        可得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-\frac{1}{2}} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}$ ,
        
        因为 $\sqrt{x} > 0$ ,可得 $f\left( x\right)  = \frac{1}{\sqrt{x}} > 0$ ,所以该幂函数的值域是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {{m}^{2} - {5m} + 7}\right) {x}^{m}$ 的图象关于 $y$ 轴对称,则实数 $m$ 的值是\blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 $f\left( x\right)$ 为幂函数,则 ${m}^{2} - {5m} + 7 = 1$ ,解得 $m = 2$ ,或 $m = 3$ ,
        
        当 $m = 2$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ ,其图象关于 $y$ 轴对称,
        
        当 $m = 3$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3}$ ,其图象关于(0,0)对称,
        
        因此 $m = 2$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$ ,则关于 $t$ 的表达式 $f\left( {{t}^{2} - {2t}}\right)  + f\left( {2{t}^{2} - 1}\right)  < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\dfrac{1}{3},1}\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意可知, $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {\left( -x\right) }^{\frac{1}{3}} =  - {x}^{\frac{1}{3}} =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,
        
        所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,
        
        由幂函数的性质知,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{\frac{1}{3}}$ 在函数 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增,
        
        由 $f\left( {{t}^{2} - {2t}}\right)  + f\left( {2{t}^{2} - 1}\right)  < 0$ ,得 $f\left( {{t}^{2} - {2t}}\right)  <  - f\left( {2{t}^{2} - 1}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( {{t}^{2} - {2t}}\right)  < f\left( {1 - 2{t}^{2}}\right)$ ,
        
        所以 ${t}^{2} - {2t} < 1 - 2{t}^{2}$ ,即 $3{t}^{2} - {2t} - 1 < 0$ ,解得 $- \frac{1}{3} < t < 1$ ,
        
        所以关于 $t$ 的表达式 $f\left( {{t}^{2} - {2t}}\right)  + f\left( {2{t}^{2} - 1}\right)  < 0$ 的解集为 $\left( {-\dfrac{1}{3},1}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 ${\left( 2x + 1\right) }^{\frac{2}{3}} < {\left( x - 3\right) }^{\frac{2}{3}}$ 的解为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-4,\dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{\frac{2}{3}} = \sqrt[3]{{x}^{2}}$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,且函数在 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上单调递增,
        
        又 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \sqrt[3]{{\left( -x\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt[3]{{x}^{2}} = f\left( x\right)$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上单调递减,
        
        则由不等式 ${\left( 2x + 1\right) }^{\frac{2}{3}} < {\left( x - 3\right) }^{\frac{2}{3}}$ 可得 $\left| {{2x} + 1}\right|  < \left| {x - 3}\right|$ ,平方后整理得 $3{x}^{2} + {10x} - 8 < 0$ ,
        
        即 $\left( {{3x} - 2}\right) \left( {x + 4}\right)  < 0$ ,解得 $- 4 < x < \dfrac{2}{3}$ ,则不等式的解集为 $\left( {-4,\dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{a}$ 的图象过点 $\left( {2,\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ ,若 $f\left( {{2m} + 1}\right)  < f\left( 3\right)$ ,则 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\infty , - 2}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{a}$ 的图象过点 $\left( {2,\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ 得 ${2}^{a} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,解得 $a =  - 2$ ,
        
        则 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-2} = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,定义域为 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ .
        
        由 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{\left( -x\right) }^{2}} = f\left( x\right)$ 可得 $f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数,
        
        又幂函数的单调性可知,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减.
        
        于是 $f\left( {{2m} + 1}\right)  < f\left( 3\right)$ 等价于 $\left| {{2m} + 1}\right|  > 3$ ,解得 $m <  - 2$ 或 $m > 1$ .
        
        所以 $m$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {-\infty , - 2}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-{m}^{2} + {4m}}\left( {m \in  Z}\right)$ 的图象关于 $y$ 轴对称,且在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数.
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 求 $m$ 的值
        \item 求满足不等式 $f\left( {{2a} - 1}\right)  < f\left( {a + 1}\right)$ 的实数 $a$ 的取值范围.
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 因为幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-{m}^{2} + {4m}}$ 在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,
            
            所以 $- {m}^{2} + {4m} > 0$ ,解得 $0 < m < 4$ ,
            
            又因为 $m \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,所以 $m = 1$ 或 $m = 2$ 或 $m = 3$ ,
            
            当 $m = 1$ 或 $m = 3$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3}$ 为奇函数,图象关于原点对称 (舍);
            
            当 $m = 2$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{4}$ 为偶函数,图象关于 $y$ 轴对称,符合题意;
            
            综上所述, $m = 2$ .
            \item 由(1)得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{4}$ 为偶函数,且在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,
            
            则由 $f\left( {{2a} - 1}\right)  < f\left( {a + 1}\right)$ 得 $\left| {{2a} - 1}\right|  < \left| {a + 1}\right|$ ,
            
            即 ${\left( 2a - 1\right) }^{2} < {\left( a + 1\right) }^{2}$ ,即 ${a}^{2} - {2a} < 0$ ,解得 $0 < a < 2$ ,
            
            所以满足 $f\left( {{2a} - 1}\right)  < f\left( {a + 1}\right)$ 的实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $0 < a < 2$ .
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知幂函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {{m}^{2} + {4m} + 4}\right) {x}^{m + 2}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减.
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 求 $m$ 的值
        \item 若 ${\left( 2a - 1\right) }^{-m} < {\left( a + 3\right) }^{-m}$ ,求 $a$ 的取值范围.
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 由幂函数的定义可得 ${m}^{2} + {4m} + 4 = 1$ ,即 ${m}^{2} + {4m} + 3 = 0$ ,解得 $m =  - 1$ 或 $m =  - 3$ .
            
            因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减,所以 $m + 2 < 0$ ,即 $m <  - 2$，则 $m =  - 3$ .

            \item 设 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3},g\left( x\right)$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的增函数.
            
            由( 1 )可知 ${\left( 2a - 1\right) }^{-m} < {\left( a + 3\right) }^{-m}$ ,即 ${\left( 2a - 1\right) }^{3} < {\left( a + 3\right) }^{3}$ ,
            
            则 ${2a} - 1 < a + 3$ ,解得 $a < 4$ ,
            
            即 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\infty ,4}\right)$ .
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 若关于 $x$ 的不等式 ${x}^{\frac{4}{3}} + {2x} + a + \left| {{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} - a}\right|  \geq  2$ 的解集为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,则实数 $a$ 能取到的最小值为\blkda[4]{3}.
    \begin{jieda}
        ${x}^{\frac{4}{3}} + {2x} + a + \left| {{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} - a}\right|  \geq  2 \Longleftrightarrow \left| {{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} - a}\right| \geq -{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} + (2-a)$

        即${x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} - a \geq -{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} + (2-a)$ 或 ${x}^{\frac{4}{3}} - {2x} - a \leq{x}^{\frac{4}{3}} + {2x} + (a-2)$

        即${x}^{\frac{4}{3}} \geq 1$或$2x+a-1 \geq 0$

        因此应有两个不等式的解集的并集为$\mathbf{R}$，即$(-\infty, -1] \cup [1, +\infty) \cup \left[\dfrac{1-a}{2}, +\infty\right) = \mathbf{R}$，故$a \geq  3$
    \end{jieda}

    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c\left( {a,b,c \in  \mathbf{R},a > 0}\right)$ ,则 “ $f\left( {f\left( {-\dfrac{b}{2a}}\right) }\right)  < 0$ ” 是 “ $f\left( x\right)$ 与 $f\left( {f\left( x\right) }\right)$ 都恰有两个零点”的\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
    \xx{充分不必要条件}{必要不充分条件}{充要条件}{既不充分也不必要条件}
    \begin{jieda}
        设 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图象的顶点, ${x}_{1}\text{ 、 }{x}_{2}$ 且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的两个零点 (若有). 因为 $f\left( {f\left( {-\dfrac{b}{2a}}\right) }\right)  < 0$ ,即 $f\left( {y}_{0}\right)  < 0$ ,故 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 必存在,且 ${x}_{1} < {y}_{0} < {x}_{2}$ . 故此时显然 $f\left( x\right)$ 恰有两个零点; 再令 $f\left( {f\left( x\right) }\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{1}$ 或 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{2}$
        
        根据 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象可知,当 $k > {y}_{0}$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = k$ 有两解; 当 $k = {y}_{0}$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = k$ 有一解; 当 $k < {y}_{0}$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = k$ 无解; 因此 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{1}$ 无解, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{2}$ 有两解,综上, $f\left( {f\left( x\right) }\right)$ 恰有两个零点,满足充分性. 
        
        若 $f\left( x\right)$ 有两个零点,则 ${x}_{1}\text{ 、 }{x}_{2}$ 必存在. 由 $f\left( {f\left( x\right) }\right)  = 0$ 得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{1}$ 或 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{2}$ ,因为 $f\left( {f\left( x\right) }\right)  = 0$ 也只有两个零点,因此 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{1}$ 无解, $f\left( x\right)  = {x}_{2}$ 有两个不等实根,即 ${x}_{1} <$  ${y}_{0} < {x}_{2}$ ,所以 $f\left( {f\left( {-\dfrac{b}{2a}}\right) }\right)  < 0$ ,必要性得证.
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}

\newpage
\quad
\newpage
\section*{指数函数及其性质}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 设 $a > 0,s \in  \mathbf{R}$ . 下列各项中,能推出 ${a}^{s} > a$ 的一项是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
    \xx{$a > 1$ ,且 $s > 0$}{$a > 1$ ,且 $s < 0$}{$0 < a < 1$ ,且 $s > 0$}{$0 < a < 1$ ,且 $s < 0$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because a > 0,{a}^{s} > a,\therefore {a}^{s - 1} > 1 = {a}^{0}$ ,
        
        当 $a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $y = {a}^{x}$ 定义域上严格单调递减,
        
        此时若 $s - 1 < 0$ ,则一定有 ${a}^{s - 1} > 1 = {a}^{0}$ 成立,故 ${D}$ 正确, ${C}$ 错误;
        
        当 $a \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时, $y = {a}^{x}$ 定义域上严格单调递增,要满足 ${a}^{s - 1} > 1 = {a}^{0}$ ,需 $s > 1$ ,即 ${A}\text{ 、 }{B}$ 错误.
    \end{jieda}
    \item  函数 $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{{x}^{2} - {2x}}$ 的值域为\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
    \xx{$(0,2\rbrack$}{$\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$}{$\lbrack 2, + \infty )$}{$\lbrack 1, + \infty )$}
    \begin{jieda}
        依题意，令 $t = {x}^{2} - {2x}$ ,则 $t = {x}^{2} - {2x} = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} - 1 \geq   - 1$，因为 $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{t}$ 单调递减,且 $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{t} > 0$
        
        所以 $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{t} \leq  {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{-1} = 2$ ,
        
        所以 $y \in  (0,2\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 下列函数是指数函数的序号为\blkda[4]{\ding{173}\ding{174}\ding{176}}. (请填入全部正确的序号)
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item $y = {\left( -4\right) }^{x}$
        \item $y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{x}$
        \item $y = {4}^{x}$
        \item $y = {x}^{-4}$
        \item $y = {\left( \sqrt{4}\right) }^{x}$
    \end{dingautolist}

    \item 若函数 $y = \left( {{m}^{2} - {2m} - 2}\right)  \cdot  {m}^{x}$ 是指数函数,则 $m$ 等于\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
    \xx{$-1$ 或 $3$}{$-1$}{3}{$\dfrac{1}{3}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $y = \left( {{m}^{2} - {2m} - 2}\right)  \cdot  {m}^{x}$ 是指数函数，所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {m}^{2} - {2m} - 2 = 1 \\  m > 0 \\  m \neq  1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  m = 3}\right.$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若函数 $y = \left( {{a}^{2} - {3a} + 3}\right) {a}^{x}$ 为指数函数,则 $a =$ \blkda[4]{2}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $y = \left( {{a}^{2} - {3a} + 3}\right) {a}^{x}$ 为指数函数,
        
        所以 ${a}^{2} - {3a} + 3 = 1$ 且 $a > 0$ 且 $a \neq  1$ ,解得 $a = 2$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 函数 $y = {a}^{x} - 1\left( {a > 0\text{ 且 }a \neq  1}\right)$ 的图像一定过点\blkda[4]{$(0, 0)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        函数 $y = {a}^{x} - 1\left( {a > 0\text{ 且 }a \neq  1}\right)$ ,令 $x = 0$ 可得 $y = {a}^{0} - 1 = 0$
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 函数 $y = {a}^{x + 2} - 3\left( {a > 0\text{ ,且 }a \neq  1}\right)$ 的图象过定点 $A$ ,则点 $A$ 的坐标是\blkda[4]{$(-2, -2)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $y = {a}^{x + 2} - 3\left( {a > 0\text{ ,且 }a \neq  1}\right)$ 的图象过定点 $A$，令 $x + 2 = 0$ ,则 $x =  - 2,y = {a}^{0} - 3 =  - 2$，所以点 $A$ 的坐标为$(-2, - 2)$.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{matrix} {2}^{x},x > 0 \\  1,x \leq  0 \end{matrix}\right.$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 的值域是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 1, + \infty )$}
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $x > 0$ 时,根据指数函数的图象与性质知 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} > 1$ ,
        
        当 $x \leq  0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = 1$ .
        
        综上: $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 函数 $y = {3}^{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 上的最小值是\blkda[4]{$\sqrt{3}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        因函数 $y = {3}^{x}$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的增函数,且 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$，则 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时,函数 $y = {3}^{x}$ 取得最小值为 ${3}^{\frac{1}{2}} = \sqrt{3}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 函数 $y = \sqrt{{3}^{x} - 1}$ 的定义域是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 0, + \infty )$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题知, ${3}^{x} - 1 \geq  0$ ,解得 $x \geq  0$，所以函数 $y = \sqrt{{3}^{x} - 1}$ 的定义域是 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 函数 $y = \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{x} - 1}$ 的定义域为\blkda[4]{$( - \infty ,0\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题设 ${\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{x} - 1 \geq  0$ ,故 $x \leq  {\log }_{\frac{1}{2}}1 = 0$ ,故定义域为 $( - \infty ,0\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若关于 $x$ 的方程 ${\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{x} = a$ 有负根,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围\blkda[4]{$\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        关于 $x$ 的方程 ${\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{x} = a$ 有负根等价于指数函数 $y = {\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{x}$ 与 $y = a$ 在第二象限有交点
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2ff4jbef24c73b0g1j0_4_240_195_458_309_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        当 $a > 1$ 时, $y = {\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{x}$ 与 $y = a$ 在第二象限有交点，所以实数 $a$ 的取值范围 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的奇函数,当 $x < 0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x}$ ,则 $f\left( {{\log }_{2}7}\right)  =$ \blkda{$-\dfrac{1}{7}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $log_27 > 0$，故$f\left( {{\log }_{2}7}\right)  = - f(-{{\log }_{2}7}) = - f\left({{\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{7}}\right) = -\dfrac{1}{7}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知常数 $a > 0$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{x}}{{2}^{x} + {ax}}$ 的图像经过点 $P\left( {p,\dfrac{6}{5}}\right) \text{ 、 }Q\left( {q, - \dfrac{1}{5}}\right)$ ,若 ${2}^{p + q} =$  ${25pq}$ ,则实数 $a =$ \blkda{5}
    \begin{jieda}
