        由条件可知 $\dfrac{{2}^{p}}{{2}^{p} + {ap}} = \dfrac{6}{5}$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{1 + \dfrac{ap}{{2}^{p}}} = \dfrac{6}{5}$ ,得 $\dfrac{ap}{{2}^{p}} =  - \dfrac{1}{6}$
        
        由 $\dfrac{{2}^{q}}{{2}^{q} + {aq}} =  - \dfrac{1}{5}$ ,得 $\dfrac{1}{1 + \dfrac{aq}{{2}^{q}}} = - \dfrac{1}{5}$，则 $\dfrac{aq}{{2}^{q}} =  - 6$
        
        故得 $\dfrac{{a}^{2}{pq}}{{2}^{p + q}} = 1$ ,又因为 ${2}^{p + q} = {25pq}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{a}^{2}}{25} = 1$ ,又 $a > 0$ ,得 $a = 5$
    \end{jieda}  
    \item 不等式 ${2}^{{x}^{2} - {2x} - 3} < {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{{3x} - 3}$ 与不等式 ${x}^{2} + {ax} + b < 0$ 解集相同,则 $a + b =$ \blkda[4]{$-5$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $\because {2}^{{x}^{2} - {2x} - 3} < {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{{3x} - 3} = {2}^{3 - {3x}}$ ,
        
        $\because y = {2}^{x}$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上单调递增,
        
        $\therefore {x}^{2} - {2x} - 3 < 3 - {3x}$ ,即 ${x}^{2} + x - 6 < 0$ ,
        
        $\therefore a = 1,b =  - 6$ ,
        
        $\therefore a + b =  - 5$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 高斯函数属于初等函数,以大数学家约翰·卡尔·弗里德里希·高斯的名字命名,高斯函数应用范围很广,在自然科学、社会科学、数学以及工程学等领域都能看到它的身影,设 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ , 用 $\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ 表示不超过 $x$ 的最大整数,则 $y = \left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ 称为高斯函数,例如: $\left\lbrack  {-{3.1}}\right\rbrack   =  - 4,\left\lbrack  {4.8}\right\rbrack   = 4$，则函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{{2}^{x + 1}}{1 + {2}^{x}} - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right\rbrack$ 的值域为\blkda{$\{-1, 0, 1\}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        先求$g(x) = \dfrac{{2}^{x + 1}}{1 + {2}^{x}} - \dfrac{1}{3}$的值域：

        $g(x) = \dfrac{5}{3} - \dfrac{2}{1+2^x}$，$x \in \mathbf{R}$，$2^x = (0, +\infty)$，$1+2^x \in (1, +\infty)$，$\dfrac{1}{1+2^x} \in (0, 1)$，$\dfrac{2}{1+2^x} \in (0, 2)$，$g(x) \in \left(-\dfrac{1}{3}, \dfrac{5}{3}\right)$

        故$f(x) \in \{-1, 0, 1\}$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {4}^{x} - \dfrac{5}{2} \cdot  {2}^{x + 1} - 6$ ,其中 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 的最大值为\blkda[4]{$18$}
    \begin{jieda}
        设$t = 2^x$，则$t \in [1, 8]$
        
        $f(x) = g(t) = t^2-5t-6 \leq g(8) = 18$
    \end{jieda}

    \item 已知 $- 1 \leq  x \leq  1$ ,求函数 $y = 4 \cdot  {3}^{x} - 2 \cdot  {9}^{x}$ 的最大值为\blkda[4]{2}
    \begin{jieda}
        令$t = 3^x, t \in \left[\dfrac{1}{3}, 3\right]$，$y = 4t-2t^2 \leq 2$
    \end{jieda}
  
    \item 函数$y=3\cdot4^x-20\cdot2^x+3\cdot4^{-x}-20\cdot2^{-x}$的最小值为\blkda[4]{$-\dfrac{118}{3}$} 
    \begin{jieda}
    令$t=2^x+2^{-x}\in[2,+\infty)$, 则
    $y = 3t^2-20t-6 = 3(t-\dfrac{10}{3})^2-\dfrac{118}{3}$ \\
    故$y_{\min}=-\dfrac{118}{3}$, 当$t=\dfrac{10}{3}$即$2^x = \dfrac{1}{3}\mbox{或}3, x=log_23 \mbox{或}-log_23$时取到. 
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若关于 $x$ 的方程 $\left| {{3}^{x} - 1}\right|  = k$ 有两解,则实数 $k$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$(0, 1)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        数形结合
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若实数 $x$ 、 $y$ 满足 $x + {2y} = 1$ ,则 ${2}^{x} + {4}^{y}$ 的最小值为\blkda[4]{$2\sqrt{2}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        ${2}^{x} + {4}^{y} \geq  2\sqrt{{2}^{x} \times  {4}^{y}} = 2\sqrt{{2}^{x} \times  {2}^{2y}} = 2\sqrt{{2}^{x + {2y}}} = 2\sqrt{2}$ ,当且仅当 $x = {2y}$，即 $x = \dfrac{1}{2},y = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时取到等号.
    \end{jieda}
    \item 设 $t \in  {R}$ ,若在区间(1,2)上,关于 $x$ 的不等式 ${2}^{x} > \dfrac{1}{x + t}$ 有意义且能恒成立,则 $t$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left( -\infty , - 2 \right \rbrack \cup \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty  }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意得 ${2}^{x} > \dfrac{1}{x + t}$ 在$(1,2)$上有意义,故 $x + t \neq  0$ 在(1,2)上恒成立，故 $t =  - x \in  \left( {-\infty , - 2\rbrack \cup \lbrack  - 1, + \infty }\right)$ 

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 $t \in  ( - \infty , - 2\rbrack$ 时, $x + t < 0$ ,而 ${2}^{x} > 0$ ,满足 ${2}^{x} > \dfrac{1}{x + t}$ ,符合题意
            \item 当 $t \in  \lbrack  - 1, + \infty )$ 时, $x + t > 0,{2}^{x} > \dfrac{1}{x + t} \Longrightarrow  t > \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}} - x$ 在$(1,2)$上恒成立

            令 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}} - x,x \in  \left( {1,2}\right)$，$g\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {1,2}\right)$ 上单调递减,
            
            故 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}} - x \in  \left( {-\dfrac{7}{4}, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$，故 $t \geq   - \dfrac{1}{2}$
        \end{dingautolist}
        综上, $t$ 的取值范围是 $\left( -\infty , - 2 \right \rbrack \cup \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty  }\right)$ 
    \end{jieda}
    \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}^{x},x \geq  1 \\  \left( {4 - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right) x + 2,x < 1 \end{array}\right.$ 在区间 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ 严格单调递增函数,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$[4,8)$}. 
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}^{x},x \geq  1 \\  \left( {4 - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right) x + 2,x < 1 \end{array}\right.$ 是 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ 上的单调递增函数,
        
        则满足 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a > 1 \\  4 - \dfrac{a}{2} > 0 \\  \left( {4 - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right)  \times  1 + 2 \leq  {a}^{1} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $4 \leq  a < 8$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 $m \in  \mathbf{R}$ , $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2}^{x},x \geq  0 \\  \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}},x < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,则满足 $f\left( {m - 2}\right)  \geq  f\left( {m + 3}\right)$ 的 $m$ 的最大值为\blkda[4]{$- \dfrac{1}{2}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数，由指数函数的单调性可知, $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增,
        
        因此,不等式 $f\left( {m - 2}\right)  \geq  f\left( {m + 3}\right)$ 等价于 $\left| {m - 2}\right|  \geq  \left| {m + 3}\right|$ ,
        
        即 ${\left( m - 2\right) }^{2} \geq  {\left( m + 3\right) }^{2}$ ,解得 $m \leq   - \dfrac{1}{2}$ .
        
        故 $m$ 的最大值为 $- \dfrac{1}{2}$
    \end{jieda} 
    
    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2}^{-\left| {x - a}\right| },x \leq  1 \\   - \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1,x > 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,若 $f\left( 1\right)$ 是函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的最大值,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $x > 1$ 时 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1$ 函数单调递减且 $f\left( x\right)  < \dfrac{1}{2}$
        
        当 $x \leq  1$ 时 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{-\left| {x - a}\right| } = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{\left| x - a\right| }$，结合图像可得在 $x < a$ 单调递增，在 $x > a$ 时函数单调递减，故必有$a \geq 1$，且 ${\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{a - 1} \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,解得 $1 \leq  a \leq  2$       
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知 $0 < a < 2$ ,函数 $y = \left\{  \begin{matrix} \left( {a - 2}\right) x + {4a} + 1, & x \leq  2 \\  2{a}^{x - 1}, & x > 2 \end{matrix}\right.$ ,若该函数存在最小值,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\{ a \left |  0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2} \text{或} a = 1\right\}\right.$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意,令 $g\left( x\right)  = \left( {a - 2}\right) x + {4a} + 1,x \in  ( - \infty ,2\rbrack ,h\left( x\right)  = 2{a}^{x - 1},x \in  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$

        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 当 $0 < a < 1$ 时, $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 上单调递减, $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减,则 $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 上的值域为$(0,2a)$,
            
            因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 存在最小值,故需 $g\left( 2\right)  = \left( {a - 2}\right)  \times  2 + {4a} + 1 \leq  0$ ,解得 $a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
            
            结合 $0 < a < 1$ ,此时 $0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$
            \item 当 $1 < a < 2$ 时, $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 上单调递减, $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增,则 $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 上的值域为 $\left( {{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ ,
            
            因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 存在最小值,故需 $g\left( 2\right)  \leq  {2a}$ ,即 $\left( {a - 2}\right)  \times  2 + {4a} + 1 \leq  {2a}$ ,解得 $a \leq  \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,
            
            这与 $1 < a < 2$ 矛盾

            \item 当 $a = 1$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  =  - x + 5$ 在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 上单调递减,且在 $( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 上的值域为 $\lbrack 3, + \infty ),h\left( x\right)  = 2$ , 此时存在最小值 2
        \end{dingautolist}
        故实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left\{  {a\left| {0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}}\text{或} a = 1\right. }\right\}$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = 3 \cdot  {2}^{x} + 2$ ,对于任意的 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,都存在 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {2f}\left( {{x}_{2} + m}\right)  = {13}$ 成立,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {{\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{6},{\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{3}}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        对于任意的 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,都存在 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 ${f}\left( {{x}_{2} + m}\right)  = \dfrac{{13} - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{2}$ 成立，故等式左侧的取值范围是等式右侧取值范围的子集.
        
        $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {5,8}\right\rbrack  $，故$\dfrac{{13} - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{5}{2},4}\right\rbrack$
        
        $f\left( {{x}_{2} + m}\right)  = 3 \cdot  {2}^{{x}_{2} + m} + 2 \in  \left\lbrack  {3 \cdot  {2}^{m} + 2,3 \cdot  {2}^{1 + m} + 2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        由题意得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} 3 \cdot  {2}^{m} + 2 \geq  \dfrac{5}{2} \\  3 \cdot  {2}^{m + 1} + 2 \leq  4 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {2}^{m} \geq  \dfrac{1}{6} \\  {2}^{m + 1} \leq  \dfrac{2}{3} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{6} \leq  m \leq  {\log }_{2}\dfrac{1}{3}}\right. }\right.$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} \dfrac{{x}^{2} + 4}{x} & x \geq  2 \\  {2}^{\left| x - a\right| } & x < 2 \end{array}\right.$ ,若对任意的 ${x}_{1} \in  \lbrack 2, + \infty )$ ,都存在唯一的 ${x}_{2} \in  \left( {-\infty ,2}\right)$ ,满足 $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$0 \leq  a < 4$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        设函数 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{2} + 4}{x}$，其在$x \in [2, +\infty)$的函数值取值范围为 ${A}$ ,函数 $h\left( x\right)  = {2}^{\left| x - a\right| }$，其在$x \in (-\infty, 2)$只对应一个自变量的函数值的取值范围为 $B$ , 因为对任意的 ${x}_{1} \in  \lbrack 2, + \infty )$ ,都存在唯一的 ${x}_{2} \in  \left( {-\infty ,2}\right)$ ,满足 $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$，则 $A \subseteq  B$.
        
        $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{2} + 4}{x} = x + \dfrac{4}{x}$，所以 $A = \lbrack 4, + \infty )$
        
         $h\left( x\right)  = {2}^{\left| x - a\right| }$在$(-\infty, a)$严格减，在$(a, +\infty)$严格增，结合图像可知
         \begin{dingautolist}{172}
             \item 当 $a \geq  2$ 时, $B = \left( {{2}^{a - 2}, + \infty }\right)$

             $\therefore {2}^{a - 2} < 4$ ,解得 $2 \leq  a < 4$

             \item 当 $a < 2$ 时, $B = \left( {{2}^{2-a}, + \infty }\right)$
             
             $\therefore {2}^{2 - a} \leq  4$ ,解得 $0 \leq  a < 2$
         \end{dingautolist}
        综合得 $0 \leq  a < 4$ 
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{a \cdot  {2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1}$ 
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 当 $a = 1$ 时,解方程 $\lg f\left( {2x}\right)  - \lg f\left( x\right)  = 1 - \lg {18}$
        \item 当 $x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 时, $\left| {f\left( {2x}\right)  - f\left( x\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ 恒成立,求实数 $a$ 的取值范围
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 当 $a = 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{a \cdot  {2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1} = \dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1},f\left( {2x}\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{2x} + 1}{{2}^{2x} - 1} = \dfrac{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1}$ 
            
            所以方程 $\lg f\left( {2x}\right)  - \lg f\left( x\right)  = 1 - \lg {18}$ 化为 $\lg \dfrac{f\left( {2x}\right) }{f\left( x\right) } = \lg \dfrac{10}{18}$ 且 $f\left( {2x}\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)  > 0$
            
            即 $\dfrac{f\left( {2x}\right) }{f\left( x\right) } = \dfrac{5}{9}$ 且 $\dfrac{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1} > 0$ ,
            $\dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1} > 0$
            
            所以 $\dfrac{\dfrac{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1}}{\dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1}} = \dfrac{5}{9}$ ,即 $\dfrac{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x} + 1\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{5}{9}$ 
            
            令 ${2}^{x} = t$ ,则原方程化为 $\dfrac{{t}^{2} + 1}{{\left( t + 1\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{5}{9}$ ,整理得 $2{t}^{2} - {5t} +$
            $2 = 0$ ,解得 $t = 2$ 或 $t = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 ${2}^{x} = 2$ 或 ${2}^{x} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,解得 $x = 1$ 或 $x =  - 1$
            
            当 $x =  - 1$ 时, $\dfrac{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1} < 0,\dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1} <0$,故舍去,故原方程的解为 $x = 1$
            
            \item 由 $\left| {f\left( {2x}\right)  - f\left( x\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ ,得 $\left| {\dfrac{a \cdot  {\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} + 1}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1} - \dfrac{a \cdot  {2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1}}\right|  \geq  1$ ,即
            $\left| \dfrac{-a \cdot  {2}^{x} - {2}^{x}}{{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - 1}\right|  \geq  1$
            
            令 ${2}^{x} = t$ ,当 $x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 时, $t \in  (1,2\rbrack$
            
            所以当 $x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 时,
            $\left| {f\left( {2x}\right)  - f\left( x\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ 恒成立，等价于当 $t \in  (1,2\rbrack$ 时, $\left| \dfrac{\left( {a + 1}\right)  \cdot  t}{{t}^{2} - 1}\right|  \geq  1$ 恒成立，即 $\left| {a + 1}\right|  \geq  \dfrac{{t}^{2} - 1}{t}$ 在
            $t \in  (1,2\rbrack$ 时恒成立
            
            令 $g\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{{t}^{2} - 1}{t} = t - \dfrac{1}{t}$，$g\left( t\right)$在 $(1,2\rbrack$ 上严格增
            
            $g\left( 2\right)  = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{3}{2},g\left( 1\right)  = 1 - 1 = 0$，则 $0 < g\left( t\right)  \leq  \dfrac{3}{2}$
            
            所以 $\left| {a + 1}\right|  \geq  \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,解得 $a \leq   - \dfrac{5}{2}$ 或 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}

\section*{对数函数及其性质}
\subsection*{A Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {4}^{x} + {\log }_{2}x$ ,则 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{1}.
    \begin{jieda}
        函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {4}^{x} + {\log }_{2}x$ ,所以 $f\left( \frac{1}{2}\right)  = {4}^{\frac{1}{2}} + {\log }_{2}\frac{1}{2} = 2 - 1 = 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\log }_{3}\left( {1 - x}\right) ,x < 1, \\  1 + {3}^{x - 1},x \geq  1, \end{array}\right.$ 则 $f\left( {-8}\right)  + f\left( {{\log }_{3}{15}}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{8}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意，$f\left( {-8}\right)  + f\left( {{\log }_{3}{15}}\right)  = {\log }_{3}9 + 1 + {3}^{{\log }_{3}{15} - 1} = {\log }_{3}9 + 1 + {3}^{{\log }_{3}5} = 2 + 1 + 5 = 8$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 函数 $y = \lg \left( {{x}^{2} - {5x} + 4}\right)$ 的定义域为\blkda[4]{$\left( {4, + \infty }\right)  \cup  \left( {-\infty ,1}\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由对数函数定义可得 ${x}^{2} - {5x} + 4 > 0$ ,解得 $x > 4$ 或 $x < 1$ ,
        
        所以函数定义域为 $\left( {4, + \infty }\right)  \cup  \left( {-\infty ,1}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 在对数式中 ${\log }_{\left( x - 1\right) }\left( {3 - x}\right)$ ,实数 $x$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$1 < x < 3$ 且 $x \neq  2$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由对数式 ${\log }_{\left( x - 1\right) }\left( {3 - x}\right)$ 可知:
        
        $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x - 1 > 0 \\  x - 1 \neq  1 \\  3 - x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解之得: $1 < x < 3$ 且 $x \neq  2$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 函数 $y = {\log }_{3}\left( {{x}^{2} - {2x} + 4}\right)$ 的值域是\blkda[4]{$\lbrack 1, + \infty )$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        易知 ${x}^{2} - {2x} + 4 = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + 3 \geq  3$， 又 $y = {\log }_{3}x$ 定义域上单调递增，所以 $y = {\log }_{3}\left( {{x}^{2} - {2x} + 4}\right)  \geq  {\log }_{3}3 = 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = 1 + {\log }_{a}\left( {{2x} - 3}\right) \left( {a > 0,a \neq  1}\right)$ 恒过定点$(m, n)$,则 $m + n =$\blkda[4]{3}
    \begin{jieda}
        令 ${2x} - 3 = 1$ ,解得 $x = 2$ ,此时 $f\left( 2\right)  = 1 + {\log }_{a}1 = 1$，所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 恒过定点(2,1),则 $m = 2,n = 1$，所以 $m + n = 3$ .
    \end{jieda}
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}\left( {x - b}\right)$ ( $a > 0$ 且 $a \neq  1,a,b$ 为常数) 的图象如图,则下列结论正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
    
    \begin{center}
    \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gik7bef24c73b0ne3g_0_239_1183_471_383_0.jpg}
    \end{center}

    \xx{$a > 0,b <  - 1$}{$a > 0, - 1 < b < 0$}{$0 < a < 1,b <  - 1$}{$0 < a < 1, - 1 < b < 0$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}\left( {x - b}\right)$ 为减函数,所以 $0 < a < 1$
        
        又因为函数图象与 $x$ 轴的交点在正半轴,所以 $x = 1 + b > 0$ ,即 $b >  - 1$
        
        又因为函数图象与 $y$ 轴有交点,所以 $b < 0$ ,所以 $- 1 < b < 0$
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 根据下列不等式,比较正数 $m$ 及 $n$ 的大小:
    \begin{dingautolist}{172}
        \item ${\log }_{3}m < {\log }_{3}n$
        \item ${\log }_{a}m < {\log }_{a}n$ ( $a > 0$ 且 $a \neq  1$ )
        \item ${\log }_{m}N < {\log }_{n}N\left( {0 < m < 1,0 < n < 1,0 < N < 1}\right)$ 
    \end{dingautolist}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item $n > m$
            \item 当 $a > 1$ 时, $n > m$ ；当 $0 < a < 1$ 时, $m > n$
            \item $n > m$
        \end{dingautolist}
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 不等式 $\lg \left( {x + 1}\right)  > 1$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {9, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由不等式 $\lg \left( {x + 1}\right)  > 1$ ,得 $x + 1 > {10}$ ,解得 $x > 9$ ,
        
        所以不等式 $\lg \left( {x + 1}\right)  > 1$ 的解集为 $\left( {9, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda} 
    \item 已知 $a \in  R,{\log }_{2}\left( {{4a} + 3}\right)  < 2$ ,则 $a$ 的取值范围为\blkda[4]{$\left( {-\dfrac{3}{4},\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        由 ${\log }_{2}\left( {{4a} + 3}\right)  < 2$ ,得 $0 < {4a} + 3 < 4$ ,解得 $- \frac{3}{4} < a < \frac{1}{4}$ ,
        
        所以 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{4},\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 方程 $\lg \left( {2 - x}\right)  + \lg \left( {3 - x}\right)  = \lg {12}$ 的解是\blkda[4]{$x =  - 1$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由方程 $\lg \left( {2 - x}\right)  + \lg \left( {3 - x}\right)  = \lg {12}$ ,可得 $\lg \left\lbrack  {\left( {2 - x}\right) \left( {3 - x}\right) }\right\rbrack   = \lg {12}$
        
        $\therefore \left\{  \begin{array}{l} 2 - x > 0 \\  3 - x > 0 \\  \left( {2 - x}\right) \left( {3 - x}\right)  = {12} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $x =  - 1$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 不等式 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + {14}}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 2}\right)  > 3 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 6}\right)$ 的解集为\blkda[4]{$\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + {14}}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 2}\right)  > 3 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 6}\right)$，可得 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + {14}}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  > {\log }_{2}8\left( {x + 6}\right)$，又 $y = {\log }_{2}x$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {x + {14}}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)  > 8\left( {x + 6}\right) \\  x + {14} > 0 \\  x + 2 > 0 \\  x + 6 > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解不等式组可得 $x > 2$，所以不等式 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + {14}}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 2}\right)  > 3 + {\log }_{2}\left( {x + 6}\right)$ 的解集为 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{B Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 下列函数中,在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增的是 \dotfill{(\kh{C})}
    \xx{$f\left( x\right)  =  - \ln x$}{$f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}}$}{$f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{x}$}{$f\left( x\right)  = {3}^{\left| x - 1\right| }$}
    \item 函数 $y = \dfrac{\ln \left| x\right| }{{x}^{2} + 2}$ 的图像大致为\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
    \xx{\includegraphics[width=0.37\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_0_330_545_457_391_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.37\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_0_865_548_461_389_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.37\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_0_332_937_453_388_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.37\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_0_867_937_444_385_0.jpg}}
    \begin{jieda}
        设 $y = f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\ln \left| x\right| }{{x}^{2} + 2}$ ,则函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\{ x \mid  x \neq  0\}$ ,关于原点对称,
        
        又 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{\ln \left| {-x}\right| }{{\left( -x\right) }^{2} + 2} = f\left( x\right)$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数,排除 ${{AC}}$ ;
        
        当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $\ln \left| x\right| < 0, {x}^{2} + 2 >  0$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,排除 ${D}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 在天文学中, 天体的明暗程度可以用星等或亮度来描述. 两颗星的星等与亮度满足 ${m}_{2} - {m}_{1} = \dfrac{5}{2}\lg \dfrac{{E}_{1}}{{E}_{2}}$ ,其中星等为 ${m_k}$ 的星的亮度为 ${E_k}\left( {k = 1,2}\right)$ . 已知太阳的星等是-26.7,天狼星的星等是 -1.45 ,则太阳与天狼星的亮度的比值为\blkda[4]{${10}^{10.1}$}
    \begin{jieda}
        两颗星的星等与亮度满足 ${m}_{2} - {m}_{1} = \dfrac{5}{2}\lg \dfrac{{E}_{1}}{{E}_{2}}$ ,令 ${m}_{2} =  - {1.45},{m}_{1} =  - {26.7}$，$\lg \dfrac{{E}_{1}}{{E}_{2}} = \dfrac{2}{5} \cdot  \left( {{m}_{2} - {m}_{1}}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{5}\left( {-{1.45} + {26.7}}\right)  = {10.1},\dfrac{{E}_{1}}{{E}_{2}} = {10}^{10.1}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 若 $f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,且当 $x > 0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = 2 + \lg x$ ,则 $f\left( {-{10}}\right)  =$ \blkda[4]{$-3$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        由题意得 $f\left( {-{10}}\right)  =  - f\left( {10}\right)  =  - \left( {2 + \lg {10}}\right)  =  - 3$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - {x}^{2} - {2ax} - a,x < 0 \\  {{e}}^{x} + \ln \left( {x + 1}\right) ,x \geq  0 \end{array}\right.$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上单调递增,则 $a$ 的取值范围是 \blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$}
    \begin{jieda}
        因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 ${R}$ 上单调递增,且 $x \geq  0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + \ln \left( {x + 1}\right)$ 单调递增,
        
        则需满足 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - \dfrac{-{2a}}{2 \times  \left( {-1}\right) } \geq  0 \\   - a \leq  {{e}}^{0} + \ln 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $- 1 \leq  a \leq  0$ ,
        
        即 $a$ 的范围是 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}x\left( {a > 0\text{ 且 }a \neq  1}\right)$ 满足 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  = 1$ 
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 求 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( {2}^{10}\right)$ 的值
        \item 若 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {2 - x}\right)  + f\left( {1 + x}\right)$ ,求 $g\left( x\right)$ 的单调区间
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 由已知 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}x\left( {a > 0\text{且}a \neq  1}\right)$ 满足 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  = 1$ ,则 ${\log }_{a}\dfrac{1}{2} + {\log }_{a}4 = 1$ ,即 $- {\log }_{a}2 + 2{\log }_{a}2 = 1$ ,得 ${\log }_{a}2 = 1$ ,所以 $a = 2$ ,得 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 2\right)  + f\left( 4\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( {2}^{10}\right)  = {\log }_{2}1 + {\log }_{2}2 + {\log }_{2}4 + {\log }_{2}8 + \cdots  + {\log }_{2}{2}^{10} = 1 + 2 + 3 + \cdots  + {10} = {55}$
            
            \item 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 2 - x > 0, \\  1 + x > 0, \end{array}\right.$ 得 $- 1 < x < 2$，故$g(x)$的定义域为 $\left( {-1,2}\right)$
            
            $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {2 - x}\right)  + f\left( {1 + x}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {2 - x}\right)  + {\log }_{2}\left( {1 + x}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {-{x}^{2} + x + 2 }\right)$，根据同增异减可知 $g\left( x\right)$ 的单调增区间为 $\left( {-1,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,单调减区间为 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right)$
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 设正实数 $a\text{ 、 }b\text{ 、 }c$ 分别满足 $a \cdot  {2}^{a} = 1,b \cdot  {\log }_{2}b = 1,c \cdot  {\log }_{3}c = 1$ ,则 $a\text{ 、 }b\text{ 、 }c$ 的大小关系为\dotfill(\kh{C})
    \xx{$a > b > c$}{$b > a > c$}{$c > b > a$}{$a > c > b$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $2^a = \dfrac{1}{a}$，$log_2b = \dfrac{1}{b}$，$log_3c = \dfrac{1}{c}$，根据函数图像即可判断.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {\lg x}\right|$ ,若 $f\left( {\lg m}\right)  > f\left( 2\right)$ ,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left( {1,\sqrt{10}}\right)  \cup  \left( {{100}, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        如图,画出 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {\lg x}\right|$ 的大致图象,易知 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = f\left( 2\right)$ ,所以 $0 < \lg m < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 或 $\lg m > 2$ , 解得 $1 < m < \sqrt{10}$ 或 $m > {100}$ ,即实数 $m$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {1,\sqrt{10}}\right)  \cup  \left( {{100}, + \infty }\right)$
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_3_245_1700_506_377_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
    \end{jieda} .
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{3}x}\right|$ ,若 $a < b$ ,且 $f\left( a\right)  = f\left( b\right)$ ,则 $a + {2b}$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left( {3, + \infty }\right)$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{3}x}\right|$ 的图象如下:
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_4_241_612_373_252_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        
        
        因为 $0 < a < b$ 且 $f\left( a\right)  = f\left( b\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {{\log }_{3}a}\right|  = \left| {{\log }_{3}b}\right|$ 且 $0 < a < 1,b > 1$ ,
        
        所以 $- {\log }_{3}a = {\log }_{3}b$ ,所以 ${ab} = 1$ ,故 $a + {2b} = a + \frac{2}{a}$ ,
        
        由对勾函数 $y = x + \dfrac{2}{x}$ 在(0,1)上单调递减,所以 $a + {2b} = a + \dfrac{2}{a} > 1 + 2 = 3$ ,
        
        所以 $a+ 2b$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {3, + \infty }\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}x}\right|$ ,实数 $a,b$ 满足 $0 < a < b$ ,且 $f\left( a\right)  = f\left( b\right)$ ,若 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {{a}^{2},b}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值为 2,则 $\dfrac{1}{a} + b =$ \blkda[4]{4}.
    \begin{jieda}
        如图所示: 根据函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}x}\right|$ 的图象

        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_4_242_1769_257_197_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        
        
        得 $0 < a < 1 < b$ ,所以 $0 < {a}^{2} < a < 1$ . 结合函数图象,
        
        易知当 $x = {a}^{2}$ 时 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {{a}^{2},b}\right\rbrack$ 上取得最大值,所以 $f\left( {a}^{2}\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}{a}^{2}}\right|  = 2$
        
        又 $0 < a < 1$ ,所以 $a = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
        
        再结合 $f\left( a\right)  = f\left( b\right)$ ,可得 $b = 2$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{a} + b = 2 + 2 = 4$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $y = {{e}}^{\left| \ln x\right| } - \left| {x - 2}\right|$ 与 $y = {ax}$ 的图像有 3 个不同公共点 (其中 ${e}$ 为自然对数的底数),则实数 $a$ 的取值范围是\blkda{$(0, 1)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        $f(x) = {{e}}^{\left| \ln x\right| } - \left| {x - 2}\right| = \left\{\begin{array}{ll}
            \dfrac{1}{x} + x - 2 & 0 < x \leq 1 \\
            2x - 2 & 1 < x \leq 2 \\
            2 & x > 2
        \end{array}\right.$
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.25\linewidth]{bodyxb//src/yikeiylian4-10.png}
        \end{center}

        根据函数图像可知$a \in (0, 1)$
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - {x}^{2} - {2x},x \leq  0 \\  \left| {1 - \ln x}\right| ,x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,若 $f\left( x\right)  = m$ 存在四个不相等的实根 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$ ,且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3} < {x}_{4}$ ,则 ${x}_{4} - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) {x}_{3}$ 的最小值是\blkda[4]{$2\sqrt{2}{e}$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        作函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - {x}^{2} - {2x},x \leq  0 \\  \left| {1 - \ln x}\right| ,x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 与 $y = m$ 图像如下:
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_5_249_1281_576_445_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        
        
        $y = m\because f\left( x\right)  = m$ 存在四个不相等的实根
        
        ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$ ,且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3} < {x}_{4}$ ,
        
        则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - 2,{x}_{3}\text{ 、 }{x}_{4} > 0$ ,且 $1 - \ln {x}_{3} =  - \left( {1 - \ln {x}_{4}}\right)$ ,
        
        则 $\ln {x}_{3} + \ln {x}_{4} = 2$ ,即 $\ln( {x}_{3}{x}_{4}) = 2$ ,得 ${x}_{3}{x}_{4} = {{e}}^{2}$ ,
        
        则 ${x}_{4} - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) {x}_{3} = {x}_{4} + 2{x}_{3} \geq  2\sqrt{2{x}_{3}{x}_{4}} = 2\sqrt{2}{e}$ ,
        
        当且仅当 ${x}_{4} = 2{x}_{3}$ 时,即 ${x}_{3} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}{e},{x}_{4} = \sqrt{2}{e}$ 时,等号成立
    \end{jieda}
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{C Level}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2},x \leq  1 \\  \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right) }\right| ,x > 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,若 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ (其中$\left. {{x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3} < {x}_{4}}\right)$ ,则 $\dfrac{4}{{x}_{4} + 1} + \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + 2}\right) {x}_{3}$ 的取值范围是 \blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {4,\dfrac{16}{3}}\right)$}
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $x \leq  1$ 时,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right\rbrack$ 上递减,函数值集合为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上递增, 函数值集合为 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,

        当 $x > 1$ 时,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right) }\right|$ 在 $(1,2\rbrack$ 上递减,函数值集合为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,在 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ 上递增, 函数值集合为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,
        
        作出函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象,如图,设 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{4}\right)  = t$ ,
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d2gj7jref24c73b0nip0_2_241_1692_494_349_0.jpg}
        \end{center}
        
        
        当 $0 < t \leq  1$ 时,直线 $y = t$ 与函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象有四个交点,且交点的横坐标分别为
        
        ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$ ,且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3} < {x}_{4}$ ,
        
        当 $x > 1$ 时,由 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {x - 1}\right) }\right|  = 1$ ,解得 $x = \dfrac{3}{2}$ 或 $x = 3$ ,于是 $\dfrac{3}{2} \leq  {x}_{3} < 2,2 < {x}_{4} \leq  3$ ,
        
        由 $f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ ,得 $- {\log }_{2}\left( {{x}_{3} - 1}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {{x}_{4} - 1}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{3} - 1 = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{4} - 1}$ ,即 ${x}_{3} = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{4} - 1} + 1$ ,而 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 0$ ,
        
        因此 $\dfrac{4}{{x}_{4} + 1} + \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + 2}\right) {x}_{3} = \dfrac{4}{{x}_{4} + 1} + 2{x}_{3} = \dfrac{4}{{x}_{4} + 1} + \dfrac{2}{{x}_{4} - 1} + 2$ ,
        
        令 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{4}{x + 1} + \dfrac{2}{x - 1} + 2$ ,显然函数 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $(2,3\rbrack$ 上递减,且 $g\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{16}{3},g\left( 3\right)  = 4$ ,于是
        
        $g\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {4,\dfrac{16}{3}}\right) ,$
        
        所以 $\dfrac{4}{{x}_{4} + 1} + \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + 2}\right) {x}_{3}$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {4,\dfrac{16}{3}}\right)$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{3}\left( {{3x} + {12}}\right) ,g\left( x\right)  = m \cdot  {2}^{x} + 2\left( {m \neq  0}\right)$ ,若 $\forall {x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,\exists {x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$，使得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,则实数 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda[4]{$\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2},0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$}.
    \begin{jieda}
        当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack$ ；
        
        当 $m > 0$ 时,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,g\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {m + 2,{2m} + 2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        又 $\forall {x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,\exists {x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $\left\lbrack  {m + 2,{2m} + 2}\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m + 2 \geq  1 \\  {2m} + 2 \leq  3 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $0 < m \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ;
        
        当 $m < 0$ 时,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,g\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {{2m} + 2,m + 2}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        又 $\forall {x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,\exists {x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,
        
        所以 $\left\lbrack  {{2m} + 2,m + 2}\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack$ ,
        
        所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2m} + 2 \geq  1 \\  m + 2 \leq  3 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{1}{2} \leq  m < 0$ .
        
        综上,实数 $m$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2},0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$ .
    \end{jieda}
    \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {x + a}\right)$
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 当 $a = 2$ 时,解不等式: $f\left( x\right)  < {\log }_{2}x + 1$
        \item 当 $a = 0$ 时,不等式 ${\left\lbrack  f\left( x\right)  + 2\right\rbrack  }^{2} > m \cdot  f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上恒成立,求实数 $m$ 的取值范围
        \item 是否存在实数 $a$ ,使得函数 $y = \left| {f\left( x\right) }\right|$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,2}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值为 ${\log }_{2}3$ ,若存在,请求出 $a$ 的值；若不存在,请说明理由
    \end{enumerate}
    \begin{jieda}
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 由已知得 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + 2}\right)  < {\log }_{2}x + 1$，即 ${\log }_{2}\left( {x + 2}\right)  < {\log }_{2}{2x}$ ,因为 $y = {\log }_{2}x$ 是增函数,
            所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + 2 > 0 \\  {2x} > 0 \\  x + 2 < {2x} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $x > 2$，所以原不等式的解集为 $\{ x \mid  x > 2\}$
            \item 由题意令 $t = {\log }_{2}x$ ,因为 $x > 1$ ,所以 $t > 0$ ,
            所以不等式 ${\left\lbrack  f\left( x\right)  + 2\right\rbrack  }^{2} > m \cdot  f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上恒成立,
            可化为 ${\left( t + 2\right) }^{2} > {mt}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恒成立,
            分离参数得 $m < \dfrac{{t}^{2} + {4t} + 4}{t} = t + \dfrac{4}{t} + 4$ ,因为 $t + \dfrac{4}{t} \geq  2\sqrt{t \cdot  \dfrac{4}{t}} = 4$ ,当且仅当 $t = 2$ 时取等号,
            则要使原式恒成立,只需 $m < 4 + 4 = 8$ 即可,即实数 $m$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\infty ,8}\right)$
            \item 首先要使函数在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,2}\right\rbrack$ 上有意义,需 $- 1 + a > 0$ ,所以 $a > 1$ .
            易知函数 $y = \left| {f\left( x\right) }\right|$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,2}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值必在端点处产生,
            故只需 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {-1 + a}\right) }\right|  = {\log }_{2}3 \\  \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {2 + a}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\log }_{2}3 \end{array}\right.$ ,或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {-1 + a}\right) }\right|  \leq  {\log }_{2}3 \\  \left| {{\log }_{2}\left( {2 + a}\right) }\right|  = {\log }_{2}3 \end{array}\right.$ ,
            均无解，故不存在 $a$ 使结论成立.
        \end{enumerate}
    \end{jieda} 
\end{enumerate}
